**Rule:** Find the tax year first, then read that year's topic file. A rule that holds across years is here, a figure for one year is in the year folder. This is not tax advice, and the Operator or a bookkeeper decides any personal case.

---

## 1. OVERVIEW

This file is the entry point for Dutch tax questions for a ZZP freelancer with an eenmanszaak, and for a director who owns a BV, for tax years 2020 to 2027. Each year has its own folder with one file per topic. Every value there carries a tax year, a status, a check tag and a primary source URL, checked on 2026-10-11 against belastingdienst.nl, wetten.overheid.nl, officielebekendmakingen.nl, tweedekamer.nl, eerstekamer.nl, rijksoverheid.nl, ondernemersplein.overheid.nl, kvk.nl and rvo.nl.

---

## 2. HOW TO USE IT

1. Find the tax year the work belongs to. A transaction belongs to the year of its date, and a return to the year it covers.
2. Open that year's README, then the topic file. Each year folder holds the same twelve topic files.
3. For how a rule works across years, read section 3 of this file.
4. Respect the tags. Do not present an UNVERIFIED, proposed or unknown value as settled. Say what would confirm it.
5. For 2027, check the Status column on every row. On 2026-10-11 the Belastingplan 2027 was not adopted and the December 2026 indexation was not published.

### Tags

| Tag | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Verified | A fetched primary page states this value for this year |
| Corrected | An earlier research value was wrong. The file gives the value from the primary page |
| UNVERIFIED | No primary page confirmed it. The value is kept with the source that was tried |
| enacted | In law for that year |
| adopted-not-yet-in-force | Adopted, but in force later |
| proposed | A bill or announcement, not law |
| unknown | Not yet published or not found |

The rules below hold across tax years. Each says which years it covers, carries its check tag and names its sources.

### 3.1 BTW (VAT)

Year files: `btw.md` in each year folder.

#### Rate structure 21, 9 and 0 percent (Verified)
Art. 9 lid 1 Wet OB: "De belasting bedraagt 21 percent." Art. 9 lid 2: 9 percent for goods and services in Tabel I, nihil for those in Tabel II "mits is voldaan aan bij algemene maatregel van bestuur vast te stellen voorwaarden". The identical text was read in the 2020-01-01, 2021-07-01, 2022-01-01, 2023-01-01, 2024-01-01, 2025-01-01 and 2026-01-01 versions. Years 2020 to 2027. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2022-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2026-01-01

#### Tabel I goods and services at 9 percent (Verified for the current list)
Goods: voedingsmiddelen, water, sierteeltproducten, geneesmiddelen en hulpmiddelen, kunst, verzamelvoorwerpen en antiek, boeken en periodieken. The Belastingdienst links the Besluit Omzetbelasting Toelichting Tabel 1 (BWBR0052086, 2026-01-01). Services: repair of bicycles, of shoes and leather goods, of clothing and household linen, hairdressers, certain work on homes, camping, access to cultural and recreational events and facilities, performing artists, sport and bathing, passenger transport, e-books comparable to a 9 percent physical publication, access to news websites and apps unless mainly advertising, video or audible music. Related services named on the page: het opleveren van goederen, selling food in horeca, renting and repairing medical aids, lending books, lending art by the artist himself. The current pages carry no year, so year-specific post history before 2026 rests on the instruments below. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/tarieven_en_vrijstellingen/goederen_9_btw/goederen_met_9_btw, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/tarieven_en_vrijstellingen/diensten_9_btw/

#### Electronic publications at 9 percent from 1 January 2020 (Verified, source corrected)
Post b.21 of Tabel I applies "vanaf 1 januari 2020" to the electronic supply or lending of publications and to access to news websites. The research rested this date on a court summary on nlfiscaal.nl, which is not a primary source. The Toelichting Tabel I besluit settles it. Years 2020 to 2027. Source: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2022-9114.html (besluit van 31 maart 2022, nr. 2022-6334)

#### Logies from 9 to 21 percent on 1 January 2026, other reduced posts kept (Verified, added)
Stb. 2025, 339 (Wet van 29 oktober 2025, Wet behoud verlaagd btw-tarief op cultuur, media en sport) amends the Belastingplan 2025 so that "In tabel I vervalt onderdeel b, post 11" from 1 januari 2026, and limits the transitional rule to "het verstrekken van logies". The Belastingplan 2025 had also scheduled other Tabel I posts (culture, media, sport) to lapse on 1 January 2026. Stb. 2025, 339 withdrew that, so those posts stay at 9 percent in 2026. The Belastingdienst confirms 21 percent "vanaf 1 januari 2026", also for 2025 payments for stays in 2026 or later and for single-use vouchers for 2026 stays, and also for gas, electricity, water, sanitary, laundry and parking facilities tied to logies. Separately offered breakfast or pool and attraction access stays at 9 percent. Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2025-339.html, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/btw-logies, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/berichten/nieuws/btw-logies

#### Zero rate is not an exemption (Verified)
A 0 percent supply keeps the right to deduct input VAT, an exempt supply does not. For an intra-EU supply of goods the 0 percent rate needs three conditions: proof from the records that the goods went to another EU country, a buyer with a valid VAT ID of another member state, and an opgaaf ICP filed "op tijd, juist en volledig". A late or wrong ICP can cost the 0 percent rate and draw a fine. Years 2020 to 2027 (2024 guide and the 2026 handbook). Sources: https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/toelichting-digitale-opgaaf-intracomm-pres-ob1291t41fd.pdf, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2026_aa9701z62fd.pdf

#### Exemptions art. 11 Wet OB (Verified)
Art. 11 lid 1 onderdelen a to w were read in the 2020-01-01 and 2026-01-01 versions with the same headings: immovable property supply and letting (with exceptions), care in institutions, youth work, sport, social and cultural bodies, health care, funeral services, money, securities and fund management, credit and payments, insurance, games of chance, universal postal service, public broadcasting, education, lectures at cost, composers, writers and journalists, movable goods used only for exempt supplies, organs and blood, member services of interest groups, cost-sharing groups (koepelvrijstelling), fundraising, childcare. The Belastingdienst list adds beleggingsgoud and kantines and says the exemptions also apply to imports. Exempt supplies carry no right to deduct. Years 2020 to 2027. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/tarieven_en_vrijstellingen/vrijstellingen/

#### Solar panels at 0 percent from 1 January 2023 (Verified)
"Met ingang van 1 januari 2023 is een btw-nultarief ingevoerd op de levering en installatie van zonnepanelen" on or near homes (besluit van 16 juni 2023, nr. 2023-6393). The Belastingdienst page lists what is covered (non-integrated and integrated panels, cables, PV-verdeler, mounting material, optimizers and inverters, removal and reinstallation, plug and play panels) and the 21 percent items (multi-function panels such as PVT or window panels, solar collectors for heat, panels on non-residential business buildings or public buildings, roof reinforcement, infrezen van bekabeling, replacing the whole groepenkast, a smart meter, bird or snow guards, goods not bought for the installation such as a replacement inverter, heat pumps, batteries and thuisbatterijen). Integrated panels on a new-build home are 21 percent when the home itself is also supplied, 0 percent when only the panels are supplied and installed. The page states no start date, the besluit does. Years 2023 to 2027. Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2023-17463.html, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/tarieven_en_vrijstellingen/goederen_0_btw/btw-tarief-zonnepanelen

#### Aangifte periods (Verified)
Quarterly is the usual period, monthly or yearly on request by letter to the belastingkantoor, reply within 6 weeks, the change starts at the start of the new period. Late filing or payment can lead to a forced monthly period. The Handboek 2020 adds "Betaalt u doorgaans meer dan € 15.000 btw per kwartaal aan ons, dan is uw aangiftetijdvak 1 maand." The Handboek 2026 and the current page no longer state that trigger. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/wijziging-aangiftetijdvak-btw, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2020_aa9701z01fd.pdf, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2026_aa9701z62fd.pdf

#### Yearly return conditions (Verified for 2020 and 2026)
All of: less than € 1.883 VAT a year, no vergunning artikel 23, a natural person or a partnership of natural persons only (maatschap or vof), and less than € 10.000 a year for each of intracommunautaire leveringen, intracommunautaire diensten, intracommunautaire verwervingen and afgenomen intracommunautaire diensten, "voor elk van deze activiteiten afzonderlijk". Identical in Handboek 2020 and Handboek 2026. The current page names no year and says nothing on indexation. Sources: https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2020_aa9701z01fd.pdf, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2026_aa9701z62fd.pdf, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/wijziging-aangiftetijdvak-btw

#### Filing and payment term (Verified)
AWR art. 10 lid 2: a period return is filed "binnen een door de inspecteur gestelde termijn van ten minste een maand na het einde van het tijdvak". AWR art. 19 lid 1: payment "binnen één maand na het einde van dat tijdvak". Same text in the 2020-01-01 and 2026-01-01 versions. The Belastingdienst rule: monthly and quarterly returns "uiterlijk op de laatste dag van de maand die volgt op het kwartaal of de maand", the yearly return "vóór 1 april van het eerstvolgende jaar", the aangiftedatum is the day of receipt, filing and payment share one date. Extension only "als er sprake is van een calamiteit". Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_aangifte_doen_en_betalen/btw-aangifte-waar-moet-u-aan-denken/, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/uiterste-aangifte-en-betaaldatums

#### Submission window for software returns (Verified, 2026 page)
"Stuur dan uw btw-aangifte in vanaf de 24e van de maand voor het einde van het betreffende tijdvak." Earlier submission gives a foutmelding. Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/uiterste-aangifte-en-betaaldatums

#### Old KOR to 31 December 2019 (Verified)
Art. 25 lid 1 (2019-01-01 version): for a natural person living or established in the Netherlands (or with a vaste inrichting there) whose VAT after deduction is "niet hoger is dan € 1883", the VAT is reduced by "2,5 maal het verschil tussen € 1883" and the VAT due, at most to nil. Lid 3: on request ontheffing of administrative obligations. The € 1.345 full-relief point is not printed in the statute. It follows from the formula (2,5 x (1.883 minus x) equals x at x = 1.345). Source: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2019-01-01

#### KOR 2020 to 2024, old art. 25 (Verified, baseline Corrected)
Wet modernisering kleineondernemersregeling (Wet van 19 december 2018): Art. IV lid 1 general entry into force 1 juni 2019, Art. IV lid 2 Articles I and II "met ingang van 1 januari 2020", Art. III the 20 November 2019 notice date. Art. 25 lid 1: an entrepreneur established in the Netherlands or with "aldaar een vaste inrichting" "van wie de omzet in een kalenderjaar in Nederland niet meer bedraagt dan € 20.000" may choose the exemption for supplies "in dat en volgende kalenderjaren". No lid mentions the preceding calendar year (checked in the 2020, 2021-07-01, 2022, 2023 and 2024 versions). Lid 6: notice "uiterlijk vier weken voorafgaand aan het belastingtijdvak", refusal by beschikking when the conditions will plausibly not be met. Lid 7: "ten minste voor drie jaren na aanvang", re-entry three years after revocation. Lid 8: on exceeding, no exemption from the crossing supply onward, re-entry three years after the overrun. Lid 4: no input VAT deduction and no VAT on the invoice. Handboek 2020 adds: legal persons (bv, nv, stichting, vereniging) may join, no option for taxed letting under the KOR, herziening stays out when the total is less than € 500 a year for investment goods used before that year, and deregistration only per the first day of an aangiftetijdvak. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0041782, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2024-01-01, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2019-4774.html, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2020_aa9701z01fd.pdf

#### KOR from 2025, art. 25 to 25e (Verified)
Wet van 10 juli 2023 (Stb. 2023, 278) in force 1 januari 2025, its Art. II (transition) from 1 oktober 2024. Art. 25: jaaromzet in Nederland and in de Unie defined as totals of consideration "gedurende een kalenderjaar", lid 2 excludes business-used immovable property and depreciable movable assets. Art. 25a lid 1: "Een ondernemer die in Nederland is gevestigd en van wie de jaaromzet in Nederland niet meer bedraagt dan € 20.000" may choose the exemption. Lid 2: established in the Union but not in the Netherlands, Union turnover at most € 100.000. Lid 3: not for new means of transport sent to another member state or business-used immovable property. Lid 4: no deduction and no mention of VAT on the invoice in any way. Lid 5: start from the first tax period "minimaal vier weken na ontvangst van de melding", the melding states turnover of "het voorafgaande kalenderjaar" and of the current year to the notice. Lid 7: revocation takes effect on the first day of the next calendar quarter "minimaal vier weken na ontvangst van de wederopzegging", re-entry after "het kalenderjaar waarin de vrijstelling is beëindigd en het daaropvolgende kalenderjaar". Lid 9: exceeding € 20.000 (or € 100.000 Union) excludes the exemption also "in het daaropvolgende kalenderjaar", and the crossing supply is taxed "gedurende het resterende kalenderjaar en het daaropvolgende kalenderjaar". Same amounts in the 2026-01-01 version. Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2023-278.html, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2025-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2026-01-01

#### KOR turnover-test conflict with the baseline (settled)
The baseline row "turnover of at most € 20.000 in the year of application and the year before" for 2020 to 2027 is Corrected for 2020 to 2024. Old art. 25 lid 1 tests "de omzet in een kalenderjaar" and no lid names the preceding year, in every dated version from 2020 to 2024. The Raad van State advice and nader rapport on the 2020 KOR speak of the expected turnover in the year itself ("verwacht in het jaar 2020 de omzetgrens van € 20.000 niet te overschrijden"). A prior-year overrun mattered before 2025 only through the three-year lock of lid 8. From 1 January 2025 the prior year counts: art. 25a lid 5 requires prior-year turnover in the melding and lid 9 bars the year after an overrun. The transition law barred 2025 entry when 2024 Dutch turnover "meer dan € 20.000 bedraagt". The Belastingdienst KOR page and Handboek 2026 now state the limit "voor het kalenderjaar waarin u zich aanmeldt, én voor het kalenderjaar daarvóór". Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2024-01-01, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2019-4774.html, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2025-01-01, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2023-278.html, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/kor-voorwaarden

#### KOR turnover that counts (Verified for 2025 to 2026, current page)
Counted: supplies taxed with Dutch VAT, including 0 percent (export, intra-EU), domestic supplies reverse-charged to the customer, and exempt supplies relating to immovable property, means of payment and securities, and financial and insurance services in the Netherlands. Other exempt turnover does not count. Not counted: VAT on private use, sale of business-used investment goods, domestic supplies reverse-charged to you as the buyer, supplies taxed in another country, intra-community acquisitions. Before joining the turnover is measured excluding VAT, during participation it is the total amount charged. All subnummers are added and the KOR applies to all of them. Handboek 2020 gives the same principle in short form for 2020 (all Dutch taxed turnover whatever the rate, also when reverse-charged, plus some exempt supplies). Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/kor-voorwaarden, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2020_aa9701z01fd.pdf

#### KOR registration threshold without notice (Verified, added)
Besluit van 10 juni 2021, nr. 2021-12740: an entrepreneur not yet registered "met een jaaromzet van maximaal € 1.800" may apply the KOR without the notice, retroactive to 1 januari 2020. From 1 januari 2025 art. 25b Wet OB and art. 25 Uitvoeringsbeschikking omzetbelasting 1968 set the amount, "verhoogd van € 1.800 naar € 2.200" (besluit van 21 november 2024, nr. 2024-29602). Art. 25b also requires that the entrepreneur has not registered with the inspector or the KVK and is not obliged to, and does not use the exemption in other member states. Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2021-30899.html, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2024-38565.html, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2025-01-01

#### KOR practice from 2025 (Verified)
Apply only after receiving the omzetbelastingnummer and btw-id. The aanmelding must arrive "uiterlijk 4 weken vóór de ingangsdatum", quarterly filer cut-offs 3 maart (1 april), 2 juni (1 juli), 2 september (1 oktober), 3 december (1 januari), a late aanmelding starts the next quarter, returns continue until the definitive start date, confirmation within 8 weken. Voluntary afmelding via Mijn Belastingdienst Zakelijk "alleen met ingang van de 1e dag van uw aangiftetijdvak", at least 4 weeks ahead, no re-entry for "de rest van het kalenderjaar waarin u bent afgemeld, en het jaar erop", confirmation within 8 weken. Paper forms are no longer handled. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/aanmelden-kor, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/afmelden-kor

#### EU KOR from 2025 (Verified)
Art. 25c: prior notification and an individual number with suffix "EX", start on the date the inspector issues or confirms the EX number, issued "binnen 35 werkdagen", not when Union turnover exceeds € 100.000 in the previous or current year (then a beschikking) or when the other state reports its threshold exceeded or art. 288 bis conditions unmet, and not where more time is needed against fraud. Ending takes effect on the first day of the next calendar quarter, or the first day of the second month of the next quarter when the information arrives in the last month of a quarter. Art. 25d: notice contains name, activity, legal form, address, the member states, previous-year totals in the Netherlands and each other state (two years for states that extended the period under art. 288 bis), and current-year totals to the notice. Art. 25e: quarterly totals per state ("0" if none) "binnen één maand te rekenen vanaf het einde van het kalenderkwartaal", and notice "binnen 15 werkdagen" when Union turnover passes € 100.000 with supplies from the start of the quarter. The Belastingdienst: Union turnover at most € 100.000 "in dit én vorig kalenderjaar" and sometimes one more year, combinable with the Unieregeling per country, not with the Invoerregeling, intra-EU acquisitions stay taxable in the arrival state (exception when the supplier also uses the KOR, never for new means of transport), no return or ICP for exempt intra-EU supplies except new means of transport, an ICP is due when the business has no Dutch exemption but participates in the arrival state, reverse-charged services still need an incidental return there. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2025-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/deelnemen-aan-de-eu-kor-en-andere-btw-regelingen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/opgaaf-kwartaalomzet

#### Suppletie (Verified, timing and amount by period)
UBOB art. 15 (2020 version): correct returns of "de afgelopen vijf kalenderjaren", before the taxpayer knows or should suspect the inspector knows, "zo spoedig mogelijk op de door de inspecteur aangegeven wijze", failure is an overtreding, the vergrijpboete power lapses five years after the end of the year. Stb. 2024, 441 (Besluit van 18 december 2024, Eindejaarsbesluit 2024) Art. VII onderdeel A adds to lid 2 "maar niet later dan acht weken nadat de belastingplichtige de onjuistheid of onvolledigheid heeft geconstateerd" and drops "zo spoedig mogelijk", in force 1 januari 2025 (Art. XXIV), the eight weeks start no earlier than 1 januari 2025 (Art. XXIII). The nota cites Rechtbank Zeeland-West-Brabant 14 juli 2023, the earlier of the two terms governs, vergrijpboete up to 100 percent of the VAT for opzet or grove schuld. In the 2026 version the offence and five-year lapse sit in art. 15a. Belastingdienst amount boundary: Handboek 2020 "Hebt u een correctie van minder dan € 1.000? Dan kunt u dit vermelden in de eerstvolgende aangifte." Handboek 2026 "een correctie van € 1.000 of minder". The current page: "maximaal €1000" in the next return, "meer dan € 1.000" requires the form within 8 weeks, corrections "tot en met 5 jaar na het jaar", a 2024 period "tot en met eind 2029", no belastingrente when filed "binnen 3 maanden na afloop van het jaar" or corrected within the year, reply within 8 weken. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002633/2020-01-01, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2024-441.html, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002633/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_aangifte_doen_en_betalen/aangifte_corrigeren/aangifte_corrigeren

#### Verleggingsregeling (Verified, research Corrected on article numbers)
Art. 12 Wet OB (2020 and 2026 versions): lid 1 levy from the supplier, lid 2 and 3 shift to the customer for non-established suppliers, lid 4 a Dutch vaste inrichting not involved counts as non-established, lid 5 AMvB cases "ten einde voor de inning daarvan meer waarborgen te scheppen". UBOB Afdeling E holds arts. 24b (onderaanneming and staff lending in construction and shipbuilding, invoice "btw verlegd"), 24ba (further cases including certain immovable property, gold, emission rights, gas and electricity certificates and electronics) and 24bb (old materials, waste and scrap). The research said "arts. 24 to 24bb". Art. 24 has lapsed and art. 24a belongs to Afdeling D. Art. 24ba lid 1 onderdeel g: mobile phones, chips before end-product, game consoles, tablets and laptops only "voor een totaalbedrag van € 10 000 of meer per levering per soort goed". Belastingdienst case list: buitenland, onderaanneming and staff lending (construction, shipbuilding, cleaning, gardening), waste, the electronics, immovable property (joint option for taxed supply between entrepreneurs, not where the supply is taxed by law such as a building within two years of first use or a building plot), executieverkopen, gas and electricity certificates, gold, emission rights, and telecom services between providers in the Netherlands. Invoice: "btw verlegd", the customer's VAT ID, the consideration per rate. The supplier "blijft wel verantwoordelijk". The customer reports in rubriek 2 (domestic) or 4 (from abroad) and deducts when used for taxed activity. Onderaanneming exceptions: work more than half in the own workshop, staff lent for such work, subordinate work on sold goods, disputes to the main contractor's belastingkantoor. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2026-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002633/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002633/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_berekenen_aan_uw_klanten/waarover_btw_berekenen/verleggingsregeling/wanneer_btw_verleggen/, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_berekenen_aan_uw_klanten/waarover_btw_berekenen/verleggingsregeling/hoe_werkt_btw_verleggen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_berekenen_aan_uw_klanten/waarover_btw_berekenen/verleggingsregeling/wanneer_btw_verleggen/bij_onderaanneming_en_personeel_uitlenen/uitzonderingen_onderaanneming_en_personeel_uitlenen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_berekenen_aan_uw_klanten/waarover_btw_berekenen/verleggingsregeling/wanneer_btw_verleggen/verlegging_bij_onroerende_zaken

#### Intra-EU B2B services (Verified)
The service is taxed in the customer's country, invoice without VAT marked "btw verlegd", check the VAT ID, report it in the return and in the opgaaf ICP. Some services are excepted (a separate page). Not listed in the ICP: services exempt or 0 percent in the customer's country, OSS services, services connected with immovable property, passenger transport, admission to cultural, sport and similar events (2024 guide). Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/zakendoen_met_het_buitenland/goederen_en_diensten_naar_andere_eu_landen/btw_berekenen_bij_diensten/btw_berekenen_bij_diensten_aan_afnemers_in_andere_eu_landen, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/toelichting-digitale-opgaaf-intracomm-pres-ob1291t41fd.pdf

#### Opgaaf ICP (Verified)
Report intra-EU supplies and services, own goods moved to another EU country and call-off stock transfers. The total equals rubriek 3b for the same period. Goods in the period of the invoice date, services in the period the service was supplied. No ICP for a period with nothing to report, corrections in the next ICP, at most 100 lines per rubriek on the form. Goods: monthly, quarterly only when goods supplies stay at or below € 50.000 in the quarter and in each of the previous 4 quarters, yearly with a vergunning. Crossing in month 1 gives 3 monthly ICPs, in month 2 two (first two months, then the last), in month 3 one quarterly ICP and monthly afterwards. Quarterly again after 4 consecutive quarters at or below the threshold, from the fifth quarter if that also stays below. Services: monthly, quarterly or yearly (vergunning), the € 50.000 test does not apply to services. Goods and services may be split or combined (combined follows the goods rules). 2024 guide: a yearly ICP only for natural persons and partnerships of natural persons, domestic deadline "binnen 1 maand na afloop van het gekozen tijdvak", including a tweemaandelijks option, quarterly Q4 and the yearly ICP both due 31 januari of the next year. Businesses not established in the Netherlands have 2 months. Call-off stock conditions (2024 guide): agreement with a known buyer, the buyer's VAT ID, own transport, no establishment in the arrival state, supply within 12 months of arrival, a register, and listing per buyer in the ICP of the period the transport began. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/zakendoen_met_het_buitenland/goederen_en_diensten_naar_andere_eu_landen/opgaaf_icp/opgaaf_icp, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/zakendoen_met_het_buitenland/goederen_en_diensten_naar_andere_eu_landen/opgaaf_icp/tijdvak_opgaaf_icp/tijdvak_opgaaf_icp, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/toelichting-digitale-opgaaf-intracomm-pres-ob1291t41fd.pdf

#### Distance sales, € 10.000 threshold and OSS from 1 July 2021 (Verified)
The Wet implementatie richtlijnen elektronische handel "treedt in werking met ingang van 1 juli 2021" (Besluit van 28 mei 2021, Stb. 2021, 261). "De mini One Stop Shop-regeling (MOSS-regeling) is per 1 juli 2021 vervallen" and the eenloketsysteem took its place. Art. 6k lid 1: the € 10.000 threshold applies only to a supplier established in one member state, the total "in het lopende kalenderjaar niet hoger dan € 10 000" and also not exceeded in the previous calendar year, lid 2 applies the normal rules from the moment of overrun. Belastingdienst: goods sent from the Netherlands directly to non-VAT-filing customers in another EU country with transport arranged or paid by the supplier, excluding margin goods, (nearly) new means of transport and assembly supplies. Digital services to consumers count toward the threshold, measured excluding VAT. Below the threshold the supplier may opt for foreign VAT, the choice holds "minimaal 2 jaar". Above it foreign VAT from the overrun, Dutch VAT again "vanaf 1 januari van het jaar daarna" after a full calendar year at or below € 10.000. No threshold with a vaste inrichting in another EU country (a warehouse alone is not one). The Unieregeling is optional, one return per quarter for all EU distance sales, foreign input VAT cannot be reclaimed through it. Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2021-261.html, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/zakendoen_met_het_buitenland/goederen_en_diensten_naar_andere_eu_landen/btw_berekenen_bij_diensten/wijziging_in_digitale_diensten_vanaf_2015/eu_btw_melding_doen/, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/btw-goederen-eu-particulieren

#### OSS mechanics (Verified, one research item UNVERIFIED)
Unieregeling: one melding per quarter in the month after, by the last day of that month (Q3 by 31 oktober), also a nil melding. Usable from the first day of the quarter after registration, for a first supply the registration must arrive "op de 10e dag van de maand die volgt op de maand van de levering". Three consecutive quarters without a melding gives exclusion for 2 years. A melding cannot be resubmitted, corrections go in a later melding "uiterlijk binnen 3 jaar" after the original due date. Records kept 10 years. Euro, ECB rate of the last day of the quarter, one payment for all states. Invoerregeling: monthly, maximum € 150 per zending, three consecutive missed months gives 2-year exclusion. The research claim that "two years without supplies (while still filing)" ends participation is not on the page and stays UNVERIFIED. Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/btw-melden-eenloketsysteem

#### Private use, last return of the year (Verified)
Private use by the entrepreneur includes use by family. Two situations: mixed use (car, business computer, painting a partly private building) and withdrawal of goods for exclusively private use. Staff private use often limits deduction (BUA). The last return of the year (Q4 or December) settles private use of goods and services, the car, the pro rata of taxed and exempt turnover, and personnel provisions, gifts and business gifts above € 227. Handboek 2020 and 2026 also name gas, water and electricity, fictieve (2020: interne) diensten and herzieningen. A last return already filed is corrected with Suppletie btw. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_aftrekken/privegebruik/privegebruik, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_aangifte_doen_en_betalen/btw-aangifte-waar-moet-u-aan-denken/laatste-btw-aangifte-van-het-jaar, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2020_aa9701z01fd.pdf, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2026_aa9701z62fd.pdf

#### Business car private use (Verified)
Besluit "Omzetbelasting, heffing privégebruik auto en toepassing BUA" (Den Haag, 25 juni 2020, geldend van 04-07-2020 t/m heden, replaces BLKB 2012/639M). § 2.2: without records the private use is "2,7% van de catalogusprijs (inclusief btw en bpm)", for cars in the business assets and cars obtained "op een andere wijze", capped at the VAT deducted in the year plus "1/5 deel van de btw" on purchase. § 2.7: 1,5 percent when no purchase VAT could be deducted. § 2.7.1: 1,5 percent "na afloop van het vierde jaar volgende op het jaar waarin de ondernemer de auto is gaan gebruiken". Handboek 2020 and 2026 say the same as "meer dan 5 jaar in gebruik (het jaar van aanschaf telt mee als een vol jaar)". Commuting is private use, exempt use reduces the amount proportionally, the amount goes in the last return of the year, a part year is pro rata. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0043783/2020-07-04, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_aftrekken/btw_en_de_auto/privegebruik_auto_van_de_zaak/privegebruik_auto_van_de_zaak, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2020_aa9701z01fd.pdf, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2026_aa9701z62fd.pdf

#### BUA (Verified, gap closed)
Besluit uitsluiting aftrek omzetbelasting 1968 (BWBR0002636), one version "geldend van 23-02-2019 t/m heden". Art. 1 lid 1: no deduction for "het voeren van een zekere staat", business gifts and other gifts, and staff housing, wages in kind, sport, leisure, private transport (making a car available excluded). Lid 3 carve-outs: food and drink for staff, licensed besloten busvervoer for commuting, a commuting bicycle at most "€ 749 inclusief omzetbelasting" (no bicycle in the year or two before, not 50 percent or more of travel days otherwise covered), outplacement. Art. 2: no exclusion up to the VAT owed on a charged consideration. Art. 3: staff meals charged below cost plus 25 percent, deduction excluded "tot 9 percent van het verschil". Art. 4 lid 1: provisions to the same person with total cost "niet meer bedraagt dan € 227" per boekjaar stay out of the exclusion. This closes the research gap on where the € 227 sits. Besluit van 6 oktober 2023, nr. 2023-20692 (Stcrt. 2023, 28124): the website explanation that let the eigen bijdrage reduce the tested amount "wordt om die reden beëindigd met ingang van 1 januari 2024". Handboek 2026 adds that gifts are hit when the recipient would have less than 30 percent deduction. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002636/2019-02-23, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2023-28124.html, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_aftrekken/personeelsvoorzieningen_en_relatiegeschenken/drempelbedrag_personeelsvoorzieningen_giften_en_relatiegeschenken, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2026_aa9701z62fd.pdf

#### Invoicing (Verified)
Invoice every supply to another entrepreneur and to a legal person that is not an entrepreneur, no invoice needed for private customers (with exceptions on a separate page). Issue by "de 15e dag van de maand die volgt op de maand waarin u hebt geleverd". Handboek example: delivered on 10 april, invoice by 15 mei. VAT is due at the latest in the period the customer paid. Contents: full name and address of both parties (trade name allowed when registered with address at the KVK, no PO box alone), VAT ID with NL, KVK number when registered, invoice date, a unique consecutive number, kind and quantity of goods or kind and scope of services, date of supply or prepayment, amounts excluding VAT per rate with unit price, the rate, the VAT amount. Fiscal unity: the VAT ID of the supplying part. Simplified invoice at most € 100 including VAT or for a correction invoice, with issue date, name and address, the goods or services, the VAT or data to calculate it, and the reference to the original, not for intra-EU supplies, distance sales or reverse-charged supplies in a state where the supplier is not established. Digital invoice: customer agreement and assurance of "de echtheid van herkomst, de inhoud en de leesbaarheid", no general obligation. Kasstelsel is compulsory for listed trades (shops including webshops in 2026, market traders, hairdressers, horeca and others) and on request when more than 80 percent goes to consumers. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/administratie_bijhouden/facturen_maken/, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/administratie_bijhouden/facturen_maken/factuureisen/factuureisen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/administratie_bijhouden/facturen_maken/factuureisen/aangepaste_regels_facturen/vereenvoudigde_factuur, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2020_aa9701z01fd.pdf, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2026_aa9701z62fd.pdf

#### E-invoicing and ViDA (Verified in part)
Suppliers to central government must send e-invoices, municipalities, provinces and water boards may require them, Peppol is named (business.gov.nl, the government business portal run with RVO and KVK). Kamerbrief 26 juni 2025 (Kamerstuk 22112, nr. 4090): ViDA e-invoicing and digital reporting "moet op 1 juli 2030 in werking treden", platform rules implemented by 30 juni 2028 with possible deferral to 1 januari 2030, OSS legislation expected in Q3 2026, no decision yet then on national B2B. Rijksoverheid 11 september 2026: national B2B "elektronisch factureren (per 1 juli 2030) en rapportage (per 1 juli 2031)", KOR businesses "zijn vrijgesteld van e-facturatie en -rapportage", consultation this autumn, bill expected before summer 2027 (proposed). The EU adoption and publication dates of the ViDA acts (11 and 25 March 2025, in force 14 April 2025) were not found on a Dutch primary page and stay UNVERIFIED. Sources: https://business.gov.nl/regulations/e-invoicing-government-organisations/, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-22112-4090.html, https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/09/11/kabinet-kiest-voor-invoering-e-facturatie-en-rapportage-voor-bedrijven

#### Bill 36.920 ViDA single VAT registration (Corrected status, added)
The research marked this bill as pending. The Eerste Kamer page shows it adopted as a hamerstuk by the Tweede Kamer on 17 September 2026 and by the Eerste Kamer on 6 October 2026, no Staatsblad yet, entry into force 1 January 2027 (if published later, the day after publication with retroactive effect to 1 January 2027). The memorie van toelichting: Articles I and II from 1 January 2027 (art. 28tb clarifies that the Invoerregeling cannot be combined with the KOR, art. 6k lid 1 relief cannot be used by a business that already uses the Unieregeling, certain gas, electricity, heat and cooling supplies treated as distance sales), Article III from 1 July 2028 (wider Unieregeling, a new optional overbrengingsregeling, own-goods transfers under it left out of the ICP), Article IV from 1 July 2029 (voorraad op afroep ends, no new call-off shipments from 1 July 2028). Status adopted-not-yet-in-force. Sources: https://www.eerstekamer.nl/wetsvoorstel/36920_wet_implementatie_richtlijn, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36920-3.html

#### Late filing and payment penalties (Verified, page for foreign-established businesses)
"De hoogte van de boete voor het niet (op tijd) doen van aangifte bedraagt € 82." Late payment: 3 percent of the unpaid VAT, maximum € 6.709, minimum € 50. Read on 2026-10-11 on the page for businesses established outside the Netherlands, not generalised. Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/internationaal/btw_voor_buitenlandse_ondernemers/btw_aangifte_doen_en_betalen/uiterste_ontvangstdatum_aangifte_en_betaling_btw_aangifte

#### Corona bijzonder uitstel (Verified)
Debts built up "van 12 maart 2020 tot en met 31 maart 2022", repaid "van 1 oktober 2022 tot 1 oktober 2027", first term due 31 oktober 2022, 60 monthly terms, quarterly payment possible, one pause of up to 6 months or 2 quarters, extension to 84 months only for a scheme of at least € 10.000. Added: for recipients of the letter "Afronding UHT en einde invorderingspauze" the 60 months run from 1 juli 2025. Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/betalenenontvangen/content/betalingsregeling-voor-opgebouwde-belastingschuld-door-bijzonder-uitstel

#### Box 1 table structure and the premie volksverzekeringen

Verified. Wet IB 2001 art. 2.10 lid 1 holds only the income-tax part. The first statutory band is income tax only (9,70% in 2020, 9,45% in 2021, 9,42% in 2022, 9,28% in 2023, 9,32% in 2024, 8,17% in 2025, 8,10% in 2026). The premie volksverzekeringen of 27,65% (AOW 17,90%, Anw 0,10%, Wlz 9,65%) is charged over the premie-inkomen up to a ceiling that equals the end of that first band. The published combined first-bracket rate is the income-tax part plus 27,65%. Above the premium ceiling the statutory income-tax rate equals the combined rate, which is why 2020 to 2024 show one combined rate up to the top boundary. The ceiling is € 34.712 (2020), € 35.129 (2021), € 35.472 (2022), € 37.149 (2023), € 38.098 (2024), € 38.441 (2025) and € 38.883 (2026), and for people born before 1 January 1946 it is € 35.375, € 35.941, € 36.409, € 38.703, € 40.021, € 40.502 and € 41.123. Art. 2.10a holds the table for people born before 1 January 1946 with that higher first boundary. Once the AOW age is reached no AOW premium is due, which is why the AOW-age first-bracket rate is lower. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/premie_volksverzekeringen to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2023/premie_volksverzekeringen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/boxen_en_tarieven/box_1/box_1, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/werk_en_inkomen/sociale_verzekeringen/premies_volks_en_werknemersverzekeringen/volksverzekeringen/hoeveel_moet_u_betalen. The https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/werk_en_inkomen/sociale_verzekeringen/premies_volks_en_werknemersverzekeringen/volksverzekeringen/hoeveel_moet_u_betalen page states only that the AOW premium stops at AOW age. That Anw and Wlz continue is inference from the AOW-age first-bracket rates (for example 17,85% = 8,10% plus 0,10% plus 9,65% in 2026) and stays UNVERIFIED as a stated rule.

#### Top rate and bracket count

Verified. The top rate is 49,50% in every year 2020 to 2026, in the enacted 2027 text and in the 2027 bill. Below AOW age the published table has two brackets in 2020 to 2024 and three brackets from 2025. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/belastingberekening to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2023/belastingberekening, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/boxen_en_tarieven/box_1/box_1, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-37022-2.html.

#### AOW transition year first-bracket rate

Verified. In the year in which the AOW age is reached the first-bracket rate depends on the month of reaching it, rising by about 1,49 points per month from the AOW-age rate. Schijven 2 and 3 keep the normal rates. Monthly tables per year are in the year sections. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/belastingberekening to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/belastingberekening, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/boxen_en_tarieven/box_1/box_1.

#### Tariefsaanpassing (art. 2.10 lid 2)

Verified. When grondslagverminderende posten have been deducted, the tax under lid 1 is raised by a percentage of the amount by which the belastbaar inkomen uit werk en woning plus those posten exceeds the top bracket boundary, capped at that percentage of the deducted posten. The percentage is 3,5% (2020), 6,5% (2021), 9,5% (2022), 12,57% (2023), 12,53% (2024), 12,02% (2025), 11,94% (2026) and 11,91% in the enacted 2027 text. The effective deduction value is 49,50% minus the percentage. The posten are the persoonsgebonden aftrek (onderhoudsverplichtingen including partneralimentatie, weekenduitgaven voor gehandicapten, aftrekbare giften, specifieke zorgkosten, restant persoonsgebonden aftrek voorgaande jaren), the aftrek kosten eigen woning (hypotheekrente, periodieke erfpacht, opstal of beklemming, kosten afsluiten hypotheek, restschuldkosten), the ondernemersfaciliteiten (zelfstandigenaftrek, aftrek speur- en ontwikkelingswerk, meewerkaftrek, startersaftrek bij arbeidsongeschiktheid, stakingsaftrek, mkb-winstvrijstelling) and the vrijstelling voor terbeschikkingstelling van bezittingen. It is applied automatically and shown on the aanslag. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/aftrek-en-kortingen/content/afbouw-tarief-aftrekposten-bij-hoog-inkomen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/belastingberekening. Corrected: the topic file says the https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/aftrek-en-kortingen/content/afbouw-tarief-aftrekposten-bij-hoog-inkomen page states that a € 1.000 deduction is worth at most € 375,60. The fetched https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/aftrek-en-kortingen/content/afbouw-tarief-aftrekposten-bij-hoog-inkomen page has no worked example with amounts, so that sentence is arithmetic, not an official example.

#### Winst uit onderneming into box 1

Verified in part. Belastbare winst uit onderneming is the winst as ondernemer reduced by the ondernemersaftrek and the MKB-winstvrijstelling (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.2). Belastbaar inkomen uit werk en woning is the inkomen uit werk en woning reduced by te verrekenen verliezen, and that inkomen includes the belastbare winst uit onderneming (art. 3.1). The MKB-winstvrijstelling is computed on the winst after the ondernemersaftrek (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/ondernemersaftrek_en_investeringsaftrek and MKB). Vrijgestelde winstbestanddelen are taken out of the winst first (bosbouwvrijstelling, landbouwvrijstelling, vrijstelling kwijtscheldingswinst for the part above verrekenbare verliezen, pension claims under a mandatory scheme, vergoedingen voor bedrijfsbeëindiging, Waz-uitkeringen, certain forest and nature subsidies, benefits under eindheffing, mobiliteitsproject and spitsmijdenproject vrijstellingen), and a medegerechtigde or geldverstrekker gets neither the ondernemersaftrek nor the MKB-winstvrijstelling (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/winst_uit_onderneming). The box 1 saldo is taxed by art. 2.10 lid 1, then lid 2 adds the tariefsaanpassing, and the heffingskortingen reduce the income tax and the premie volksverzekeringen through a belastingdeel and premiedelen (the yearly FISIN heffingskortingen pages, for example https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/heffingskortingen). The full sequence as one statement is not printed on any page read, so the order of the last two steps rests on the article structure and stays an inference.

#### Verzamelinkomen

Verified. Verzamelinkomen is the sum of the belastbaar inkomen uit werk en woning, the belastbaar inkomen uit aanmerkelijk belang and the belastbaar inkomen uit sparen en beleggen, minus te verrekenen persoonsgebonden aftrek (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 2.18). It is the AHK phase-out base from 2025 (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/algemene_heffingskorting/tabel-algemene-heffingskorting-2025, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/algemene_heffingskorting/tabel-algemene-heffingskorting-2026).

#### Algemene heffingskorting

Verified. Granted to every taxpayer, applied automatically in the return and by the employer or uitkeringsinstantie in the payroll. The phase-out base is the belastbaar inkomen uit werk en woning in 2020 to 2024 and the verzamelinkomen from 2025. The phase-out runs from the afbouwpunt to the top bracket boundary, where the korting reaches nil. The AHK and AK afbouwpunten are linked to the wettelijk minimumloon (https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D43301 footnote to Tabel 1). Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/heffingskortingen to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/heffingskortingen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/algemene_heffingskorting/tabel-algemene-heffingskorting-2024 to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/algemene_heffingskorting/tabel-algemene-heffingskorting-2026, https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D43301.

#### Arbeidskorting

Verified. Granted over the arbeidsinkomen, which for an IB-ondernemer includes the winst uit onderneming before the ondernemersaftrek and the MKB-winstvrijstelling and excludes the part earned as medegerechtigde or winstdelende schuldeiser (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2022/heffingskortingen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2025/heffingskortingen). The table has four bands: a build-up percentage, two further build-up steps, then a phase-out of 6% (2020, 2021), 5,860% (2022) or 6,510% (2023 to 2026) to nil. From 2024 the year tables state that if the employer applied a higher AK and the arbeidsinkomen is not above the third-band boundary, the Belastingdienst takes over the employer's amount up to the maximum. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/heffingskortingen to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/heffingskortingen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/arbeidskorting/tabel-arbeidskorting-2024 to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/arbeidskorting/tabel-arbeidskorting-2026.

#### AOW transition year for the heffingskortingen

Verified for 2024 to 2026. The ordinary AHK, AK and IACK tables do not apply in the year in which the AOW age is reached. The amount is calculated with the online form Verzoek of wijziging voorlopige aanslag of that year in Mijn Belastingdienst. A rounding difference of a few euros between the table and the aanslag can occur and the aanslag is correct. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/algemene_heffingskorting/tabel-algemene-heffingskorting-2024 to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/algemene_heffingskorting/tabel-algemene-heffingskorting-2026, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/arbeidskorting/tabel-arbeidskorting-2024 to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/arbeidskorting/tabel-arbeidskorting-2026, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting-2024 to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting-2026. For 2020 to 2023 the FISIN pages read give no rule or amount. UNVERIFIED for those years.

#### Belastingdeel and premiedelen

Verified. Each heffingskorting splits into a belastingdeel (the income-tax part of the first-bracket rate) and premiedelen AOW 17,90%, Anw 0,10% and Wlz 9,65%. Someone not insured for one scheme loses that part, and part-year premieplicht gives a time-proportional premiedeel. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/heffingskortingen to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/heffingskortingen.

#### Payout to the minstverdienende partner

Verified. The payout of unused heffingskortingen to a minstverdienende partner was afgebouwd since 2009: 80% withheld in 2020, 86⅔% in 2021, 93⅓% in 2022. The AK and IACK payouts lapsed from 2023. The AHK payout lapsed from 2023 for people born in 1963 or later and continues for people born before 1 January 1963, who were never in the afbouw. Conditions: same fiscal partner for more than 6 months, the partner owes enough tax, only the part that cannot be set off against own tax and premie is paid. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/heffingskortingen to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/heffingskortingen. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/heffingskortingen says the payout lapses in 2024. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2021/heffingskortingen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2022/heffingskortingen and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2023/heffingskortingen say 2023, and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2023/heffingskortingen shows it ended from 2023. Corrected to 2023.

#### Inkomensafhankelijke combinatiekorting

Verified. 11,45% of the arbeidsinkomen above a threshold, up to a maximum. Applied only in the return or through a voorlopige aanslag, not in the payroll. Child under 12 on 1 January. Through 2024 the child must be registered at the taxpayer's address for at least 6 months (2024 also accepts 6 months in the same household under a Staatscourant approval). From 2025 the child must belong to the household for at least 6 months. A death of the taxpayer or child before the 6 months still gives the korting. No fiscal partner, or one for 6 months or less, or a lower arbeidsinkomen than the partner, and with equal incomes only the older partner. Qualifying children are own, step, adopted, a child from the spouse's earlier relationship or a fiscal partner's child, and a pleegkind only when raised and maintained as own and not for a pleegvergoeding. Residents of the EU, EER, Switzerland or BES can qualify. Co-ouders: the https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting page (covering 2024 to 2026) requires roughly equal care with at least 156 days per calendar year in each household, prorated if co-parenting lasts at least 6 months but not the full year (from 1 April: 117 days). Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting-2024 to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting-2026, the yearly FISIN heffingskortingen pages, for example https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/heffingskortingen.

#### IACK phase-down from 2027

Verified. Belastingplan 2024 (art. IV onderdeel B and art. V onderdeel B) enacted the afbouw of the IACK for 2027 and 2028 (https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D43301). The IACK is phased down in nine steps from 2027 and abolished per 2035, first step € 149 then steps of € 355 at 2025 prices, with an expected IACK of € 2.663 in 2028 and € 2.333 in 2029 subject to indexation (https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2025D26369, answer 1). https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/vraag-en-antwoord/inkomstenbelasting/wat-is-een-heffingskorting-en-welke-heffingskortingen-zijn-er states the maximum is afgebouwd in 9 steps from 2027. The 2027 amount depends on the Belastingplan 2027 indexation proposal (see 2027).

#### Zvw inkomensafhankelijke bijdrage

Verified. Three channels. The werkgeversheffing is paid by the employer or uitkeringsinstantie over loon uit loondienst, DGA loon when insured for the werknemersverzekeringen, bijstand, early-retirement benefits from before 2006 for ziekenfonds-insured people of 2005, and WAO, WIA and WW benefits. The ingehouden bijdrage is withheld from pension, lijfrente and other uitkeringen with loonheffing. The bijdrage by (voorlopige) aanslag covers winst uit Nederland (not the conversion of an oudedagsreserve into a lijfrente), inkomsten uit overig werk, loon from a foreign employer (2020 to 2024 pages), pension from a foreign uitkeringsinstantie and periodieke uitkeringen without loonheffing such as partneralimentatie. The aanslag Zvw comes with the (voorlopige) aanslag inkomstenbelasting. The maximum bijdrage-inkomen applies once per person across channels, so income that already bore a contribution uses up the headroom. Exceptions: militairen in active service or on buitengewoon verlof with retention of military income, deelvissers where another pays 30% or more of the care costs of a sea trip, correction for income that already bore a foreign statutory health contribution, and reduction for the part of the year insured in the Netherlands. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/werk_en_inkomen/zorgverzekeringswet/veranderingen-bijdrage-zvw/percentages-zvw.

#### Zvw base for an IB-ondernemer

Corrected (the topic file left this open). Zvw art. 43 lid 2 onderdeel b includes the belastbare winst uit onderneming determined under afdeling 3.2 Wet IB 2001, except the amount added under art. 3.70 lid 2 for a decrease of the oudedagsreserve. Art. 3.2 Wet IB 2001 defines belastbare winst as winst minus ondernemersaftrek minus MKB-winstvrijstelling. So the Zvw base for a winst-ondernemer is the profit after the ondernemersaftrek and the MKB-winstvrijstelling. Art. 43 lid 3 sets the bijdrage-inkomen at no less than nil and no more than a yearly maximum set by ministerial regulation. Art. 45 lets the minister set the percentages. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/jci1.3:c:BWBR0018450&hoofdstuk=4&paragraaf=4.2&artikel=43&z=2026-01-01&g=2026-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01, both read in the 2026 version only.

#### Zvw at AOW age

Verified in part. https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/site/binaries/site-content/collections/documents/2026/09/15/xvi-volksgezondheid-welzijn-en-sport-rijksbegroting-2027/16-volksgezondheid-welzijn-en-sport.pdf Tabel 77 footnote 2 states that zelfstandigen and gepensioneerden pay the verlaagde IAB. No AOW-age exemption appears in Zvw art. 41 to 45 as read, which is inference from absence (https://wetten.overheid.nl/jci1.3:c:BWBR0018450&hoofdstuk=4&paragraaf=4.2&artikel=43&z=2026-01-01&g=2026-01-01). The FISIN Zvw pages do not mention the AOW age.

#### Zvw declaration and deadlines

UNVERIFIED (not re-opened). The topic file states the same aangifte is used, a voorlopige aanslag Zvw exists and the Zvw follows the IB filing timeline. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet confirms the aanslag Zvw comes with the (voorlopige) aanslag inkomstenbelasting. The filing deadline belongs to the procedure topic.

#### Ondernemer voor de inkomstenbelasting

Verified. Winst uit onderneming requires that the business is run for the taxpayer's account, that the taxpayer can make binding agreements for it and is liable for its debts. Handelsregister registration alone does not decide. The Belastingdienst weighs profit, independence, capital, time, customers, marketing and business risk (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/winst_uit_onderneming). Detail belongs to the ondernemersaftrek topic.

#### Oudedagsreserve

Verified. Additions of 9,44% of the profit with a yearly maximum reduced by beroepspensioenpremie, for an ondernemer who meets the urencriterium and is below AOW age (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/winst_en_verliesrekening_en_balans to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2022/winst_en_verliesrekening_en_balans). Since 2023 no new oudedagsreserve can be formed and an existing reserve can stay on the balance sheet (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/fiscale_reserves/oudedagsreserve). Reduction or release follows on buying a lijfrente with a matching reserve decrease, when the reserve exceeds the ondernemingsvermogen, on cessation, on reaching the AOW age on 1 January, on failing the urencriterium in both the current and the prior year, and on death unless a continuing partner takes it over on request (FOR, as summarised on fetch).

### 3.3 Ondernemersaftrek and MKB-winstvrijstelling

Year files: `ondernemersaftrek.md` in each year folder.
