#### IB-ondernemer status is its own test
Verified. Who is an ondernemer for the inkomstenbelasting is judged by the Belastingdienst per activity, separately from the btw. Someone who is a btw-ondernemer is not automatically an IB-ondernemer, because the Belastingdienst looks at more conditions for the inkomstenbelasting. The Belastingdienst weighs whether profit is made and how much, independence, capital, time spent, number of clients, publicity, entrepreneurial risk and liability for business debts. Activities are judged one by one and only sufficiently connected activities form one source. Activities in the hobby or family sphere do not make someone an IB-ondernemer. Someone who is not an IB-ondernemer reports inkomsten uit overig werk, the profit is calculated the same way, but there is no right to schemes such as the zelfstandigenaftrek or the investeringsaftrek. The statute defines the ondernemer as the taxpayer for whose account a business is run and who is directly bound for its obligations (art. 3.4), and the zelfstandig beroep counts as an onderneming (art. 3.5). The fetched pages do not literally say that KVK registration alone does not make someone an IB-ondernemer, they say only that the Belastingdienst makes its own assessment after registration. Years 2020 to 2027.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/wanneer_bent_u_ondernemer_voor_de_inkomstenbelasting/ and https://ondernemersplein.overheid.nl/bedrijf-starten/inschrijven-bij-kvk/wanneer-bent-u-ondernemer/ and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 (art. 3.4 and 3.5)

#### What the ondernemersaftrek consists of
Corrected. Art. 3.74 lists five parts: the zelfstandigenaftrek, the aftrek voor speur- en ontwikkelingswerk, the meewerkaftrek, the startersaftrek bij arbeidsongeschiktheid and the stakingsaftrek. The startersaftrek is not a separate list item. It is a verhoging of the zelfstandigenaftrek in art. 3.76 lid 3. The research listed the startersaftrek as its own part. The article text is identical in every version 2020 to 2027. Bill 37 022 would delete art. 3.74 onderdeel d (startersaftrek bij arbeidsongeschiktheid) from 1 January 2029 (proposed), and the start-ups bill would delete onderdelen c and d (meewerkaftrek and stakingsaftrek after renumbering) three calendar years after its cuts take effect (proposed). Medegerechtigden get no zelfstandigenaftrek, meewerkaftrek, stakingsaftrek or MKB-winstvrijstelling on their medegerechtigde profit. Years 2020 to 2027.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.74) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/ondernemersaftrek_en_investeringsaftrek

#### Urencriterium, 1.225 hours and the second test
Verified. Art. 3.6 lid 1: at least 1.225 hours in the calendar year on work for one or more businesses from which the taxpayer earns profit as an ondernemer, and either (a) the total time spent on those businesses and on employment and other work is "grotendeels" spent on the businesses, or (b) the ondernemer was not an ondernemer in one or more of the five preceding calendar years. So the second test falls away for anyone with at least one non-ondernemer year in the five-year lookback, and it comes back only after five straight ondernemer years. The text is identical in every version 2020 to 2027. The FISIN handbooks print the lookback window per year (2021: 2016 to 2020, 2023: 2018 to 2022, 2024: 2019 to 2023, 2025: 2020 to 2024, 2026: 2021 to 2025). FISIN 2020 prints 2014 to 2018 and FISIN 2022 prints "20167 tot en met 2021", both misprints against the statute, which gives 2015 to 2019 and 2017 to 2021.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.6) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/ondernemersaftrek_en_investeringsaftrek

#### Urencriterium, no pro-rating for a part year
Verified. Hours cannot be recalculated to the part of the year someone was an ondernemer. The Belastingdienst example: a webhosting business started on 1 July means 6 months as an ondernemer and still at least 1.225 hours in that period. KVK example: a start on 1 May leaves eight months, about 117 working days, and at 8 hours a day 936 hours, so extra hours are needed. Years 2020 to 2027.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/voorwaarden_urencriterium and https://www.kvk.nl/starten/de-magische-1225-uur-alles-over-het-urencriterium

#### Urencriterium, which hours count and proof
Verified. All hours spent on the business count, also non-billable work such as quotes, administration and building the business website. Standby time does not count: a webhosting owner reachable 24 hours a day in the weekend counts only the 4 hours spent fixing the one outage. Hours must be made plausible, for example with an agenda, quotes, timesheets and invoices. Years 2020 to 2027.
Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/voorwaarden_urencriterium

#### Urencriterium, samenwerkingsverband with connected persons
Verified. Art. 3.6 lid 2 to 4: hours for a business that is part of a samenwerkingsverband with connected persons do not count if (a) the taxpayer's work is mainly supporting and such a samenwerkingsverband between non-connected persons would be unusual, or (b) the samenwerkingsverband relates to a business from which a connected person earns profit as an ondernemer but the taxpayer does not. Connected persons are members of the taxpayer's household and blood or affinal relatives in the straight line or members of their household. A samenwerkingsverband with a body in which the taxpayer or a connected person holds an aanmerkelijk belang counts under (b). The Belastingdienst reads "mainly supporting" as 70% or more, with a dentist and dental assistant partnership as example for (a) and an ondermaatschap for (b). Years 2020 to 2027.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 (art. 3.6) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/voorwaarden_urencriterium

#### Pregnancy and the verlaagd urencriterium
Verified. Art. 3.6 lid 5: if the work is interrupted by the taxpayer's pregnancy, it counts as not interrupted during a period equal to the zwangerschaps- en bevallingsverlof for employees. The Belastingdienst states this as non-worked hours counting as worked hours over a total of 16 weeks. The verlaagd urencriterium (art. 3.78a lid 3) is the art. 3.6 test with 1.225 replaced by 800 hours. The Belastingdienst words it as at least 800 and not more than 1.225 hours. It is a condition only of the startersaftrek bij arbeidsongeschiktheid. Years 2020 to 2027.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 (art. 3.6 lid 5 and 3.78a lid 3) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2023/ondernemersaftrek_en_investeringsaftrek

#### Zelfstandigenaftrek, conditions, profit cap and carry-forward
Verified. Art. 3.76: the zelfstandigenaftrek applies to the ondernemer who meets the urencriterium (lid 1). Lid 2 sets the amount for an ondernemer below the AOW age at the start of the year. Lid 4: at the AOW age at the start of the year, the aftrek is 50% of the amount under lid 2 and lid 3, so the startersaftrek is halved too. Lid 5: the aftrek is not more than the profit, except for an ondernemer who qualifies for the startersaftrek. The amount cut off by this cap is niet-gerealiseerde zelfstandigenaftrek, and if the cap sets the aftrek to nil the year still counts as a year with zelfstandigenaftrek for the startersaftrek count. Lid 6: the inspecteur sets the niet-gerealiseerde amount in a beschikking open to bezwaar. Lid 7: it is offset in the next nine calendar years by raising the zelfstandigenaftrek of those years, at most by the amount by which that year's profit exceeds that year's zelfstandigenaftrek, oldest first. Lid 8: the offset is also set by beschikking open to bezwaar. The text is the same 2020 to 2027 apart from the amounts.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 (art. 3.76) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/ondernemersaftrek/zelfstandigenaftrek1/verrekenen_niet_gerealiseerde_zelfstandigenaftrek

#### Startersaftrek, conditions
Verified. Art. 3.76 lid 3: the zelfstandigenaftrek is raised by the startersaftrek if the ondernemer was not an ondernemer in one or more of the five preceding calendar years and the zelfstandigenaftrek was applied not more than twice in that period. It does not apply to an ondernemer who continues a business under art. 14c Wet Vpb 1969 (geruisloze terugkeer uit een bv) where that started in the calendar year or one of the five preceding years. The Belastingdienst adds that it is given at most 3 times in the first 5 years as an ondernemer, that it requires a right to the zelfstandigenaftrek, and that the combined zelfstandigenaftrek and startersaftrek can exceed the profit, so the excess is a loss that can be offset against other box 1 income or carried to other years. The Belastingdienst words the prior-years test as "U was in de afgelopen 5 jaar niet elk jaar ondernemer voor de inkomstenbelasting", which is the same test as the statute. Years 2020 to 2027 (enacted text), with the 2027 and 2028 proposals under 2027.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 (art. 3.76 lid 3) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/ondernemersaftrek/startersaftrek

#### Startersaftrek bij arbeidsongeschiktheid
Verified. Art. 3.78a: for the ondernemer who was not an ondernemer in one or more of the five preceding calendar years, has a right in the calendar year to an arbeidsongeschiktheidsuitkering or to arbeidsondersteuning under the Wajong, does not meet the urencriterium but does meet the verlaagd urencriterium, and has not reached the AOW age at the start of the year. Not for an ondernemer who continues a business under art. 14c Wet Vpb 1969 started in the year or the five years before. The benefits that count are WIA, WAO, WAZ, Wajong, a comparable foreign statutory scheme, a scheme designated by ministerial regulation, and a periodic benefit from an invaliditeit or ongeval insurance. Amount: € 12.000 if not applied in the five preceding years, € 8.000 if applied for one year, € 4.000 if applied for two years, each not more than the profit. The statute has no tier for more than two uses, so there is no aftrek then (FISIN prints € 0). The text and amounts are identical 2020 to 2027.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.78a) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/veranderingen-inkomstenbelasting-2026/ondernemersaftrek-2026/startersaftrek-bij-arbeidsongeschiktheid-2026

#### Aftrek voor speur- en ontwikkelingswerk
Verified. Part of the ondernemersaftrek (art. 3.74, art. 3.77). A base amount plus a starter increase for an ondernemer who was not an ondernemer in one or more of the five preceding years and had the aftrek applied at most twice in that period. FISIN adds that it requires an S&O-verklaring from RVO, at least 500 hours of S&O work and the urencriterium. Amounts are indexed per year (see the year tables). Years 2020 to 2027.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 (art. 3.77) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/ondernemersaftrek_en_investeringsaftrek

#### MKB-winstvrijstelling and the order of application
Verified. Art. 3.79a: the MKB-winstvrijstelling is a percentage of the total profit as ondernemer from one or more businesses after that amount is reduced by the ondernemersaftrek. Art. 3.2: belastbare winst uit onderneming is the profit reduced by the ondernemersaftrek and the MKB-winstvrijstelling. So the order is profit, then the ondernemersaftrek, then the MKB-winstvrijstelling. No urencriterium is needed (the statute has no such condition and the Belastingdienst page names none). No vrijstelling on profit as medegerechtigde. In a loss year the vrijstelling enlarges the fiscal loss, which the Belastingdienst calls disadvantageous. The benefit of the ondernemersaftrek and the MKB-winstvrijstelling is capped through the tariefsaanpassing. Years 2020 to 2027.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 (art. 3.2 and 3.79a) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/mkb_winstvrijstelling

#### Tariefsaanpassing on the ondernemersaftrek and the MKB-winstvrijstelling
Verified. Art. 2.10 lid 2 and 3: if grondslagverminderende posten (expressly the ondernemersaftrek of art. 3.74 and the MKB-winstvrijstelling) are deducted, the tax rises by a percentage of the amount by which taxable box 1 income plus those deductions exceeds the start of the top bracket, but at most that percentage of the deductions. The maximum value of these deductions is the top rate of 49,50% minus that percentage. Percentages per year are in the year tables. Years 2020 to 2027.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 2.10)

#### Meewerkaftrek
Verified. Art. 3.78: for the ondernemer who meets the urencriterium and whose partner works in the business without any pay. The band runs from "gelijk aan of meer dan" the lower number to "minder dan" the upper number: 525 to under 875 hours 1,25%, 875 to under 1.225 hours 2%, 1.225 to under 1.750 hours 3%, 1.750 hours or more 4% of the profit. This settles the boundary-hour gap: exactly 875 hours falls in the 2% band, exactly 1.225 in the 3% band and exactly 1.750 in the 4% band. The profit base excludes profit replacing benefits lost by onteigening, profit from (partial) staking including staking by death, and profit from moving assets abroad or the final settlement of art. 3.60 and 3.61. Art. 3.16 lid 4: pay to the partner below € 5.000 is not deductible from profit. The Belastingdienst therefore treats a partner paid less than € 5.000 as working without pay, and such pay is no income for the partner. Hours must be made plausible. Text identical 2020 to 2027.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.78 and 3.16) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/ondernemersaftrek/meewerkaftrek

#### Stakingsaftrek
Corrected (the oudedagsreserve link moved in 2023). Art. 3.79: the stakingsaftrek applies to profit made with or on the staking of one or more whole businesses. It equals that profit, at most € 3.630, and this maximum is reduced, not below nil, by stakingsaftrek enjoyed in earlier years. For a business continued under doorschuiving on dissolution of a huwelijksgemeenschap (art. 3.59 lid 2), on death (art. 3.62) or to ondernemers (art. 3.63), it applies to the benefits of that business only if it was run for the taxpayer's own account for at least three years. Until 2022 art. 3.79 lid 1 onderdeel b also covered profit from an afneming of the oudedagsreserve after a geruisloze doorschuiving or omzetting (art. 3.59 lid 2, 3.62, 3.63 or 3.65). That onderdeel was removed from art. 3.79 on 1 January 2023 and the same rule now sits in the transitional art. 10a.29 lid 7 for an oudedagsreserve that existed on 31 December 2022. The research cited art. 3.79 lid 1 onderdeel b for all years. The Belastingdienst adds that the maximum is available only once in a lifetime, an unused remainder can be used at a later staking, and there is no aftrek on profit as medegerechtigde.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01 (art. 3.79 and 10a.29) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/ondernemersaftrek/stakingsaftrek

#### Oudedagsreserve additions, 2020 to 2022
Verified. Art. 3.67: the ondernemer who meets the urencriterium and has not reached the AOW age at the start of the calendar year "kan" add to the oudedagsreserve, so it is optional (FISIN 2020: "U kunt elk jaar aan de oudedagsreserve toevoegen, maar dit is niet verplicht"). The urencriterium is not required for the extra addition of art. 3.69 lid 1 onderdeel b. Art. 3.68: the addition is 9,44% of the profit as ondernemer, at most the yearly maximum, reduced by pension premiums charged to that profit, and at most the amount by which the ondernemingsvermogen at the end of the year exceeds the oudedagsreserve at the start of the year. Profit here is profit before addition to and decrease of the reserve, plus those pension premiums, without profit covered by a double-tax relief. The percentage can be changed by AMvB, lowered by 0,36 point per year that the pensioenrichtleeftijd changes, published at least a year ahead. The statute texts of 1 January 2020, 2021 and 2022 all print 9,44%, so no change was made in those years. Art. 3.69: the addition can be raised by the amount of a forced decrease in the previous year (where art. 3.72 lid 3 did not apply) and by taxable periodic benefits from a lijfrente formed from an oudedagsreserve, capped at the ondernemingsvermogen at year end minus the reserve at the start of the year. Art. 3.71: ondernemingsvermogen is the book value of the business capital minus reserves under art. 3.53 other than the oudedagsreserve, corrected for a terugkeerreserve. Art. 3.72 covers more than one business. A negative calculation gives a nil addition (FISIN 2020).
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2021-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01 (art. 3.67 to 3.72) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/winst_en_verliesrekening_en_balans

#### Oudedagsreserve from 2023, end of additions and the afneming rules
Corrected (the legal basis). Articles 3.67 to 3.72 lapsed on 1 January 2023 ("Vervallen per 01-01-2023"), so no addition is possible from 2023. Art. 10a.29 lid 1 keeps art. 3.70, 3.71, 3.72 lid 2 and 3 and 3.73 as they read on 31 December 2022 in force for an oudedagsreserve that existed on 31 December 2022, or at the end of the last broken book year that started before 1 January 2023. The research cited art. 3.70 directly for 2023 to 2027. The afneming rules: the reserve decreases by an amount the taxpayer chooses, at most the lijfrente premiums deducted as uitgaven voor inkomensvoorzieningen in the year, and it decreases by the excess over the ondernemingsvermogen at year end if the business or part of it was staked in the year, the AOW age was reached at the start of the year, or the urencriterium was not met in the year and the previous year. Decreases are added to profit. Art. 10a.29 lid 12 lets the taxpayer deduct lijfrente premiums for converting the reserve up to the decrease of that year, and lid 14 lets premiums paid within six months after the year end count for that year by choice in the return. Art. 10a.29 lid 3 to 5 carry the reserve over to a spouse or partner who continues the business (huwelijksgemeenschap, death, transfer to partner), lid 6 switches off art. 3.65 lid 1 for this reserve, and lid 7 keeps the stakingsaftrek for an afneming after a geruisloze doorschuiving. On death the reserve is settled unless the partner continues the business and takes over the reserve on a request with the return (Belastingdienst). The text of art. 10a.29 is the same 2023 to 2027.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.67 to 3.72 and 10a.29) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/fiscale_reserves/oudedagsreserve

#### Zvw interaction
Verified for 2020 and 2021 only. The belastbare winst uit onderneming is part of the income on which the self-assessed Zvw contribution is levied, up to a maximum contribution income. FISIN 2020 states the contribution is not levied on the omzetting of an oudedagsreserve into a lijfrente. Wet Zvw art. 43 itself was not reopened. Detail belongs to the Zvw topic.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2021/inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet

#### KIA structure and table (art. 3.41)
Verified. The KIA is a per-onderneming deduction, claimed by choice in the aangifte, on the total investment in bedrijfsmiddelen in the calendar year. Five bands: nil up to the first threshold, 28% of the investment up to the second amount, a fixed amount up to the third amount, then the fixed amount minus 7,56% of the part above the third amount up to the top amount, nil above the top amount. The yearly amounts are in each year block and match the research for every year 2020 to 2026. The Belastingdienst year pages phrase the window as "moet u een bedrag tussen € 2.601 en € 353.973 investeren" (2023) and "tussen € 2.901 en € 398.236" (2026). Years 2020 to 2027. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/veranderingen-inkomstenbelasting-2026/investeringsaftrek-2026/kleinschaligheidsinvesteringsaftrek-2026, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-vorige-jaren/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-2023

#### KIA samenwerkingsverband (art. 3.41 lid 3)
Verified. In a samenwerkingsverband the investments for the partnership and the taxpayer's own investments are added, the table is applied to that sum, and the result is multiplied by the taxpayer's own investment amount divided by the sum. The Belastingdienst KIA page adds that each firmant or vennoot takes a share of the investment, normally in proportion to the (over)winst, and another reasonable key is allowed if applied consistently. Years 2020 to 2027. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.41 lid 3, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/investeringsaftrek_en_desinvesteringsbijtelling/kleinschaligheidsinvesteringsaftrek_kia

#### Excluded assets and per-asset minimums (art. 3.45)
Corrected (article reference). The research placed the € 450 and € 2.500 minimums in art. 3.41 and 3.42. They are in art. 3.45. Lid 1 excludes, for every investeringsaftrek, assets intended mainly for certain uses including use in an onderneming whose profit falls under a regeling ter voorkoming van dubbele belasting (onderdeel a), gronden including the ground under buildings except grondverbetering (b), woonhuizen and woonschepen (c), personenauto's as defined in art. 3 Wet BPM 1992 (d), vaartuigen of art. 3.14 lid 1 onderdeel b (e), effecten, vorderingen, goodwill, vergunningen, ontheffingen and concessies (f) and dieren (g). Lid 2 adds for the KIA only: assets intended directly or indirectly mainly to be made available to third parties (a) and assets with an investment amount below € 450 (b). Lid 3: for the MIA lid 1 onderdelen c and d do not apply, so woonhuizen and personenauto's can still earn MIA. Lid 4 adds for the EIA and MIA: assets made available mainly to non-residents or to foreign enterprises (a) and assets with an investment amount below € 2.500 (b). Lid 5 lets the Minister disapply lid 1 onderdeel a onder 2° and lid 4 onderdeel a. RVO phrases the MIA/Vamil floor as "minimaal € 2.500 per aanvraag". Years 2020 to 2027, same amounts in every version. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2021-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.45, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01, https://www.rvo.nl/subsidies-financiering/mia-vamil/ondernemers/voorwaarden

#### Excluded obligations, including assets bought from an own BV (art. 3.46)
Verified. No investeringsaftrek for obligations between the taxpayer and members of the household (a), blood or in-law relatives in the direct line or their household members (b), heirs to an estate the asset belongs to (c), and a person holding at least one third of a lichaam and that lichaam (d). The Minister can disapply lid 1 (lid 2), which is the ontheffing route. FISIN: buying from "de bv waarvan u voor ten minste een derde deel aandeelhouder bent" gives "in principe geen recht op investeringsaftrek". Years 2020 to 2027. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.46, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/ondernemersaftrek_en_investeringsaftrek

#### Payment cap for an asset not yet in use (art. 3.44)
Corrected (article reference, the rule itself holds). Lid 1: if the asset is not in use at the end of the calendar year, the deduction is taken over an amount equal to the payments made, and the excess follows in later years. Lid 2: at staking of the onderneming the full investeringsaftrek is taken in the year of staking. Applies to KIA, EIA and MIA. Years 2020 to 2027. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.44, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/ondernemersaftrek_en_investeringsaftrek

#### EIA and MIA conditions
Verified. Only new assets ("niet eerder gebruikt") qualify. A second-hand, revised, refurbished or rebuilt asset does not. Costs to adapt a second-hand asset to the Milieulijst requirements can qualify, as can (second-hand) parts bought from several suppliers and installed oneself, as voortbrengingskosten. RVO treats a vehicle as new if used for less than 6 months and at most 6.000 km. No MIA on costs that already received EIA, Vamil remains possible. EIA and MIA cannot be combined for one asset. EIA and MIA can each be combined with the KIA. The EIA asset must be on the Energielijst, the MIA and Vamil asset on the Milieulijst. RVO states a maximum of € 25 miljoen per bedrijfsmiddel and per applicant per year for MIA/Vamil, unless the Milieulijst sets a lower maximum (current RVO page, year not stated). Other subsidies for the same asset reduce the investment amount. The research rule that a medegerechtigde or geldverstrekker cannot use EIA or MIA was not found on the fetched pages (UNVERIFIED). Years 2020 to 2027. Sources: https://www.rvo.nl/subsidies-financiering/mia-vamil/ondernemers, https://www.rvo.nl/subsidies-financiering/mia-vamil/ondernemers/voorwaarden, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/investeringsaftrek_en_desinvesteringsbijtelling/energie_investeringsaftrek_eia, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-vorige-jaren/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-2023

#### RVO notification deadline for EIA, MIA and Vamil
Verified. Apply within 3 months after signing the koopovereenkomst or placing the order (aanschafkosten), within 3 months after the quarter in which voortbrengingskosten were made, and milieu-advieskosten together with those costs under the same deadlines. The Milieulijst valid on the signing or order date applies. FISIN 2020 to 2026 all say "U meldt uw investering binnen 3 maanden bij de Rijksdienst voor Ondernemend Nederland (RVO)." Years 2020 to 2027. Sources: https://www.rvo.nl/subsidies-financiering/mia-vamil/ondernemers, https://www.rvo.nl/subsidies-financiering/mia-vamil/ondernemers/voorwaarden, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/ondernemersaftrek_en_investeringsaftrek

#### MIA categories and Milieulijst codes
Verified. From 1 January 2022 the Milieulijst maps codes starting with F or G to categorie I (45%), A or D to categorie II (36%) and B or E to categorie III (27%). Codes starting with A, B, C or F qualify for 75% Vamil. Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2021-47737.html (Milieulijst 2022, in force 1 January 2022), https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-vorige-jaren/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-2023

#### Vamil (art. 3.31)
Verified. The costs of a designated milieu-bedrijfsmiddel "kunnen door de belastingplichtige voor 75 percent willekeurig worden afgeschreven", the rest follows the normal schedule. Art. 3.30a lid 1 (bodemwaarde limit) does not apply to these assets. Only assets not yet common in the Netherlands, not used before and that materially reduce environmental harm can be designated. RVO: "Uw totale belastingvoordeel (MIA en Vamil) kan netto oplopen tot ruim 14% van het investeringsbedrag." Same 75% in the 2020, 2021, 2023, 2024, 2025, 2026 and 2027 texts. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2021-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.31, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01, https://www.rvo.nl/subsidies-financiering/mia-vamil/ondernemers

#### Desinvesteringsbijtelling (art. 3.47)
Verified. Lid 1: if in a calendar year goods are disposed of against transfer prices for more than the yearly threshold, a percentage of the transfer prices equal to the investeringsaftrek percentage taken is added to profit. Lid 2: only for disposals within five years after the start of the calendar year of the investment, and never over more than the investment amount over which aftrek was taken. Lid 3 treats as disposal: (a) withdrawing the good from the onderneming, (b) changing its destination so that it meets art. 3.45 lid 2 onderdeel a (mainly made available to third parties, KIA) or lid 4 onderdeel a (made available to non-residents, EIA and MIA), (c) a change bringing it under art. 3.45 lid 1 onderdeel a onder 2°. Lid 4: in those cases the waarde in het economische verkeer counts as transfer price. Lid 5: undoing an investment, or receiving a vermindering, teruggaaf or vergoeding, counts as disposal for that amount. Lid 6: undoing includes not having paid at least 25% of the investment amount within twelve months after the obligation unless the asset is in use, and not having taken the asset into use within three years after the start of the investment year. Lid 7: the Minister may disapply lid 3 onderdeel c and lid 6. The Belastingdienst page phrases rental as counting only for the KIA, not for EIA or MIA, and adds staking of (part of) the onderneming within the term. Years 2020 to 2027. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.47, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/investeringsaftrek_en_desinvesteringsbijtelling/desinvesteringsbijtelling

#### Ordinary depreciation (art. 3.30)
Verified. Lid 1: yearly depreciation is the part of the not yet depreciated aanschaffings- or voortbrengingskosten attributable to the year. Lid 2: per year at most 10% of the cost for goodwill and at most 20% for other bedrijfsmiddelen, so at least 10 and 5 years. Lid 3: voortbrengingskosten of immateriële activa may be written off at once in the year of voortbrenging. Lid 4: voorwerpen van geringe waarde normally treated as current costs are written off at once in the year of acquisition or voortbrenging. Same text in 2020 to 2027. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2021-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.30, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01

#### Building depreciation and bodemwaarde (art. 3.30a)
Verified. Lid 1: depreciation on a building only while its book value is above the bodemwaarde, at most the difference. Lid 2: a building, its parts, the ground under it and aanhorigheden are one bedrijfsmiddel, detachable werktuigen separate. Lid 3 bodemwaarde: 2020 to 2023 the WOZ-waarde for a gebouw ter belegging and 50% of the WOZ-waarde for another (own use) building, from 2024 one bodemwaarde "de WOZ-waarde van het gebouw". Lid 4: the WOZ-waarde is the value set by beschikking under the Wet WOZ for the calendar year of the depreciation. The 2026 text also has lid 5 (co-owned building pro rata), lid 6 (ground owned by an unrelated party reduces the WOZ-waarde) and lid 7 to 10 (related persons and verbonden lichaam). The 2021 and 2022 texts were opened this time and both print the 50% rule. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.30a, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01

#### Willekeurige afschrijving for starters (URWA art. 7 to 9)
Corrected (article numbers and the cap amount). Statutory basis art. 3.34 Wet IB 2001 (willekeurige afschrijving on assets designated by ministerial regulation that serve economic development, including entrepreneurship). URWA art. 7 lid 1: designated are assets for which obligations were entered or voortbrengingskosten made in a calendar year in which the verhoogde zelfstandigenaftrek of art. 3.76 lid 3 (the startersaftrek) applies. Lid 2: also the year immediately before such a year if the ordinary zelfstandigenaftrek does not apply in that earlier year. Lid 3: only for costs made in an onderneming from which the taxpayer earns winst as ondernemer. Art. 8: willekeurige afschrijving is allowed "voor zover het gezamenlijke bedrag van die kosten in het kalenderjaar niet uitgaat boven het in de tabel van artikel 3.41, tweede lid, van de wet opgenomen maximum bedrag waarover kleinschaligheidsinvesteringsaftrek kan worden verkregen", and not for assets already randomly depreciated on another ground. Art. 9: the art. 3.38 period is five years from the start of the year of the obligation or costs. Art. 12: ten years for the zeeschepen of art. 10. The research cited "art. 7 and 10", the right articles are 7, 8 and 9 (and 12 for zeeschepen). The Belastingdienst adds: eenmanszaak, maatschap, cv or vof, startersaftrek conditions met, only assets that qualify for investeringsaftrek, free choice of assets, and the minimum total investment of the KIA table does not apply. Herwaardering to the normal book value (the difference is winst) within 5 years (10 for zeeschepen) if the asset is rented out, falls under a double-tax treaty or is used in the bosbedrijf. Cap amount: the URWA wording names the maximum investment amount over which KIA can be obtained, which is the top of the art. 3.41 lid 2 table (2026: € 398.236, the Belastingdienst window "tussen € 2.901 en € 398.236"). The baseline instead used the end of the 28% band (€ 58.238 for 2020, € 69.765 for 2024, € 71.683 for 2026). This is a reading of a cross-reference, the pages do not print the cap as a number. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0012035/2025-01-01/0 art. 7 to 9 and 12, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.34, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/afschrijving/willekeurige_afschrijving/startende_ondernemer, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/veranderingen-inkomstenbelasting-2026/investeringsaftrek-2026/kleinschaligheidsinvesteringsaftrek-2026

#### Temporary willekeurige afschrijving 2023 (URWA art. 13 to 15)
Corrected (article numbers, the research cited art. 11). Art. 13 lid 1: assets not used before, obligations entered or voortbrengingskosten made in calendar year 2023, taken into use by the taxpayer before 1 January 2026. Lid 2 exclusions: gebouwen, schepen, vliegtuigen, bromfietsen, motorrijwielen, personenauto's except for beroepsvervoer over de weg or 0 gram CO2 per km, immateriële activa, dieren, assets mainly made available to third parties (short rental to successive hirers excepted), public roads and paths, assets already randomly depreciated on another ground. Lid 3: the 1 January 2026 date moves by the days justified when special circumstances delayed use. Art. 14: once, at most 50% of the costs, in the year of the obligation or costs. Art. 15: the period in which the asset must keep meeting art. 13 ends 31 December 2025. The Belastingdienst adds that only 2023 costs count and that an asset not yet in use is capped at the amount paid in the investment year. IB and Vpb both. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0012035/2025-01-01/0 art. 13 to 15, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-vorige-jaren/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-2023, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2022-33377.html

#### No temporary scheme found for 2020, 2021 or 2022
UNVERIFIED (absence). The Eindejaarsregeling 2022 toelichting says the 2023 scheme is "op hoofdlijnen gelijk aan de regeling die gold voor de jaren 2009, 2010, 2011 en (deels) 2013" and names no other year. The URWA 2025 text lists no 2020 or 2021 scheme. An absence cannot be confirmed on one page. Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2022-33377.html, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0012035/2025-01-01/0

#### Gemengde kosten drempel and 80% keuze (art. 3.15)
Costs connected with voedsel, drank en genotmiddelen, representatie (including recepties, feestelijke bijeenkomsten en vermaak) and congressen, seminars, symposia, excursies, studiereizen en dergelijke are not deductible up to a yearly drempel. The related travel and stay count as part of these posts (lid 2). The restriction does not apply where the enterprise directly makes or trades these goods or services and they are part of turnover (lid 3). No onttrekking is taken into account for the taxpayer himself on these costs (lid 4). Instead of the drempel the taxpayer may elect in the return to deduct 80% of these costs (lid 5). The same 80% applies to resultaatgenieters. The drempel is indexed under art. 10.1, which lists art. 3.15 eerste lid only, so the kilometre amount in lid 6 is not indexed. Drempel by statute: € 4.700 (2020, 2021), € 4.800 (2022), € 5.100 (2023), € 5.600 (2024), € 5.700 (2025, 2026, and the 2027 base text). Verified. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.15, art. 10.1) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/veranderingen-inkomstenbelasting-2026/drempel-beperkt-aftrekbare-kosten-2026

#### Content of the gemengde-kosten posts
Voedsel covers zakenlunches and zakendiners including fooien. Drank covers koffie, thee, melk en frisdrank. Genotmiddelen covers sigaretten en sigaren. Voedsel, drank en genotmiddelen used privately are never deductible. Representatie includes recepties, and relatiegeschenken generally fall under representatie. Relatiegeschenken are a subcategory of representatiekosten per the wetsgeschiedenis of art. 8a Wet IB 1964. Verified (current page, no year label, and the 2020 beleidsbesluit). Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/zakelijke_kosten/overzicht-mogelijk-aftrekbare-zakelijke-kosten and https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2020-25430.html (section 3.3)

#### Drempel not time-proportioned
The art. 3.15 lid 1 amount is not adjusted proportionally when a taxpayer starts an enterprise during the calendar year. For a boekjaar longer than 12 months the fixed amount is not increased proportionally either. Verified. Source: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2020-25430.html (Besluit van 23 april 2020, nr. 2020-54978, section 3.1)

#### Worker meals
Food costs that qualify as loonkosten are fully deductible. When an employer gives free meals to staff, the art. 3.15 restriction applies only to the part of the meal cost that is not loon in natura. Verified. Source: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2020-25430.html (section 3.2)

#### Vpb variant of the drempel (for a BV)
Under art. 8 lid 5 Wet Vpb 1969 the art. 3.15 restriction applies to a body when there are one or more employees in the Wet LB sense in the year. The fixed amount is replaced by 0,4% of the employees' wage total when that is higher than the fixed amount. The election percentage is 73,5% instead of 80%. It applies per body, so each company in a holding structure with an employee has its own fixed amount, and only in a fiscale eenheid does it apply once, at the parent. Verified for 2020 and 2026 in the statute. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2020-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2026-01-01 (art. 8 lid 5), https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2020-25430.html (section 3.4)

#### Congress, course and study-trip travel cap (art. 3.16 lid 2 onderdeel f and lid 8)
Travel and stay for cursussen en opleidingen voor studie en beroep, and for congressen, seminars, symposia, excursies, studiereizen en dergelijke, are not deductible above € 1.500 per year. The cap does not apply when the nature of the work makes attendance necessary. The amount is not indexed and is the same in every year 2020 to 2027. Verified. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.16 lid 2 f and lid 8) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/zakelijke_kosten/overzicht-mogelijk-aftrekbare-zakelijke-kosten

#### Other fixed exclusions in art. 3.16 (telefoon, kleding, literatuur, private apparatuur, partner)
Not deductible in every year 2020 to 2027: telefoonabonnementen for connections in the taxpayer's own home (lid 2 a), literatuur except vakliteratuur (lid 2 b), kleding except werkkleding (lid 2 c, werkkleding defined in lid 5), persoonlijke verzorging (lid 2 d), costs of privately owned or privately rented muziekinstrumenten, geluidsapparatuur, gereedschappen, computers en andere dergelijke apparatuur and beeldapparatuur (lid 3), and pay to the partner when it is lower than € 5000 (lid 4). Persons in the household are treated as the taxpayer (lid 11). Verified. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.16)

#### Telefoon (practical rule)
A private telephone subscription is not deductible, but business calls made through it are. A business telephone subscription is deductible, but call costs for private calls on it are not. The overview page prints Telefoonabonnement thuis 0% and Zakelijke telefoongesprekken thuis 100%. Verified (current pages, no year label, statutory basis art. 3.16 lid 2 a unchanged 2020 to 2027). Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/werk-en-inkomen/content/welke-kosten-mag-ik-aftrekken-van-mijn-bijverdiensten and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/zakelijke_kosten/overzicht-mogelijk-aftrekbare-zakelijke-kosten

#### Laptop and computer
The income-tax treatment follows vermogensetikettering. Apparatuur en instrumenten that do not belong to the ondernemingsvermogen are 0% deductible (art. 3.16 lid 3). A bedrijfsmiddel used 10% or less for business is verplicht privévermogen, 90% or more is verplicht ondernemingsvermogen, in between is keuzevermogen within reasonable bounds. On the balance sheet the asset is depreciated, and an asset with an aanschafwaarde under € 450 is expensed at once. Verified (current pages, statutory basis unchanged 2020 to 2027). Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/vermogensetikettering-bedrijfsmiddel and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 (art. 3.16 lid 3)

#### € 450 rule and depreciation
A purchase under € 450, or a cost that covers one year, is deducted in one go in the year it is incurred (kleine kantoorartikelen, vakliteratuur, onderhoud van de auto). A purchase of € 450 or more used longer than a year is depreciated taking the restwaarde into account. Costs made before the start of the business are deductible when made for the work. Receipts and invoices must be kept. Verified (current page, no year label). Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/werk-en-inkomen/content/welke-kosten-mag-ik-aftrekken-van-mijn-bijverdiensten

#### Keuze herzien and heretikettering after a change in the law
A keuzevermogen choice can be revised until the aanslag for the year of the choice is irrevocable, afterwards only on a bijzondere omstandigheid. Private use of an asset in the ondernemingsvermogen is a privéonttrekking. A change in the law can be such a bijzondere omstandigheid when the taxpayer makes plausible that he would have chosen differently under the new rule. The Kennisgroep applies this to the youngtimer change, revision is possible after the new rule takes effect on 1 January 2027. Verified. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/vermogensetikettering-bedrijfsmiddel and https://kennisgroepen.belastingdienst.nl/publicaties/kg21320261-heretikettering-bij-wetswijziging-privegebruik-auto/ (KG:213:2026:1, 6 March 2026)

#### Mixed character costs
Costs with both a business and a personal character are deductible only for the business part, and not at all when the personal character dominates. Examples with only a business part or a restriction: huur and onderhoud of a pand split into business and private parts, telefoonkosten, a car of the business, congressen, studiereizen, symposia, seminars en excursies, zakenlunches en diners, recepties and other festive gatherings. Not deductible at all: a diploma with substantial benefit outside the business (rijbewijs), health costs (pedicure, zorgverzekering), personal care such as clothing and the kapper. Werkkleding is deductible. Verified (current page). Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/zakelijke_kosten/zakelijk_of_prive

#### Werkruimte thuis (art. 3.16 lid 1, 13 and from 2025 lid 14)
Costs of a workspace including its inrichting in a home that is not ondernemingsvermogen are not deductible unless the workspace is a naar verkeersopvatting zelfstandig part of the home and an income test is met. With another workspace outside the home, the combined winst, loon and resultaat uit overige werkzaamheden must be earned mainly (hoofdzakelijk) in the home workspace. Without another workspace, it must be earned mainly in or from the home workspace and to a significant extent (in belangrijke mate) in it. Lid 13 applies the same test to a rented home whose huurrecht is ondernemingsvermogen. The official factors for zelfstandig are an own entrance or opgang, own sanitary facilities, and that the space could be rented to a third party. A loose desk in the living room or a bedroom furnished as an office is not zelfstandig. Overview page: Werkruimte thuis 0%, met uitzonderingen. From 1 January 2025 art. 3.16 lid 14 adds that costs connected with an owned home in the ondernemingsvermogen that serves as hoofdverblijf are not deductible when they would not be deductible under lid 1 if the home were private. The MvT calls this a codification of case law: for a non-zelfstandige workspace in such a home the huurderslasten (inrichting such as meubilair, stoffering, behang- en schilderwerk, and gas, water en licht) are not deductible. Corrected for 2025 onward: the Belastingdienst page still presents the second route (owner and home in ondernemingsvermogen) without this limit. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01 (art. 3.16), https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36602-3.html (MvT Belastingplan 2025, section 5.3), https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/werkruimte_in_de_woning

#### Geldboetes and dwangsommen (art. 3.14 lid 1 onderdelen c, d, e, i)
Not deductible: costs connected with geldboeten from a strafrechter, settlement payments to avoid prosecution, strafbeschikkingen, bestuurlijke boeten and comparable foreign fines, tuchtrechtelijke boeten, EU fines (c), costs connected with a misdrijf for which the taxpayer was irrevocably convicted (d) or for which a strafbeschikking became irrevocable (e), and dwangsommen under afdeling 5.3.2 Awb (i). The text is the same in every version 2020 to 2027. Wahv verkeersboeten are caught, including payments by car-rental companies following such fines. Overview page: Geldboetes 0%. Verified. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.14), https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2020-25430.html (section 1.1)

#### Timing rule for costs around a fine or dwangsom
Costs made before the geldboete is imposed are not costs that verband houden met the imposed fine and stay deductible. Costs made after imposition that relate to the fine are not deductible. For a dwangsom the line is the verbeurdverklaring. Costs of an internal legal department that in any way relate to imposed fines or forfeited dwangsommen are also caught. Verified. Source: https://kennisgroepen.belastingdienst.nl/publicaties/kg21320245-aftrek-kosten-en-lasten-in-verband-met-geldboeten-en-dwangsommen/ (published 11-03-2024)

#### Misdrijf scope
Only costs connected with the misdrijf for which the taxpayer was actually convicted (or which was taken ad informandum, or settled by transactie or strafbeschikking) are excluded. Example in the besluit: convicted only for possession of hennepplanten, not for trade, so only the costs of possession are excluded. Verified. Source: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2020-25430.html (section 2)

#### Studiekosten as a business cost
Deductible when incurred in the interest of the enterprise and only to maintain existing vakkennis. Costs to acquire new knowledge are not deductible as business costs. The boundary is hard to draw. Travel and stay for courses fall under the € 1.500 cap of art. 3.16 lid 2 f. Verified (current pages, no year label, the 2022 abolition removed only the persoonsgebonden route). Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/zakelijke_kosten/studiekosten-voor-ondernemers and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/zakelijke_kosten/overzicht-mogelijk-aftrekbare-zakelijke-kosten

#### End of the studiekostenaftrek (persoonsgebonden aftrek)
Up to and including 2021 scholingsuitgaven (lesgeld, cursusgeld, collegegeld, examengeld, promotiekosten, required leermiddelen excluding computers) were deductible above a drempel of € 250 and, outside the standaardstudieperiode, up to € 15 000 (art. 6.27 to 6.30). No deduction when there was a right to studiefinanciering for the study. The Wet afschaffing fiscale aftrek scholingsuitgaven (Stb. 2019, 514) took effect on 1 January 2022 by the Besluit van 12 juli 2021, Stb. 2021, 349. In the 2022 statute art. 6.27, 6.28 and 6.30 are marked vervallen per 01-01-2022. Art. 10a.16 keeps the old rules for scholingsuitgaven relating to study years up to and including 2014/2015 (the prestatiebeurs case). Verified. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2021-349.html, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 (art. 10a.16)

#### Niet-omgezette prestatiebeurs exception
When the diplomatermijn (usually 10 years) has passed and DUO definitively did not convert the prestatiebeurs into a gift, the taxpayer deducts in that return year the amount he could not deduct earlier because of the prestatiebeurs. Caps: € 1.693 per study year (mbo) and € 2.443 per study year (hbo or wo), part-year € 141,09 per month (mbo) and € 203,59 per month (hbo or wo). When the actual prestatiebeurs was lower, the lower amount applies. The € 250 drempel applies per person. Verified for 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025 in FISIN. For 2026 FISIN narrows the case to an extended diplomatermijn (maximum 15 years), so only study years 2010/2011 to 2014/2015. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2022/aftrek_studiekosten, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2023/aftrek_studiekosten, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2024/aftrek_studiekosten, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2025/aftrek_studiekosten, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/aftrek_studiekosten

#### STAP-budget
The non-fiscal replacement took effect at the same moment as the abolition (art. 29 of the regeling links its start to the Wet afschaffing, and Stb. 2021, 349 states it applies from 1 January 2022). Maximum subsidy € 1.000 including btw per scholing (art. 7), six application windows per calendar year. The regeling is marked "Regeling vervallen per 01-01-2024". Corrected: the research said at most € 1.000 per year, the regeling text says per scholing. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0045419/2023-01-01 and https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2021-349.html

#### Kilometre allowance for a private vehicle (art. 3.15 lid 6 and art. 3.17 lid 1 onderdeel b)
Costs of a vehicle that belongs to the private assets or is rented privately (auto, motor, fiets) are limited to a fixed amount per business kilometre. The amount is all-in: brandstof, verzekering, tol and parkeren cannot be deducted separately. Household members are treated as the taxpayer (art. 3.15 lid 7). Statute: € 0,19 (2020, 2021, 2022), € 0,21 (2023), € 0,23 (2024, 2025, 2026 and the 2027 text). For 2026 an approving beleidsbesluit allows € 0,25 retroactively to 1 January 2026. The amount is not indexed under art. 10.1. Verified. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2026-18302.html, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/veranderingen-inkomstenbelasting-2026/zakelijk-gebruik-privevervoermiddel-2026

#### Openbaar vervoer, taxi en vliegtuig
Business travel by openbaar vervoer, taxi or vliegtuig is deductible at 100% of the actual costs. The costs must be shown, for example with the train ticket or an OV-chipkaart trip overview. Verified (current page). Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/zakelijke_kosten/overzicht-mogelijk-aftrekbare-zakelijke-kosten

#### Verhuizing and dubbele huisvesting (art. 3.17 lid 1 onderdeel a)
Moving costs: the cost of moving the inboedel plus € 7750. Housing outside the place of residence: for at most two years. Unchanged 2020 to 2027. Verified. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.17)

#### Auto: vermogensetikettering
A car is verplicht ondernemingsvermogen when used 90% or more for business, or when the entrepreneur drives more business than private kilometres and at most 500 private kilometres a year. Used 90% or more privately it is verplicht privévermogen. All other cases are keuzevermogen. Verified (current page). Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/vermogensetikettering-bedrijfsmiddel

#### Auto: bijtelling base, 500 km rule and cap (art. 3.20)
When a car is also available for private use, at least 22% of the waarde van de auto is an onttrekking per year (lid 1 a), or 35% when the car was first taken into use more than the age threshold ago (lid 1 b). The car is deemed available for private use unless it is shown that private use is at most 500 km a year. Woon-werkverkeer is not private (lid 3). With a rittenregistratie or otherwise showing at most 500 private km, the onttrekking is nil. A keurmerk rittenregistratiesysteem is assumed to be sluitend, though trips can still be checked. The onttrekking is taken into account only insofar as it exceeds what the taxpayer has borne himself (lid 4), so it is capped at the total autokosten including depreciation. Auto means personenauto or bestelauto, except a bestelauto that is (almost) exclusively suited for goods (lid 5 a), for which private km x the actual cost per km is settled. A verklaring uitsluitend zakelijk gebruik bestelauto is possible (lid 6 to 10). Age threshold: 15 years (2020 to 2025), 16 years (2026), 25 years in the enacted 2027 text. Verified. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.20), https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/privegebruik_auto/privegebruik_auto

#### Auto: waarde van de auto
The waarde is the catalogusprijs within the meaning of art. 9 Wet BPM 1992 plus bpm, which the Belastingdienst describes as the original cataloguswaarde including btw and bpm and including accessories fitted by or for the manufacturer or importer before the kenteken was issued. The RDW site carries it for most cars. For an old car the waarde in het economische verkeer replaces it. The statute (art. 3.20 lid 5 b) keeps the words "meer dan vijftien jaar" in the 2026 and 2027 texts, while lid 1 moved to 16 (2026) and 25 (2027 text). The Belastingdienst applies 16 years for the WEV switch from 2026. Belastingplan 2027 would repair lid 5 b to 16 retroactively to 1 January 2026 (art. I onderdeel E onder 2 with art. LXIII lid 1 a). When the threshold is crossed mid-year the year is split by month. A laadpaal for a zero-emission car in the ondernemingsvermogen is deductible and does not raise the catalogue price. Verified. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 (art. 3.20 lid 5), https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/privegebruik_auto/waarde_van_de_auto, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-37022-2.html

#### Auto: zero-emission reduction (art. 3.20 lid 2 and lid 11)
The 22% is reduced by a percentage of the value for a car with 0 g CO2 per km, capped in euros. The reduction that applied at first admission stays fixed for 60 months from the first day of the month after first admission (lid 11). After that the rules then in force apply. Cars on waterstof are uncapped in every year. Cars with integrated solar panels are uncapped from the 2021 text (panels of at least 1 kilowattpiek, battery without lead), and from the 2024 text the solar test is that the panel power in wattpiek divided by consumption in wattuur per km is at least 7. By year of first admission: 2020 14% off capped € 6300 (8% up to € 45.000), 2021 10% off capped € 4.000 (12% up to € 40.000), 2022 6% off capped € 2.100 (16% up to € 35.000), 2023 and 2024 6% off capped € 1.800 (16% up to € 30.000), 2025 5% off capped € 1.500 (17% up to € 30.000), 2026 4% off capped € 1.200 (18% up to € 30.000), 2027 text 2% off capped € 600 (20% up to € 30.000). Verified (statute). Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 3.20 lid 2), https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/veranderingen-inkomstenbelasting-2026/bijtelling-privegebruik-auto-2026

#### Auto: pre-2017 cars (art. 10a.4)
For a car first admitted on or before 31 December 2016 the minimum under art. 3.20 lid 1 a is 25% instead of 22%. In the 2020 to 2025 texts art. 10a.4 also keeps the reduction rules that applied at first registration for 60 months. It does not affect the 35% rule for old cars. Verified. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 (art. 10a.4)

#### Fiets of the business (art. 3.20a)
When a fiets (including an e-bike or speed-pedelec that also runs on muscle power) of the business is also available privately, 7% of the consumentenadviesprijs is an onttrekking per year, capped at the costs borne. Availability for woon-werkverkeer counts as private availability. A lid 5 added by Stb. 2025, 444 with retroactive effect to 1 January 2020 sets the onttrekking at nil when the fiets is stalled no more than incidentally at the home address. Verified. Source: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 (art. 3.20a, note on terugwerkende kracht also shown in the 2020 to 2025 texts)

#### BTW on a private car used for business
VAT on use and maintenance of a private car also used for business is deductible only for the part used for belaste omzet. Woon-werkverkeer counts as private use for the btw, though it is business for the inkomstenbelasting. In a BV the DGA is not the btw-ondernemer, he counts as an employee for this purpose. Without a sluitende kilometeradministratie all VAT may be deducted and corrected at year end by 1,5% of the catalogue price including btw and bpm in rubriek 1d, pro rata for a part year and never above the VAT deducted on use and maintenance. Verified (current page). Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_aftrekken/btw_en_de_auto/zakelijk_gebruik_van_de_priveauto/zakelijk_gebruik_van_de_priveauto

#### KOR and the income-tax cost base
Deduct costs excluding VAT when the VAT is deductible in the btw-aangifte. When no btw-aangifte is filed or the VAT may not be deducted (the KOR case), deduct the costs including VAT in the IB return. Under the KOR no VAT is charged or paid, no VAT on business costs and investments is deducted (also not VAT paid in another EU country), earlier deductions may need herziening, the low bestelauto mrb rate stays available, exit is possible from the first day of the next quarter and is immediate once turnover exceeds € 20.000 in a calendar year, and the choice is per ondernemer covering all subnummers. Verified (current pages, the KOR threshold history by year belongs to the BTW topic). Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/werk-en-inkomen/content/welke-kosten-mag-ik-aftrekken-van-mijn-bijverdiensten and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/hoe_werkt_de_btw/kleineondernemersregeling/wat-betekent-meedoen-met-de-kleineondernemersregeling/
