#### System per year (Verified)
2020 to 2022 use the old band method (rendementsklassen I and II in art. 5.2 lid 1). From 1 January 2023 the Overbruggingswet box 3 (law of 21 December 2022, Stb. 2022, 534, art. II: "Deze wet treedt in werking met ingang van 1 januari 2023") applies the category method to everyone. The 2027 and 2028 texts of the act still carry the category method in art. 5.2. The Wet werkelijk rendement box 3 (bill 36 748) is not law on 2026-10-11. Belastingdienst says the fictitious system stays until new legislation takes effect, "probably 1 January 2028".
Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2022-534.html https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/wat-is-mijn-werkelijk-rendement https://www.eerstekamer.nl/wetsvoorstel/36748_wet_werkelijk_rendement_box

#### Old band method 2020 to 2022 (Verified)
The grondslag sparen en beleggen is split over three bands. Band 1 is 67% rendementsklasse I (savings) and 33% klasse II (investments). Band 2 is 21% and 79%. Band 3 is 0% and 100%. The statute writes the limits as "meer dan / niet meer dan" (2020: € 72.797 and € 1.005.572, 2021: € 50.000 and € 950.000, 2022: € 50.650 and € 962.350). The Belastingdienst page writes the same limits one euro higher as "tot / vanaf" (€ 72.798, € 1.005.573, € 50.001, € 950.001, € 50.651, € 962.351). Both describe the same bands. Rates 30% (2020), 31% (2021, 2022) in art. 2.13.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/oude-berekening-box-3-inkomen https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2021-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01

#### Most favourable method (Verified)
For 2021 and 2022 the Belastingdienst computes box 3 income under the old method and the current method and applies the more favourable one to the definitive assessment. "Ook als u over de periode 2017-2022 rechtsherstel hebt gehad, passen wij de meest gunstige methode toe."
Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/oude-berekening-box-3-inkomen

#### Kerstarrest 24 December 2021 (Verified, one part UNVERIFIED)
Hoge Raad 24 December 2021, ECLI:NL:HR:2021:1963. The memorie van toelichting to the Wet rechtsherstel describes the ruling as a breach of "artikel 1 EP, in samenhang met artikel 14 EVRM": the forfaitaire system from 2017 cannot pass the proportionality test. Forfaits are allowed but must approach reality as closely as possible. The Rijksoverheid timeline confirms the ruling and names the right to property and the discrimination ban. The claim that the Hoge Raad itself ordered rechtsherstel on the basis of the actual return stays UNVERIFIED, because hogeraad.nl returned HTTP 403 again on 2026-10-11. The MvT stresses that the legislator did not choose a levy on actual return for the rechtsherstel but a recalculation with the actual composition of the assets in three categories.
Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36203-3.html https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/themas/werk/inkomstenbelasting/box-3/tijdlijn-rechtsherstel-box-3

#### Wet rechtsherstel box 3 (Verified, entry date Corrected)
Law of 21 December 2022, Stb. 2022, 533, issued 27 December 2022, Kamerstuk 36 203. Art. 1 lid 2: applies to IB/PVV assessments for 2017, 2018, 2019 or 2020 not yet irrevocable on 24 December 2021, and to the assessments for 2021 and 2022. Art. 1 lid 3: applies only where it leads to a lower voordeel. Art. 2: voordeel set at the outcome of art. 3, not below nil. Art. 3: voordeel = effective rendementspercentage x grondslag sparen en beleggen, effective percentage = rendement / rendementsgrondslag, rendement = column I x banktegoeden plus column II x overige bezittingen minus column III x schulden at 1 January. Art. 3 lid 4: whole-year partners use the joint figures. Art. 4: the 2022 percentages of columns I and III are replaced within two months after 2022 by ministerial regulation on DNB data for January to November 2022. Art. 5: extra persoonsgebonden aftrek follows the partners' chosen division of specific care costs or gifts, with an ambtshalve-vermindering request for another split. Art. 6: the double-taxation relief is recomputed. Art. 7: in force the day after the issue date of the Staatsblad, with retroactive effect to 1 January 2017, so in force 28 December 2022. The Rijksoverheid timeline says the law "trad op 1 januari 2023 in werking", which the statute does not support. Mass objectors get rechtsherstel for 2017 to 2020 (MvT).
Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2022-533.html https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36203-3.html https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/themas/werk/inkomstenbelasting/box-3/tijdlijn-rechtsherstel-box-3

#### Overbruggingswet box 3 (Verified)
Law of 21 December 2022, Stb. 2022, 534, in force 1 January 2023. New art. 5.2: voordeel = effective rendementspercentage x grondslag sparen en beleggen, a negative rendement is set at nil. Art. 4.14 lid 1 is amended so the forfaitaire voordeel is at least nil. New art. 5.24 (peildatumarbitrage): transactions around 1 January within "een aaneengesloten periode van drie maanden die aanvangt voor en eindigt na die peildatum" are disregarded unless the taxpayer makes plausible that "zakelijke overwegingen" lay behind them. New art. 10.6ter for the yearly percentages. The law as published printed 0,01% / 5,69% / 2,46% in art. 5.2 lid 2. These were replaced for 2023 by the figures in the 2023 row.
Source: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2022-534.html

#### How the category percentages are fixed, art. 10.6ter (Corrected)
Bank percentage (lid 2): after the year, with retroactive effect to 1 January, set at "de door twaalf te delen som" of the DNB average monthly return on household deposits with a notice period of up to three months over the eleven months from 1 January to 30 November, with November counted twice. So it is an average (sum divided by twelve), not a twelve-month sum as the research wrote. Debt percentage (lid 4): same method on the DNB average monthly interest rate on the total outstanding household home mortgages. Other-assets percentage (lid 3): replaced at the start of the year by ministerial regulation, set at 53% of the long-term return on real estate plus 33% on shares plus 14% on bonds. The statute text in force therefore carries last year's bank and debt figures (2025 text 0,92% / 2,46%, 2026 text 1,44% / 2,61%, 2027 text 1,37% / 2,70%) until the regulation replaces them.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 10.6ter) https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2025-8295.html https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2026-3708.html

#### Category percentage series 2017 to 2025 (Verified)
Spaargeld / beleggingen en andere bezittingen / schulden: 2017 0,25 / 5,39 / 3,43, 2018 0,12 / 5,38 / 3,20, 2019 0,08 / 5,59 / 3,00, 2020 0,04 / 5,28 / 2,74, 2021 0,01 / 5,69 / 2,46, 2022 0,00 / 5,53 / 2,28, 2023 0,92 / 6,17 / 2,46, 2024 1,44 / 6,04 / 2,61, 2025 1,37 / 5,88 / 2,70 (all %). The page lists no 2026 row. 2026 is on the 2026 calculation page.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/met-welke-percentages-is-het-fictief-rendement-berekend https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2022-533.html

#### Six-step category calculation (Verified)
Step 1 belastbaar rendement = banktegoeden x bank % plus overige bezittingen x other % minus (schulden minus drempel) x debt %. Step 2 rendementsgrondslag = bezittingen minus (schulden minus drempel). Step 3 grondslag sparen en beleggen = rendementsgrondslag minus heffingsvrij vermogen, partners may divide it in any way that totals 100%. Step 4 aandeel = grondslag / rendementsgrondslag x 100. Step 5 voordeel = belastbaar rendement x aandeel. Step 6 tax = rate x voordeel. The rounding instruction for step 4 differs per year: 2 decimals on the 2023 and 2025 pages, 3 decimals on the 2024 and 2026 pages (see the 2026 open point).
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/berekening-box-3-inkomen-2023 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/berekening-box-3-inkomen-2024 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/berekening-box-3-inkomen-2025 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/berekening-box-3-inkomen-2026

#### Partner rule (Verified)
The heffingsvrij vermogen doubles with a fiscal partner for the whole year, or for part of the year when the partners choose whole-year partnership. Art. 5.2 lid 5 uses the share of the joint grondslag allocated under art. 2.17. The 2027 text of lid 5 adds that the effective percentage uses the joint rendementsgrondslag and the joint bank, other-asset and debt values. The schuldendrempel doubles for whole-year partners and a joint drempel needs both partners' signatures on the return(s).
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/heffingsvrij-vermogen https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/vermogen/wat_zijn_uw_bezittingen_en_schulden/uw_schulden/drempel

#### Schuldendrempel (Verified)
Only the part of total debts above the drempel is deductible, and the Belastingdienst computes it in the return. Statute: art. 5.3 lid 3 ("daarvan meer bedraagt dan € ..."). The amount is indexed under art. 10.1.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/vermogen/wat_zijn_uw_bezittingen_en_schulden/uw_schulden/drempel https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01

#### Groene beleggingen (Corrected for 2027)
Art. 5.13: green investments are not counted as bezittingen up to the stated amount, doubled for whole-year partners. Art. 5.13 lid 2 (from the 2023 text): the exemption applies first to green overige bezittingen and only the remaining room to green banktegoeden. The exemption never exceeds the value of the green investments on 1 January. A minor child must file its own return to claim it and the parents report the rest as a normal asset. Art. 8.19: korting voor groene beleggingen of 0,7% (2020 to 2024) and 0,1% (2025 to 2027) of the exempt amount on the peildatum, computed automatically, split between whole-year partners in the same ratio as the green balance. Belastingplan 2026 (Stb. 2025, 444) set the 2027 amount at € 200 (€ 400 for partners), excluded it from the 2027 indexation (art. XLV), and moved the repeal of afdeling 5.3 and art. 8.19 to 1 January 2028. The research said both facilities lapse completely on 1 January 2027. That is what the Rijksoverheid page says, but the enacted 2027 text and Stb. 2025, 444 say otherwise.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2028-01-01 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2025-444.html https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01/0/Hoofdstuk5/Afdeling5.3/Artikel5.13/informatie https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/vermogen/beleggen_met_belastingvoordeel/groene_beleggingen https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/korting_voor_groene_beleggingen

#### Wet tegenbewijsregeling box 3 (Verified, two details Corrected)
Law of 14 July 2025, Stb. 2025, 195. Entry by the besluit of 14 July 2025 (Stb. 2025, 196), issued 18 July 2025, in force the day after issue, so 19 July 2025. The Eerste Kamer adopted it on 8 July 2025 (Rijksoverheid timeline). Art. V: arts. I and III work back to 1 January 2023, art. IV to 1 January 2017. Art. II (own use of real estate, art. 5.27a) applies from 1 January 2026. Art. 5.25 lid 1: if the taxpayer makes plausible that the actual return is lower than the voordeel, the income is the actual return minus the persoonsgebonden aftrek. Lid 2: the actual return is at least nil. Lid 4 and 5: whole-year partners use the joint actual return (the research placed this in art. 5.26, which is Corrected). Lid 6: a form provided by the inspecteur, in deviation from Awb arts. 4:1, 4:2 lid 1, 6:4 lid 1 and 6:5. Lid 7: further documents up to six weeks after the form is sent. Lid 8: lid 6 and 7 do not apply when the data were asked in the invitation to file. Art. 5.26: actual return is all benefits from assets and debts, regular benefits plus vermogensaanwas. Art. III: persoonsgebonden aftrek after a recalculation for 2023 or 2024 follows the partners' chosen split, other split by ambtshalve-vermindering request. Art. IV: art. 6a in the Wet rechtsherstel with the same form and six-week rule. The nota van toelichting of Stb. 2025, 196 names HR 6 June 2024 (ECLI:NL:HR:2024:704 and 705), 14 June 2024 (ECLI:NL:HR:2024:857), 2 August 2024 (ECLI:NL:HR:2024:1086 and 1084) and 20 December 2024 (ECLI:NL:HR:2024:1788).
Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2025-195.html https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2025-196.html https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/themas/werk/inkomstenbelasting/box-3/tijdlijn-rechtsherstel-box-3

#### Own use of real estate, art. 5.27a, from 2026 (Verified, source Corrected)
The statute sets the benefit at the economic rent value per year and excludes days the property is let or leased, under construction or unusable. The fixed method of 5,06% of the WOZ value (peildatum 1 January of the previous year) times the usable days is a Belastingdienst calculation method on its werkelijk-rendement page. It is not in the text of Stb. 2025, 195, which the research cited for it.
Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2025-195.html https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/wat-is-mijn-werkelijk-rendement

#### Content rules for the actual return (Verified)
Income plus value changes over the calendar year, changes during the year count. Gains and losses in one year are netted, a negative total is € 0 and cannot be carried to another year. No heffingsvrij vermogen ("De Hoge Raad heeft bepaald dat u daarbij geen rekening mag houden met het heffingsvrij vermogen"). Costs are not deductible, except an investment in a second home that raised the WOZ value and was reported to the municipality, and interest paid on a box 3 debt. Reporting is optional and the Belastingdienst uses the most favourable amount. You never pay more than the fictitious calculation.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/wat-is-mijn-werkelijk-rendement https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2025/Box_3_Werkelijk_rendement https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/heffingsvrij-vermogen https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/werkelijk-rendement-belastingaangifte

#### Reporting route per year (Verified)
2017 to 2019: form directly with DigiD, outside Mijn Belastingdienst. 2020 to 2024: online form in Mijn Belastingdienst ("Mijn werkelijk rendement box 3 opgeven"). Paper form via 0800 - 023 01 07 (one form per call), several forms via the BelastingTelefoon 0800 - 0543. 2021 to 2024: may always be filed without a letter. 2017 to 2020: all letters have been sent, without a letter you may not report. From 2025: in the income tax return. No deadline is stated on these pages.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/formulier-opgaaf-werkelijk-rendement https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/zonder-brief-formulier-opgaaf-werkelijk-rendement-invullen https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/werkelijk-rendement-belastingaangifte

#### Hoge Raad 6 June 2024 (Verified)
The Wet rechtsherstel and the Overbruggingswet still conflict with the EVRM when the forfaitaire return is higher than the actual return. The 2024 calculation page says a letter follows if the ruling affects your box 3 income.
Sources: https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/themas/werk/inkomstenbelasting/box-3/tijdlijn-rechtsherstel-box-3 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/berekening-box-3-inkomen-2024

#### Hoge Raad 25 June 2026 on non-objectors (Verified, was UNVERIFIED)
Taxpayers who did not object for 2017 to 2020 "hebben geen recht op herstel van hun betaalde box 3-belasting over deze periode". The Rijksoverheid timeline now confirms this. The "or objected late" part of the research is not on that page and stays UNVERIFIED. hogeraad.nl still returns HTTP 403.
Source: https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/themas/werk/inkomstenbelasting/box-3/tijdlijn-rechtsherstel-box-3

#### Wet werkelijk rendement box 3, bill 36 748 (Verified)
Submitted 16 May 2025. Adopted by the Tweede Kamer 12 February 2026 (for: SP, GroenLinks-PvdA, D66, Volt, PvdD, CDA, VVD. Against: 50PLUS, DENK, SGP, ChristenUnie, JA21, BBB, Groep Markuszower, PVV, FVD). Eerste Kamer: mondeling overleg 17 March 2026, verslag 7 April 2026, nota naar aanleiding van het verslag 24 April 2026, expert meetings 19 May 2026, second verslag 29 May 2026, reply 12 June 2026, letter 19 June 2026 on improvements and the vermogenswinst route. Plenary 30 June 2026 with motions Van Rooijen (L), Van den Oetelaar c.s. (K), Kroon c.s. (J) and Schalk c.s. (M). On 30 June 2026 member Crone made an ordevoorstel (not a motion, so the research term motie-Crone is Corrected) to postpone the vote until the plenary treatment of the announced novelle(s). Vote on motion Schalk 7 July 2026. Letters of 14 September 2026 (EK Q) and 30 September 2026 (EK 37.020 / 36.748, B). Committee mondeling overleg 5 October 2026. No Eerste Kamer vote and no Staatsblad on 2026-10-11. Entry into force by royal decree, with a rule referring to 1 January 2028.
Source: https://www.eerstekamer.nl/wetsvoorstel/36748_wet_werkelijk_rendement_box

#### Cabinet letter of 29 September 2026 (Verified, Kamerstuk number UNVERIFIED)
A novelle to bill 36 748 extends the vermogenswinstbelasting to all financial instruments from 2028 (about 90% of assets with value development), with a full switch for the other assets in 2030. Box 3 heffingsvrij vermogen back to the 2020 level of € 30.846 from 2027. A heffingsvrij resultaat of € 1.000 from 2028. The forfait for overige bezittingen, including rent and own-use benefits, up by 1,5 percentage point in 2027. The novelle must be adopted by the Eerste Kamer before 31 December, and the Cabinet intends to ask the Raad van State for urgent advice. The Eerste Kamer dossier lists the letter under 37.020. The number "nr. 62" and a Raad van State request on 2 October 2026 were not found on a fetched page.
Sources: https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D47255 https://www.eerstekamer.nl/wetsvoorstel/36748_wet_werkelijk_rendement_box

#### Box 2 rate structure (art. 2.12 Wet IB 2001)
One flat rate in 2020 to 2023: 26,25% in 2020 and 26,9% in 2021, 2022 and 2023. From 2024 a two-bracket table: 24,5% up to the bracket boundary and a higher rate above it, 33% in 2024 and 31% from 2025. The table states the tax at the top of bracket 1 in column III. The table applies per taxpayer. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2021-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01

#### Origin of the two brackets and of the 33% and 31% rates
The second bracket was announced in Belastingplan 2023 and took effect on 1 January 2024 (rijksoverheid.nl: "Deze maatregel is in het Belastingplan 2023 aangekondigd", 24,5% over the first € 67.000 per person, 33% above). The 33% itself came from Belastingplan 2024: the amendement Van der Lee c.s. raised the second-bracket rate by 2 percentage points to 33% from 1 January 2024, and Belastingplan 2025 ("Terugdraaien verhoging box 2-tarief", Kamerstuk 36 602 nr. 3, section 5.2) lowered it by 2 points to 31% from 1 January 2025. Years 2024 to 2025. Verified (the 33% origin was missing from the research).
Sources: https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2023/12/20/belangrijkste-belastingwijzigingen-per-1-januari-2024 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36602-3.html

#### Indexation of the box 2 amounts (art. 10.1 lid 1)
Art. 10.1 lid 1 lists art. 2.12 among the amounts replaced at the start of each calendar year by ministerial regulation, multiplied by the tabelcorrectiefactor. Art. 4.14a (excessief lenen maximum) is not on that list, so the € 500.000 is not indexed. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Source: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01

#### Aanmerkelijk belang, the 5% test (art. 4.6)
The taxpayer has an aanmerkelijk belang if he, alone or together with his partner, directly or indirectly (a) holds at least 5% of the issued capital, (b) has rights to acquire shares up to at least 5% of the issued capital, (c) holds winstbewijzen for at least 5% of the annual profit or of the liquidation proceeds, or (d) is entitled to at least 5% of the votes in a coöperatie as meant in art. 4.5a. The text is not the same across the years, contrary to the research. Sub e was added between the 1 January 2021 and 1 January 2022 texts (a share in a body as meant in art. 2 lid 12 Wet Vpb 1969, 2022 numbering, with at least 5% of its profit). Sub f was added by Overige fiscale maatregelen 2026 (Stb. 2025, 445) with retroactive effect to 1 January 2025 (a share in a body as meant in art. 2 lid 1 onderdeel h or art. 3 lid 2 Wet Vpb 1969 with at least 5% of profit, liquidation proceeds or voting rights). In sub d "tot" became "om" in the same period. Years 2020 to 2027. Corrected (the research said the 2020 and 2027 texts read the same).
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2021-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2025-445.html

#### Soorten aandelen, omvang kapitaal, meesleep and meetrek (art. 4.7 to 4.10)
Art. 4.7, 4.8, 4.9 and 4.10 have identical text in every year 2020 to 2027 (checked year by year, not only at the endpoints). Meesleepregeling (art. 4.9): once there is an aanmerkelijk belang in a company, the taxpayer's other shares and winstbewijzen in that company also belong to it. Meetrekregeling (art. 4.10): shares in a company where the taxpayer has no aanmerkelijk belang but his partner or a blood or aanverwant in de rechte lijn of the taxpayer or partner does, also belong to an aanmerkelijk belang. The Belastingdienst page names parents, children and grandchildren of the taxpayer and the fiscal partner, plus their fiscal partners, and says a 5% holding in one class of shares also counts. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/wanneer_hebt_u_een_aanmerkelijk_belang/wanneer_hebt_u_een_aanmerkelijk_belang

#### Fictief aanmerkelijk belang (art. 4.11)
Shares or winstbewijzen that do not belong to an aanmerkelijk belang under the other articles are deemed to be one if art. 3.65, 4.17, 4.17a, 4.17b, 4.17c, 4.40 or 4.41 has been applied (rollover cases). Art. 4.40 is the route when a holding drops below 5%: on request the deemed sale of art. 4.16 lid 1 sub g is not applied to the shares the taxpayer keeps, and those shares then carry a fictief aanmerkelijk belang. Art. 4.16 lid 3 lets the taxpayer request a deemed sale of such shares. Text identical 2020 to 2027. Corrected (the research described art. 4.11 as the vbi route and said the list was not read. The vbi status is a fictieve vervreemding case listed by the Belastingdienst, not the art. 4.11 list).
Source: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01

#### Partner and minor children
The 5% test is met "al dan niet tezamen met zijn partner" (art. 4.6). The Belastingdienst states the taxpayer must also declare the income from aanmerkelijk belang of minor children, until the child turns 18. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/wanneer_hebt_u_een_aanmerkelijk_belang/wanneer_hebt_u_een_aanmerkelijk_belang

#### Fiscal partners may divide box 2 income (art. 2.17 lid 2)
Gemeenschappelijke inkomensbestanddelen of the taxpayer and partner are divided in the proportion they choose each year in the return. FISIN 2026: with fiscal partnership for the whole year the inkomsten uit aanmerkelijk belang may be divided, "Elke verdeling mag, als het totaal maar 100% is." Because art. 2.12 applies per taxpayer, each partner's own first bracket applies to his share. No primary page states a summed two-partner amount. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified (rule). The summed figure stays UNVERIFIED.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/aanmerkelijk_belang

#### Inkomen uit aanmerkelijk belang (art. 4.12) and reguliere voordelen (art. 4.13)
Income is reguliere voordelen minus deductible costs plus vervreemdingsvoordelen, minus persoonsgebonden aftrek (art. 4.12). Reguliere voordelen are dividends and other profit distributions (Belastingdienst). Art. 4.13 lid 1 adds as reguliere voordelen the forfaitair voordeel from a vrijgestelde beleggingsinstelling or a foreign investment company (sub a), certain repayments of paid-in capital above the verkrijgingsprijs or without the required decision and statutory reduction (sub b to d), bonus shares from a beleggingsinstelling (sub e), and from 2023 the excessief-lenen fiction (sub f). Interest on a receivable on the own company is not a box 2 voordeel but income from making assets available. Salary from the company is box 1 loon. Deductible: interest on a loan to buy the shares. Notary costs are added to the verkrijgingsprijs and selling costs reduce the overdrachtsprijs. Dividendbelasting is not deducted but credited as voorheffing. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/inkomsten_uit_aanmerkelijk_belang/inkomsten_uit_aanmerkelijk_belang https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/aanmerkelijk_belang

#### Forfaitair voordeel for vbi and foreign investment companies (art. 4.14)
The forfaitair voordeel of art. 4.13 lid 1 sub a is a yearly percentage of the value at the start of the year, reduced by other reguliere voordelen from those shares, never below nil, time-apportioned. The percentage is a yearly value, listed in each year block. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified (added, the research did not give the percentages).
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (art. 4.14 lid 1 in each version)

#### Vervreemdingsvoordeel, verkrijgingsprijs and onzakelijke prijs
The voordeel is the overdrachtsprijs minus the verkrijgingsprijs. The verkrijgingsprijs is the price paid or the value at acquisition, notary costs included. Special cases (inheritance, gift with or without doorschuiving, reaching 5% during the year, onzakelijke prijs) are as the Belastingdienst describes. At an onzakelijke prijs the value in het economisch verkeer applies to both sides. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/inkomsten_uit_aanmerkelijk_belang/inkomsten_uit_aanmerkelijk_belang https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/aanmerkelijk_belang

#### Fictieve vervreemding (art. 4.16)
The statute lists as a sale: inkoop of shares, afkoop and inkoop of winstbewijzen, liquidatie-uitkeringen, becoming a shareholder in another company by a transfer under general title, transfer under general title and by inheritance under special title, bringing the shares into an enterprise or a werkzaamheid, no longer having an aanmerkelijk belang, ceasing to be a resident other than by death (emigration), granting a koopoptie, shares of an afgezonderd particulier vermogen no longer attributed, and the shares starting to yield a forfaitair voordeel under art. 4.13 lid 1 sub a. A liquidation with continuation under art. 14c Wet Vpb counts as a liquidation payment. Text identical 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Source: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01

#### Doorschuiving and APV rules
The research lists rollover on request for gift, inheritance and emigration and the APV loss deferral (offset after a third-party sale, death or 10 years). FISIN 2020 confirms the 10-year APV term. The doorschuiving list was not re-fetched on a primary page in this check. Years 2020 to 2027. UNVERIFIED for the doorschuiving list, Verified for the 10-year APV term.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/aanmerkelijk_belang https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/aanmerkelijk_belang

#### Inherited aanmerkelijk belang facility (art. 4.12a)
If shares were inherited and box 2 income was taken into account with the deceased on that transfer, reguliere voordelen received within 24 months after the death are on request not counted as box 2 income, up to that amount, and are written off on the verkrijgingsprijs. This is what art. 4.12a carries. It does not carry the excessief-lenen fiction. Text identical 2020 to 2027. Verified. The Overige fiscale maatregelen 2027 bill proposes to exclude the fictief regulier voordeel from this facility from 1 January 2027 (proposed, see 2027).
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://www.eerstekamer.nl/behandeling/20260915/memorie_van_toelichting_56/document3/f=/vn15lhsbrhzz.pdf

#### Loss offset (art. 4.49) and the older-loss rule
A verlies uit aanmerkelijk belang is offset against box 2 income of the previous year and the six following years, in the order the losses arose. The loss is set by beschikking together with the assessment, shown separately on it, and open to objection. For losses that arose in 2018 or earlier: "langer te verrekenen ... in de komende 9 jaar". Art. 4.49 text identical 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/verlies_uit_aanmerkelijk_belang/verlies_uit_aanmerkelijk_belang_1

#### Conversion of an unoffset loss into a belastingkorting (art. 4.53)
Lid 1: if the taxpayer and partner had no aanmerkelijk belang in the calendar year and the year before, the unoffset loss is converted on request. A taxpayer without a partner who died in the year only needs to have had no aanmerkelijk belang at death. Lid 2: 26,25% in the 2020 text, 26,9% in the 2021 to 2023 texts, and from the 2024 text "het in de eerste regel van de vierde kolom van de tabel in artikel 2.12 opgenomen percentage", so 24,5% in 2024 to 2027. Lid 3: the inspecteur sets the korting by beschikking open to objection. Lid 4: the korting reduces box 1 tax of that year and the seven following years, at the latest the ninth year after the loss year. Lid 5: on the death of a partner part of the korting can pass to the surviving partner. The Belastingdienst adds that the request is possible only after the loss is definitively established and from the second year after the year without aanmerkelijk belang. Kennisgroep standpunt KG:003:2023:8 (published 26-09-2023, updated 22-07-2026) tests the condition for the tax year, not at the request date, so a new aanmerkelijk belang at the request does not block it, and ambtshalve vermindering of earlier assessments is possible unless five years have passed after the year of the assessment. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified. The FISIN 2025 and 2026 statement "De korting is 26,9%" is overruled by the statute (see Resolved conflicts).
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2021-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/verlies_uit_aanmerkelijk_belang/verlies_omzetten_in_belastingkorting https://kennisgroepen.belastingdienst.nl/publicaties/kg00320238-omzetting-verlies-uit-aanmerkelijk-belang-in-een-belastingkorting/

#### Wet excessief lenen bij eigen vennootschap, statute and placement
Wet van 21 december 2022, Staatsblad 2022, 531 (issued 27 December 2022), in force 1 January 2023 (Artikel III), cited as "Wet excessief lenen bij eigen vennootschap". The fiction sits in art. 4.13 lid 1 sub f ("het bovenmatige deel van schulden ... (het fictief reguliere voordeel)", debts meaning all civil-law debt relations and obligations), the amount rule in art. 4.14a ("Fictief regulier voordeel"), the verbonden-person rule in art. 4.14b and the immigration rule in art. 4.14c. Years 2023 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2022-531.html https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01

#### Excessief lenen, maximum and mechanism (art. 4.14a)
Lid 1: positive amount equals total debts above the maximum. Negative amount equals the shortfall below the maximum, capped at amounts taxed earlier (or under a comparable foreign rule) and not yet reversed. Lid 2: maximum € 700.000 in the 2023 text and € 500.000 from the 2024 text, plus amounts taxed earlier. Lid 3: debts of the taxpayer, the partner or both count once for the two together and the maximum applies to them jointly. Lid 4: measured at the end of the calendar year at nominal value. Lid 5 (missing in the research): if the taxpayer no longer has an aanmerkelijk belang at year end, other than by emigration under art. 4.16 lid 1 sub h, lid 1 is applied as if he still had one and the debts were nil, which releases a negative amount. Lid 6: an eigenwoningschuld is excluded to the extent a recht van hypotheek on the home was given to the company. Art. 10a.23 (transitional, missing in the research as an article): an eigenwoningschuld existing on 31 December 2022 without such a mortgage is also excluded. Lid 7 (Belastingplan 2025, Stb. 2024, 434, retroactive to 1 January 2023): double counting of a samenwerkingsverband debt among several participants is removed. Belastingdienst: receivables on the company do not reduce the debts, debts at several companies are added, the drempel "never drops below € 500.000 (€ 700.000 in 2023)". Years 2023 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2022-531.html https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2024-434.html https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/werk_en_inkomen/bijzondere_situaties/geld_lenen_van_uw_bv/excessief-lenen-van-bv-beperkt

#### Excessief lenen, threshold history
The bill proposed € 500.000. The derde nota van wijziging (Kamerstuk 35 496 nr. 15) replaced € 500.000 by € 700.000 in art. 4.14a lid 2, 4.14b and 4.14c lid 1, following the coalition agreement. Amendement Grinwis c.s. (Kamerstuk 36 418 nr. 60), included in the adopted text (nr. A), replaced € 700.000 by € 500.000 in the same articles. The 2024, 2025, 2026 and 2027 texts print € 500.000 in art. 4.14a, twice in art. 4.14b and in art. 4.14c. Years 2023 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-35496-15.html https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36418-60.html https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36418-A.html https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01

#### Excessief lenen, samenwerkingsverband exception (art. 4.13 lid 4 and 5)
Debts arising from a civil-law joint liability of the taxpayer or a verbonden person for a debt of a samenwerkingsverband (vof, cv, maatschap or a comparable foreign form) from which they derive business profit are left out. Inserted by Belastingplan 2025 (Stb. 2024, 434) retroactive to 1 January 2023 as lid 6 and 7 (numbered lid 4 and 5 for 2023). Overige fiscale maatregelen 2026 (Stb. 2025, 445) repaired the internal reference «zesde lid» to «vierde lid» retroactive to 1 January 2023, not for 2024. This is the technical repair the research names. Years 2023 to 2027. Verified (added).
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2025-445.html

#### Excessief lenen, verbonden personen and immigration (art. 4.14b, 4.14c)
Debts of a verbonden person (blood or aanverwant in de rechte lijn of the taxpayer or partner) to a company where the taxpayer has an aanmerkelijk belang and that person has none count with the taxpayer for the part above € 700.000 (2023) or € 500.000 (from 2024), applied to that person and his partner together. Debts already counted under art. 4.13 lid 1 sub f for the taxpayer and partner, and debts on which a regular box 2 benefit was taken, are excluded. If several taxpayers count the same debts, they share them equally. A taxpayer who comes to live in the Netherlands gets a maximum equal to his debts at that moment, at least € 700.000 (2023 text) or € 500.000 (from 2024). Years 2023 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01

#### Excessief lenen, consequences
The loan stays a loan in full, the company keeps charging interest on the whole loan and pays Vpb on it, the other terms stay. The company does not withhold dividendbelasting on the part taxed as box 2 income. Existing arrangements on company debts can lapse when the total exceeds the threshold. A negative fictief regulier voordeel is offset against box 2 income of that year, the previous year or the following six years. Years 2023 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/werk_en_inkomen/bijzondere_situaties/geld_lenen_van_uw_bv/excessief-lenen-van-bv-beperkt https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/aanmerkelijk_belang

#### Gebruikelijk loon (art. 12a Wet LB 1964)
For an employee working for a body in which he or his partner has an aanmerkelijk belang, the loon is at least the highest of (a) the loon from the most comparable employment, 75% of it in the 2020 to 2022 texts and 100% from the 2023 text, (b) the highest loon of the employees of the body or connected bodies, (c) a fixed amount. Lower loon: if the inhoudingsplichtige makes plausible that the highest amount exceeds the (75% of the) comparable loon, the comparable loon applies, in 2020 to 2022 with the fixed amount as a floor unless the comparable loon itself is lower. The comparable employment is one in which an aanmerkelijk belang plays no role and is known to the inhoudingsplichtige and the inspecteur. € 5.000 rule: the rule does not apply if the loon for the work for the body and connected bodies is not more than € 5.000 in the year (lid 6 in the 2020 to 2022 texts, lid 4 from 2023). The Belastingdienst adds that the € 5.000 applies to the total of the work for all companies, "niet per onderneming". The fixed amount is indexed at the start of each year by ministerial regulation with the tabelcorrectiefactor (lid 9 in 2020, lid 7 from 2023). Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2020-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2023-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/loon_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/loon_en_aanmerkelijk_belang

#### Gebruikelijk loon, start-up rule (art. 12a lid 3 and 4, 2020 to 2022)
For a body with an S&O-verklaring to which the verhoogd starterspercentage applies, the loon was at least 108% of twelve times the monthly minimum wage of art. 8 lid 1 sub a Wet minimumloon as at 1 January, or the comparable loon if lower, subject to the de-minimis ceiling. The rule is absent from the 2023 text. The Belastingdienst: "Vanaf 2023 is deze uitzondering voor nieuwe gevallen vervallen", it could be used at most 3 years, so a first use in 2021 ran to 2023 and a first use in 2022 to 2024. Years 2020 to 2024. Corrected (the research said "the minimum wage", the statute says 108% of twelve times the monthly minimum wage).
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2020-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2022-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2023-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/loon_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/loon_en_aanmerkelijk_belang

#### Gebruikelijk loon and box 2 interaction
The gebruikelijk loon is declared as income from employment (box 1), not as a box 2 voordeel. The rule also applies when the fiscal partner or a minor child is the aanmerkelijkbelanghouder and the taxpayer makes assets available to that company. The research's statement that the loon reduces the residual profit available for a dividend is reasoning, not a sourced statement. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified (the first two sentences).
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/aanmerkelijk_belang https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/inkomsten_uit_aanmerkelijk_belang/inkomsten_uit_aanmerkelijk_belang

#### End of pensioen in eigen beheer (PEB)
Wet uitfasering pensioen in eigen beheer en overige fiscale pensioenmaatregelen, Stb. 2017, 115 (given 8 March 2017, issued 27 March 2017). Art. 38n lid 1 keeps the old pension rules (as at 31 December 2016) for a PEB. Art. 38n lid 2 allowed prijsgeven of the part of the claim above the fiscale balanswaarde, on condition the claim was at the same moment wholly afgekocht or geruisloos converted into an oudedagsverplichting. Lid 4 required the written consent of the partner, also a former partner who kept part of the claim. Art. 38o: on afkoop the taxable loon is the afkoopwaarde minus 34,5% of the fiscale balanswaarde (capped by reference to the 2015 balance), 25% from 1 January 2018 (Artikel IV) and 19,5% from 1 January 2019 (Artikel V). Art. 38q blocked these routes for 2016 accrual above 125% of the 2015 accrual. Artikel VI: from 1 January 2020 art. 38n lid 2 to 5, art. 38o, art. 38p lid 5 and art. 38q lapse, and the 2020 Wet LB text shows art. 38o and 38q as "[Vervallen per 01-01-2020]". The Centraal Aanspreekpunt Pensioenen: afkoop or conversion was possible "vanaf 1 april 2017 tot en met 31 december 2019", and V&A 17-007 is marked vervallen. Years 2017 to 2020. Verified.
Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2017-115.html https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2020-01-01 https://centraalaanspreekpuntpensioenen.belastingdienst.nl/publicaties/va_17-007_v170401/

#### PEB start date
Stb. 2017, 115 Artikel XIX as published reads "treedt deze wet in werking met ingang van 1 januari 2017" under art. 12 Wet raadgevend referendum. The consolidated text on wetten.overheid.nl (BWBR0039380) reads in force on the first day of the first calendar month after issue of the Staatsblad, shown as "Geldend van 01-04-2017", with listed parts retroactive to 1 January 2017. The Centraal Aanspreekpunt Pensioenen gives 1 April 2017. The coulance decision of 22 March 2017 (Stcrt. 2017, 18118) let dga's adjust "uiterlijk tot en met 30 juni 2017" and lapsed on 1 July 2017, which explains the Belastingdienst phrase "Sinds 1 juli 2017 kunt u niet langer pensioen in eigen beheer opbouwen". Year 2017. Verified, see Resolved conflicts.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0039380/2017-04-01 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2017-18118.html https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/uitfaseren-pensioen-in-eigen-beheer/

#### Remaining PEB after 2020
A remaining PEB keeps the old rules (art. 38n). If the scheme provided for indexation the company must index the accrued rights every year and must value the provision every year actuarially. "Als de bv niet jaarlijks indexeert, dan kunnen wij dit zien als een vorm van prijsgeven van de rechten", with possible fiscal consequences. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/uitfaseren-pensioen-in-eigen-beheer/

#### Oudedagsverplichting (art. 38p Wet LB 1964)
The ODV is increased each year by a market rate set by ministerial regulation (art. 12.3a Uitvoeringsregeling loonbelasting 2011, the average of the monthly U-rendementen of the preceding year). It can be used geruisloos for a lijfrente, lijfrenterekening or lijfrentebeleggingsrecht, or is paid in instalments over twenty years as loon uit vroegere dienstbetrekking, each instalment being the balance at the start of the year divided by the remaining years. The first instalment comes no earlier than five years before AOW age and at the latest two months after it. An earlier start lengthens the twenty years. On death before the start the instalments begin within twelve months for the natural-person heirs. Added (Stb. 2022, 532, retroactive to 1 January 2020): under ministerial conditions the ODV can also be converted into a lijfrente after the instalments have started (art. 38p lid 5). A negative market rate must also be applied. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2020-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2026-01-01 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2025-42873.html https://centraalaanspreekpuntpensioenen.belastingdienst.nl/publicaties/overzicht-u-rendementen-en-marktrenten/

#### PEB and box 2 (art. 10a.18 Wet IB 2001)
Art. 10a.18 lid 3: the verkrijgingsprijs of the shares is not increased by the 2017, 2018 or 2019 application of art. 38n lid 2. Art. 10a.18 lid 2: in the company the ODV is valued with the market rate of art. 38p. PEB payouts are loon uit vroegere dienstbetrekking (box 1). Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2017-115.html

#### Non-residents
The research states a non-resident declares only an aanmerkelijk belang in a Netherlands company and that a 2025 beschikking partiële buitenlandse belastingplicht has the same effect. Art. 7.5 confirms the base for a non-resident (aanmerkelijk belang in a Netherlands-established company, not part of an enterprise). The 30%-rule beschikking statement was not found on the fetched Belastingdienst pages. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified for art. 7.5, UNVERIFIED for the beschikking statement.
Source: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01

#### Vpb rate table and how the tax is computed (art. 22 Wet Vpb 1969)
The tax is the column III amount plus the column IV percentage of the part of the belastbaar bedrag above the column I amount. Series read in each dated text: 2020 t/m € 200.000 16,5%, above that € 33.000 plus 25%. 2021 t/m € 245.000 15%, above that € 36.750 plus 25%. 2022 t/m € 395.000 15%, above that € 59.250 plus 25,8%. 2023 to 2026 t/m € 200.000 19%, above that € 38.000 plus 25,8%. The Belastingdienst tarieven page states 19,0% and 25,8% for 2023, 2024, 2025 and 2026. Years 2020 to 2026. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2021-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2022-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2023-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2024-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2025-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/tarieven_vennootschapsbelasting

#### Book year that straddles a change of art. 22 (art. 31 Wet Vpb 1969)
If a year starts before and ends on or after the date a change of art. 22 takes effect, the tax is (X/B x To) + (Y/B x Tn). X is the days before the change, Y the days from the change, B all days of the year, To the tax under the old art. 22 and Tn the tax under the new art. 22. The research named no article. The formula sits in art. 31 in both the 2020 and 2026 texts. Years 2020 to 2026. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2026-01-01

#### 2027 status of the Wet Vpb 1969
https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2027-01-01 returns HTTP 404 on 2026-10-11. The 2026-01-01 version reads "Geldend van 01-01-2026 t/m heden" and announces "Toekomstige wijziging(en) voorzien met ingang van: 01-01-2029". Art. 22 carries the note that changes without an entry date exist. Belastingplan 2027 (bill 37 022) changes art. 12bd, art. 13 lid 7 and adds art. 34j in Article XIX, and deletes ", 3.11" from art. 8 lid 1 from 1 January 2029 in Article XX. It does not touch art. 22, art. 15 or art. 15b. Year 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2026-01-01, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-37022-2.html

#### Verliesverrekening (art. 20 Wet Vpb 1969)
2020 and 2021: a loss is set off against the profit of the preceding year and the six following years, after the inspector fixes it by beschikking. From 2022: one year back and unlimited forward, but per year only up to € 1.000.000 plus 50% of the profit above € 1.000.000. Set-off follows the order in which losses arose. The research did not cover this. Years 2020 to 2026. Verified (added).
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2021-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2022-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2026-01-01

#### Generieke renteaftrekbeperking (art. 15b lid 1 Wet Vpb 1969)
The saldo aan renten is not deductible in so far as it exceeds the higher of a percentage of the gecorrigeerde winst and € 1.000.000. The saldo is interest costs on loans minus interest income. Percentage per dated text: 30% in 2020 and 2021, 20% in 2022, 2023 and 2024, 24,5% in 2025 and 2026. The Belastingdienst change pages state 20% for 2024, 24,5% for 2025 and 24,5% for 2026, and say non-deductible interest carries forward. Years 2020 to 2026. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2026-01-01 (each year), https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-vorige-jaren/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-2025, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-2026

#### Filing the Vpb return and extension
Book year equal to the calendar year: file before 1 June of the next year. Broken book year: within 5 months after its end. Short book year ending 31 December: before 1 June, ending in another month: before 1 April of the next calendar year. Online extension (only for a calendar book year) runs to 1 November and is requested from 1 March to 1 June. A paper request is needed for a broken book year or for more than 5 months and must be filed within 5 months after the book year ends, with reasons. Current pages, read for the 2025 and 2026 filing seasons. Verified (current page, added).
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/aangifte-vennootschapsbelasting-doen/aangifte-vennootschapsbelasting-doen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/uitstel_aangifte_vennootschapsbelasting/uitstel_aangifte_vennootschapsbelasting

#### Gebruikelijkloonregeling (art. 12a Wet LB 1964)
For an employee who works for a body in which he or his partner holds an aanmerkelijk belang, the wage is at least the highest of (a) the loon uit de meest vergelijkbare dienstbetrekking (75% of it in 2020 to 2022, 100% from 2023), (b) the highest wage of the employees of the body or of connected bodies, (c) the normbedrag. Normbedrag per dated text: € 46.000 (2020), € 47.000 (2021), € 48.000 (2022), € 51.000 (2023), € 56.000 (2024), € 56.000 (2025), € 58.000 (2026), € 58.000 in the future text for 2027. The rule does not apply when the wage for all work in the body and connected bodies is not more than € 5.000 (lid 6 in the 2020 text, present in every year). The normbedrag is replaced at the start of each calendar year by ministerial regulation, multiplied by the tabelcorrectiefactor of art. 10.2 Wet IB 2001 (lid 9 in 2020 to 2022, lid 7 from 2023). The Handboek Loonheffingen 2025 adds that the test wage is the loon for loonbelasting and premie volksverzekeringen including the car bijtelling, that a connected company is one with a 1/3 interest either way or through a common third party, and that the rule applies the same way to the partner and to working relatives in the direct line who hold shares. Years 2020 to 2027. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2026-01-01 (each year), https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2027-01-01, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek-loonheffingen-lh0221t51d.pdf, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/loon_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/loon_en_aanmerkelijk_belang

#### Gebruikelijk loon escape (art. 12a lid 2)
2020 to 2022: if the inhoudingsplichtige makes plausible that the highest amount exceeds 75% of the comparable wage, the wage is 75% of the comparable wage, but at least the normbedrag, or the comparable wage itself when that is lower than the normbedrag. From 2023: if the highest amount exceeds the comparable wage, the wage is the comparable wage. The Handboek 2025 notes a vaststellingsovereenkomst made before 2023 on the old 75% basis stays valid for its term, with the 100% wage. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2023-01-01, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek-loonheffingen-lh0221t51d.pdf

#### Start-up relief and lower wages for starters and loss makers
Statute 2020 to 2022 (art. 12a lid 3 and 4): for a body with an S&O-verklaring for R&D to which the starterspercentage of art. 23 lid 7 WVA applies, the wage is at least 108% of twelve times the art. 8 lid 1 sub a WML amount, or the lower comparable wage if made plausible, within the de-minimis ceiling of Verordening (EU) nr. 1407/2013. Belastingdienst: the de-minimis condition did not apply to a DGA, and an S&O-verklaring for part of a year was enough for the whole year. The exception lapsed for new cases from 2023. A first use for 2021 or 2022 could run at most 3 years (conditions still met in 2023 for a 2021 start and in 2024 for a 2022 start). Handboek 2025: the transitional right lapsed for all starters from 2025. Separate policy (Handboek 2025): a starting company may set a lower wage for at most 3 years when it cannot pay the usual wage, starting from the WML for the hours worked, and the 3 years are cut by the period the business ran as an eenmanszaak. A loss-making company may set a lower wage only when continuity is at risk. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2020-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/vermogen_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/aanmerkelijk_belang/loon_en_aanmerkelijk_belang/loon_en_aanmerkelijk_belang, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek-loonheffingen-lh0221t51d.pdf

#### DGA salary, loonheffing, werknemersverzekeringen and Zvw
A DGA is in dienst of his own company for loonbelasting, premie volksverzekeringen and Zvw, so the BV runs the wage through its loonadministratie and withholds loonheffing. A DGA who (alone or with spouse and relatives up to the third degree holding at least 2/3 of the votes, or through an entity) can decide on his own dismissal, or who holds an equal share with all fellow directors, is not insured for the werknemersverzekeringen. For him the company pays no premies werknemersverzekeringen and no werkgeversheffing Zvw, and instead withholds the inkomensafhankelijke bijdrage Zvw from his net wage up to the maximum bijdrageloon and pays it through the aangifte loonheffingen (Handboek 2025: 5,26% up to € 75.864). A DGA who is insured falls under the werkgeversheffing Zvw paid by the BV. Corrected: the research said the uninsured DGA pays the bijdrage himself by aanslag. The Handboek says the BV withholds it on net wage. The Belastingdienst table only says the DGA pays the bijdrage himself, collected by inhouding op nettoloon or by aanslag. Checked for 2025 in the Handboek and for 2024 to 2026 in the table.
Sources: https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek-loonheffingen-lh0221t51d.pdf (paragraphs 8.2.2 and 18.1.1), https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/werk_en_inkomen/zorgverzekeringswet/bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet/hoe_wordt_de_inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zvw_betaald/tabel_werkgeversheffing_zvw_of_bijdrage_zvw/ik_ben_directeur_grootaandeelhouder/

#### Zvw percentages and maximum per year
Bijdrage, werkgeversheffing and maximum bijdrage-inkomen per FISIN year page: 2020 5,45%, 6,70%, € 57.232. 2021 5,75%, 7,00%, € 58.311. 2022 5,50%, 6,75%, € 59.706. 2023 5,43%, 6,68%, € 66.956. 2024 5,32%, 6,57%, € 71.628. 2025 5,26%, 6,51%, € 75.864. 2026 4,85%, 6,10%, € 79.409. Each FISIN page says DGA loon falls under the werkgeversheffing only if he is insured for the werknemersverzekeringen. The research had no werkgeversheffing for 2020 to 2023. Verified.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet (each year), https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/werk_en_inkomen/zorgverzekeringswet/veranderingen-bijdrage-zvw/percentages-zvw

#### Dividendbelasting (Wet DB 1965)
Art. 5: the tax is 15% of the opbrengst, in every version read (2020 version geldend 2020 to 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, and the 2025 version geldend t/m heden). Art. 6 lid 1: if the company bears the tax, the opbrengst is multiplied by 100/85. Art. 1: the shareholder is the taxpayer, the company withholds. Art. 9 lid 1: once the opbrengst is paid, credited, settled or handed over, a dividendnota is due. Belastingdienst: a deelnemingsdividend (holding of 5% or more) to a Dutch shareholder is usually exempt from withholding under the deelnemingsvrijstelling, and no return is needed for a fully exempt payment unless invited. A beleggingsdividend to a Dutch shareholder carries 15% withholding. The return and payment are due within 1 month after the day the dividend is made available. A return is also needed when a kwalificatiebeschikking is applied. Individuals credit the withheld tax in the aangifte inkomstenbelasting, bodies in the aangifte Vpb. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002515/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002515/2022-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002515/2023-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002515/2024-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002515/2025-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/dividendbelasting/als_u_dividend_uitkeert/als_u_dividend_uitkeert, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/dividendbelasting/als_u_dividend_uitkeert/dividendbelasting-aangifte-betalen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/dividendbelasting/

#### Dividend stripping (uiteindelijk gerechtigde)
The rule sits in art. 4 lid 7 Wet DB 1965, not in art. 4 lid 3 as the research said. A person who made a counter-performance as part of a series of transactions is not the uiteindelijk gerechtigde when the opbrengst benefits someone with a lesser right to exemption or refund who keeps a position in the shares. The 2020 text holds it. From the 2024 text it reads "wordt in ieder geval niet als uiteindelijk gerechtigde beschouwd". Corrected.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002515/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002515/2023-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002515/2024-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002515/2025-01-01

#### Inkoop van eigen aandelen
Dividendbelasting is due on the difference between the buyback price and the average paid-up capital on those shares, also on an exchange buyback, where the company bears the tax and art. 6 grosses it up by 100/85 unless the art. 4c facility for listed companies applies. Verified.
Source: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2025-34752.html

#### Conditionele bronbelasting (Wet bronbelasting 2021)
Art. 4.1: the tax is the highest percentage of art. 22 Wet Vpb 1969. Interest and royalties are in scope in the 2021 version (first version with the levy articles). Dividends enter in the 2024 version (art. 3.4a and art. 5.2 on overlap with dividendbelasting). It hits payments to a related body (qualifying interest, always above 50% of the voting rights) in a low-tax jurisdiction (profit tax below 9% or on the EU list of non-cooperative jurisdictions) and abuse cases. Belastingdienst states 25,8% for 2024, 2025 and 2026. With art. 22 this gives 25% in 2021 and 25,8% from 2022. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0042952/2021-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0042952/2024-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0042952/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/bronbelasting-rente-en-royalty/

#### Box 2 rate (art. 2.12 Wet IB 2001)
2020 26,25%. 2021 to 2023 26,9%. 2024 t/m € 67.000 24,5%, above € 16.415 plus 33%. 2025 t/m € 67.804 24,5%, above € 16.611 plus 31%. 2026 t/m € 68.843 24,5%, above € 16.866 plus 31%. Future text for 2027 still prints the 2026 table. Art. 10.1 lid 1 Wet IB 2001 lists art. 2.12 among the amounts indexed each year with the tabelcorrectiefactor. Verified (the 2025 column III amount € 16.611 is added).
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 (each year)

#### Fiscale eenheid Vpb (art. 15 Wet Vpb 1969)
The mother must hold the full legal and economic ownership of at least 95% of the shares in the nominal paid-up capital of the daughter, carrying at least 95% of the statutory voting rights and a right to at least 95% of the profit and of the assets. On request of both, tax is levied as if there is one taxpayer. Identical wording in every text 2020 to 2026. Belastingdienst adds: mother a bv, nv, onderlinge waarborgmaatschappij, coöperatie, or stichting or vereniging acting as woningcorporatie (or a comparable foreign form), daughter a bv or nv, same book years and profit rules, both effectively established in the Netherlands. The daughter keeps existing but files no separate return, and losses of one company offset profits of another. A drop below 95% ends the fiscale eenheid at that moment, also when an option holder must offer the shares to the mother at once. Subjective exemption of one daughter ends it for that daughter only (art. 15 lid 10). Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/fiscale_eenheid_vennootschapsbelasting/, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2024-11814.html

#### Deelnemingsvrijstelling and holding structures
Art. 13 lid 1 leaves advantages from a deelneming out of the profit. Art. 13 lid 2: a deelneming exists from 5% of the nominal paid-up capital, identical in every text 2020 to 2026. In a holding structure the gebruikelijk loon test compares with the highest wage in connected bodies (1/3 interest). A geruisloze inbreng into an existing holding is approved under onderdeel 3.4 of the Besluit geruisloze omzetting when the conditions there are met. For btw a pure holding that only holds shares is not an ondernemer and cannot join a btw fiscale eenheid, but a holding that steers the group as policy-making body can join on written request. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2026-01-01, https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek-loonheffingen-lh0221t51d.pdf, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2025-37092.html, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2024-38545.html

#### Innovatiebox (art. 12b to 12bd Wet Vpb 1969)
Qualifying advantages from a self-made qualifying intangible asset count for 7/H (2020 text) and 9/H (2021 to 2026 texts), H being the highest art. 22 rate, so an effective 7% in 2020 and 9% from 2021. Belastingdienst: "Het effectieve tarief van de vennootschapsbelasting voor deze winsten is 9%". A smaller taxpayer needs only an S&O-verklaring. Smaller means advantages plus acquisition costs from S&O assets in the year and four preceding years below € 37.500.000 and net turnover over those five years below € 250.000.000 (art. 12ba lid 2, same 2020 and 2026). Others also need an access ticket of art. 12ba lid 1 sub b. Merken, logo's and comparable assets are excluded (art. 12ba lid 6). Art. 12bb: K/T part, K the qualifying expenditure multiplied by 1,3, capped at the whole advantage after the drempel of art. 12bc. Art. 12bd forfait: for a taxpayer who made a qualifying asset in the year or two preceding years and chooses it, 25% of the profit before art. 12b, maximum € 25.000, only when that profit is positive. Innovation losses are deductible at the normal rate. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2021-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002672/2026-01-01, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/innovatiebox/innovatiebox

#### Switch from eenmanszaak to BV, geruisloze omzetting (art. 3.65 Wet IB 2001)
On request the business is deemed not ceased. The whole business must be contributed, the shares for the contributed assets are placed with the contributor and fully paid up. Standard conditions (Besluit 2025, annex 1): selling shares within three years after the contribution is presumed part of a plan to transfer the business unless the contributor proves otherwise (this is a rebuttable presumption, not an absolute sale ban). The company steps into the contributor's place for profit determination. The contributor may be credited for IB, premie volksverzekeringen and, from the 2025 Besluit, the Zvw bijdrage materially due at the transition, plus a rounding credit of at most 5% of the paid-up amount and not more than € 30.000. The verkrijgingsprijs of the shares is the sum of the fiscal book values. Into an existing company only when it already runs a business in the same line, into a fiscale eenheid only through the mother whose shares the contributor holds directly. Retroactivity: if the voorovereenkomst is received by the Belastingdienst within nine months after the last full book year, the conversion may take effect from the first day of the next book year, strictly applied and not for an incidental tax advantage. Belastingdienst: request at the office that handles the inkomstenbelasting, register the voorovereenkomst or intentieverklaring with the Geleideformulier, the BV is a bv in oprichting until the notarial deed. Verified.
Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2025-37092.html, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/ondernemen/onderneming_wijzigen_of_beeindigen/uw_onderneming_wijzigt_van_rechtsvorm/uw_onderneming_wijzigt_van_rechtsvorm

#### Besluit geruisloze omzetting 2025
Besluit of 24 October 2025, published in Staatscourant 2025, 37092 on 4 November 2025, in force the day after publication. It replaces the besluit of 30 June 2010, nr. DGB 2010/3599M. Corrected: the research gave Stcrt. 2025, 3709 and named only onderdeel 3.4 and the eighth standard condition as substantive changes. The besluit lists more: 2.2.2.a (rented-out business or profit right qualifies when it amounts to medegerechtigdheid), 3.4 (holding approval widened to indirect holding and to a new or activity-free daughter), 4.2.2 (letteraandelen besides cumulatief preferente aandelen, plus art. 14a transfers), 6.1 (which reserves to settle), 8 (Zvw bijdrage credit), 12 (new eighth condition for a shareholder resident abroad), 13.1.2 (incidental tax advantage clarified), 13.2 (S&O approval in the voor-voorperiode), 15.3. The consolidated version is BWBR0051693 (not re-read).
Source: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2025-37092.html

#### Request timing for art. 3.65 (KG:212:2022:1)
The inspector decides by beschikking open to bezwaar that contains the conditions. A request is timely until omitting it has irrevocable tax consequences for one of the parties (onderdeel 14.2 of the old Besluit). In the case the request can be filed until the 2022 IB/PVV assessment is irrevocable, unless overdrachtswinst is reported for 2021, then the 2021 assessment decides. Standpunt adopted 27-06-2022, published 12-01-2023. Verified.
Source: https://kennisgroepen.belastingdienst.nl/publicaties/kg21220221-geruisloze-omzetting-termijn-indienen-verzoek-artikel-3-65-wet-ib-2001/

#### Ruisende inbreng
Without the facility, a change of legal form is a cessation. The owner draws up a final balance and pays IB on the stakingswinst, the difference between the book value and the real value of the business, and the fiscal reserves are released. The stakingsaftrek (art. 3.79, at most € 3.630, once in a lifetime) and an extra lijfrente premium deduction apply. Stille reserves realised by a direct onttrekking before a geruisloze omzetting do not get the stakingsaftrek but may go into a lijfrente (art. 3.129). Verified (undated current pages plus statute amounts per year).
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/ondernemen/onderneming_wijzigen_of_beeindigen/uw_onderneming_wijzigt_van_rechtsvorm/uw_onderneming_wijzigt_van_rechtsvorm, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/ondernemen/onderneming_wijzigen_of_beeindigen/u_staakt_uw_onderneming/stakingswinst_berekenen, https://ondernemersplein.overheid.nl/geldzaken-en-belastingen/alles-over-aangifte-doen/bedrijfsbeeindiging-stakingswinst-en-stakingsaftrek/, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2025-37092.html

#### BTW for a BV
The new legal form is a new business for btw and may get a new btw-identificatienummer, which goes to EU suppliers at once. Payroll gets a new loonheffingennummer if there are employees. Transfer tax on business real estate is usually not due on the change. Btw fiscale eenheid (art. 7 lid 4 Wet OB 1968): it arises by operation of law once the persons are financially, organisationally and economically intertwined, and changes by law on entry or exit. A beschikking (on request or by the inspector) states the start date and is the condition for joint and several liability. Financial intertwining: at least the majority of the ordinary shares with voting rights in the same hands. The Beleidsbesluit belastingplicht en fiscale eenheid omzetbelasting (4 December 2024, nr. 2024-13987, Stcrt. 2024, 38545, published 10 December 2024) states "Dit besluit treedt in werking met ingang van 1 juli 2025". Verified.
Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/ondernemen/onderneming_wijzigen_of_beeindigen/uw_onderneming_wijzigt_van_rechtsvorm/uw_onderneming_wijzigt_van_rechtsvorm, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2024-38545.html

#### Jaarrekening: size classes and deposit
A legal person falls in a class when on two consecutive balance dates it meets two of the three criteria. Statute 2020 (Boek 2 BW art. 395a, 396, 397): micro at most € 350.000 assets and € 700.000 net turnover and fewer than 10 employees, small € 6.000.000, € 12.000.000, fewer than 50, medium € 20.000.000, € 40.000.000, fewer than 250. Stb. 2024, 52 (5 March 2024) raises them to € 450.000 and € 900.000, € 7,5 miljoen and € 15 miljoen, € 25 miljoen and € 50 miljoen, for book years from 1 January 2024, optional from 1 January 2023. Employee counts unchanged. Deposit (art. 394): within eight days after vaststelling and at the latest twelve months after the book year ends. If not adopted within two months after the drawing-up term, the board deposits the unadopted accounts marking them so. Ondernemersplein: no extension, late filing is a criminal offence, from boekjaar 2025 (filing from 1 January 2026) all legal persons file through SBR. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0003045/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0003045/2025-01-01, https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2024-52.html, https://ondernemersplein.overheid.nl/wetten-en-regels/jaarrekening-indienen/, https://www.kvk.nl/deponeren/in-welke-bedrijfsklasse-valt-je-bedrijf/

#### Administratieplicht and bewaartermijn
Art. 52 AWR: lichamen are administratieplichtig and keep the records for seven years unless a tax law says otherwise. Data may move to another carrier except the paper balance sheet and profit and loss statement. Identical in the 2020 and 2026 texts. The research could not find the basis for longer terms. Art. 34a Wet OB 1968: records on onroerende zaken are kept for nine years following the year in which use began (in effect ten). Art. 28rl and 28sj Wet OB 1968: OSS records ten years after the year of the transaction. Verified.
Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2020-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2026-01-01, https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2026-01-01
