#### Wet DBA and the test for loondienst (2020 to 2027)
Verified, one detail Corrected. The Wet deregulering beoordeling arbeidsrelaties is the Wet van 3 februari 2016 and entered into force on 1 May 2016. It replaced the VAR. Correction: Staatsblad 2016, 165 is not the Wet DBA itself. It is the Besluit van 8 april 2016 that lets parties exclude the fictieve dienstbetrekking for thuiswerkers, gelijkgestelden and artiesten by agreement. Loondienst has three elements: the possibility of werkgeversgezag, the duty to perform (persoonlijke) arbeid, and pay for the work. The courts weigh among others the nature and duration of the work, how work and working hours are set, embedding in the organisation, the duty to work personally, how the agreements were made, how pay is set and paid and its level, commercial risk, and how far the opdrachtnemer acts or can act as an entrepreneur. Not being in loondienst does not make someone an ondernemer for income tax or VAT. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/arbeidsrelaties/content/wanneer-is-sprake-van-loondienst https://open.overheid.nl/documenten/d2c6b4e8-537c-48a8-9e17-85f1acf4825d/file https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2016-165.html

#### Fictieve dienstbetrekkingen and the opt-out (2020 to 2027)
Verified. The loondienst page lists the fictieve dienstbetrekkingen, among them aannemers van werk and helpers, agenten, artiesten and beroepssporters, gelijkgestelden (usually 2 or more days a week for the same client and at least 2/5 of the minimum wage), stagiairs, thuiswerkers and opdrachtnemers working through an intermediair. Thuiswerkers, gelijkgestelden and artiesten can be excluded in writing in the contract before the first payment of pay. The opdrachtnemer then has no entitlement to ZW, WIA and WW. Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/arbeidsrelaties/content/wanneer-is-sprake-van-loondienst

#### Handhavingsmoratorium (2020 to 2024)
Verified. Until 1 January 2025 the moratorium applied. In a non-kwaadwillend case the Belastingdienst first gave an aanwijzing: reshape the relationship so the opdrachtnemer really works as a zelfstandige, or process it as a dienstbetrekking in the loonaangifte, usually within 3 months. If the aanwijzing was not followed, a correctieverplichting applied from the date of the aanwijzing. Kwaadwillend means opzettelijk een situatie van evidente schijnzelfstandigheid laten ontstaan of voortbestaan, knowing or able to know that there is in fact a dienstbetrekking. Kwaadwillend cases could get correctieverplichtingen, naheffingsaanslagen loonheffingen and boetes. The second restriction of the moratorium exception (enforcement when aanwijzingen are not followed within a reasonable term, for periods from 1 January 2020) and the fourth postponement to 1 January 2021 were both announced in the derde voortgangsbrief of 24 June 2019. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/arbeidsrelaties/content/handhaving https://open.overheid.nl/documenten/d2c6b4e8-537c-48a8-9e17-85f1acf4825d/file

#### End of the moratorium and the ingroeimodel (2025 to 2030)
Verified. Since 1 January 2025 the normal rules apply. No aanwijzingen are given. Schijnzelfstandigheid can lead directly to correctieverplichtingen and naheffingsaanslagen loonheffingen. Corrections go no further back than 1 January 2025 unless there is kwaadwillendheid or an aanwijzing given before that date was not followed. In that case up to 5 years back. Only in 2030 can the Belastingdienst again go back the full 5 years. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/arbeidsrelaties/content/handhaving https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z52fd.pdf https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z62fd.pdf

#### DBA boetebeleid by year (2025 to 2027)
Verified. 2025: no verzuimboetes and no vergrijpboetes for the qualification of arbeidsrelaties (motie Flach/Aartsen, Kamerstuk 31 311, nr. 266). For correcties over 2025 this stays so. 2026: no verzuimboetes, vergrijpboetes possible under the normal rules from 1 January 2026, also for correcties over 2026. 2027: the remaining parts of the zachte landing (no verzuimboetes, in principle starting with a bedrijfsbezoek) lapse on 1 January 2027. This is enforcement policy, not statute. Sources: https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z62fd.pdf https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/arbeidsrelaties/content/handhaving

#### Move to regular handhaving (2026 to 2027)
Corrected. The research said the ambition is regular integration from 1 January 2027. That is right for the 2026 plan, but the tranche 2025 plan had stated integration from 1 January 2026. The 2026 plan records that the ambition was 1 January 2026, that a separate plan was made again for 2026, and that the Belastingdienst aims to integrate from 2027. Sources: https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z52fd.pdf https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z62fd.pdf

#### Doorwerking to inkomstenbelasting and omzetbelasting for the opdrachtnemer (2023 to 2027)
Verified. Richtlijnen doorwerking IH and OB exist since 2023 and were updated for the end of the moratorium. Focus is on the loonheffingen at the opdrachtgever. After LH corrections at the opdrachtgever the Belastingdienst in principle takes no targeted action for earlier years at the opdrachtnemer aimed at bronkwalificatie. Earlier years means tax years before the year of the LH correction. An opdrachtnemer in regular IH or OB supervision has the most recent open (winst)aangifte or tijdvakken examined, with attention to future returns. Sources: https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z52fd.pdf https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z62fd.pdf

#### Vooroverleg (2025 to 2027)
Verified. The Belastingdienst takes a standpoint on a complete verzoek vooroverleg. The standpoint gives only conditional certainty, valid if the work is carried out exactly as described. The streeftermijn is 8 weeks and may be extended under a large request stream. A checklist vooroverleg exists. Sources: https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z52fd.pdf https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z62fd.pdf

#### Modelovereenkomsten (2020 to 2029)
Verified. Working under an approved modelovereenkomst means no loondienst, but only if the parties work as described. Since 6 September 2024 no new modelovereenkomsten are assessed and none are renewed. Approved agreements valid on 6 September 2024 can be used up to and including 31 December 2029, even if the agreement states another validity date. Reason given: modelovereenkomsten can create schijnzekerheid because the qualification depends on how the work is actually done. The tranche 2025 plan adds that they are honoured to end 2029 after motie Van Oostenbruggen (Kamerstuk 31 311, nr. 275), on the condition that they comply with law and regulations. The approval of the modelovereenkomsten vrije vervanging was withdrawn on 14 August 2023 after the Deliveroo ruling. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/arbeidsrelaties/content/werken-met-modelovereenkomsten https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/arbeidsrelaties/content/geen-nieuwe-modelovereenkomsten-meer https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z52fd.pdf https://open.overheid.nl/documenten/d2c6b4e8-537c-48a8-9e17-85f1acf4825d/file

#### Webmodule Beoordeling Arbeidsrelatie (2021 to 2027)
Verified. For opdrachtgevers with a business in the Netherlands. Three outcomes: indication that work can be done outside dienstbetrekking, indication of a dienstbetrekking, or no judgment possible. Not a legal decision and gives no rights. Not useful for a contract with a rechtspersoon such as a bv, for a bemiddelaar or tussenpersoon, or for a foreign opdrachtnemer. Pilot from 11 January 2021 for 6 months, ended 10 July 2021, online pilot environment stayed active, no rechtskracht during or after the pilot. The pilot design used a points system with an opdrachtgeversverklaring as one outcome. Sources: https://ondernemersplein.overheid.nl/personeel/inhuren/webmodule-beoordeling-arbeidsrelatie/ https://open.overheid.nl/documenten/d2c6b4e8-537c-48a8-9e17-85f1acf4825d/file

#### Case law on the arbeidsovereenkomst (2020 to 2027)
Verified, one date UNVERIFIED. HR 6 November 2020, ECLI:NL:HR:2020:1746, on the role of partijbedoeling. HR 24 March 2023, ECLI:NL:HR:2023:443 (Deliveroo), with an omstandighedencatalogus that names inbedding and ondernemerschap, and vrije vervanging not as an absolute element. HR Uber (prejudiciële vragen): no rangorde between the gezichtspunten, so ondernemerschap is a full criterion. The government states this has no effect on handhaving. The date 21 February 2025 and ECLI:NL:HR:2025:319 come from a later court citation found by search, not from a fetched copy of the ruling. Sources: https://open.overheid.nl/documenten/d2c6b4e8-537c-48a8-9e17-85f1acf4825d/file https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2025/03/27/ondernemerschap-blijft-volwaardig-criterium-bij-beoordelen-schijnzelfstandigheid

#### VBAR: rechtsvermoeden van arbeidsovereenkomst op basis van een uurtarief (2023 to 2027)
Verified. Internet consultation of the original VBAR from 6 October 2023 with intended start 1 July 2025. To the Raad van State on 21 June 2024, critical advice published 11 November 2024. Bill 36 783 ingediend on 4 July 2025 per the Eerste Kamer and 7 July 2025 per the Tweede Kamer. Nota van wijziging of 10 March 2026 (36 783, nr. 6) removed the verduidelijking part. Tweede Kamer adopted it on 21 April 2026 (all parties except FVD), Eerste Kamer on 16 June 2026 by zitten en opstaan. Wet van 18 juni 2026, Stb. 2026, 158, published 29 June 2026. In force on 31 December 2026 by Besluit van 13 juli 2026, Stb. 2026, 207, published 15 July 2026, chosen to meet the Herstel- en Veerkrachtplan milestone (in force before 1 January 2027). New art. 7:610aa BW: lid 1 someone who works for another for pay of at most € 36 per hour is presumed to work under an arbeidsovereenkomst. Lid 2 the Minister of SZW changes the amount in the manner of art. 14 lid 1 and 2 Wet minimumloon en minimumvakantiebijslag, rounded up to whole euros, always starting from the unrounded amount. Lid 3 not applicable when the other party is a natural person not acting in a profession or business. Artikel II evaluation within 5 years. Artikel III lid 2: for the first application after entry into force the applicable amount is given by ministeriële regeling. Effect: the werkende can invoke the presumption and the opdrachtgever must prove there is no arbeidsovereenkomst. It is civil law only, with no fiscal qualification rule. Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2026-158.html https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2026-207.html https://www.eerstekamer.nl/wetsvoorstel/36783_wet_invoering https://www.tweedekamer.nl/kamerstukken/wetsvoorstellen/detail?cfg=wetsvoorsteldetails&qry=wetsvoorstel%3A36783 https://ondernemersplein.overheid.nl/wetswijzigingen/laag-uurtarief-van-zzper-kan-leiden-tot-arbeidsovereenkomst/

#### VBAR hourly amount, € 36 against € 38 (settled)
Verified and settled. The memorie van toelichting explains € 36: 120% of the WML of € 14,06 per hour at 1 January 2025, plus a 25% opslag for AOV and pension and a 15% opslag for general costs, times 1,5 for non-billable hours, gives an unrounded € 35,43 excl. btw at peildatum 1 January 2025, rounded up to € 36,00. Every later change starts from the unrounded € 35,43 and follows the WML percentage. The WML rose to € 14,71 on 1 January 2026, so the unrounded amount at peildatum 1 January 2026 is about € 37,07 and the rounded amount is € 38. This is the € 38 (peildatum 1 januari 2026) that the Eerste Kamer, rijksoverheid.nl and Ondernemersplein state. So € 36 is the statutory base text at peildatum 2025 and € 38 is the indexed amount at peildatum 2026. The ministeriële regeling under art. III lid 2 that sets the amount for the first application was not found on 2026-10-11. The arithmetic for 1 January 2026 is mine from the MvT method and the WML figures, the € 38 itself is stated on the primary pages. Sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36783-3.html https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/03/06/kabinet-kiest-voor-meer-rust-en-duidelijkheid-voor-zzpers-en-opdrachtgevers https://www.eerstekamer.nl/wetsvoorstel/36783_wet_invoering https://ondernemersplein.overheid.nl/wetswijzigingen/laag-uurtarief-van-zzper-kan-leiden-tot-arbeidsovereenkomst/ https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/themas/werk/minimumloon/bedragen-minimumloon/bedragen-minimumloon-2025 https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/themas/werk/minimumloon/bedragen-minimumloon/bedragen-minimumloon-2026

#### Zelfstandigenwet (proposed, intended January 2028)
Verified. Internet consultation open until 29 October 2026, beoogde inwerkingtreding January 2028. Zelfstandigentoets (self-invoicing, KVK registration, number of opdrachtgevers, provisions against ondernemersrisico's) and werkrelatietoets (freedom to organise the work, own account and risk). Meeting both and working that way gives a veilige haven. Those who do not qualify keep the current rules. Source: https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/10/01/zelfstandigenwet-biedt-meer-duidelijkheid-en-erkenning-voor-zzpers

#### BAZ, bill 36 912 (proposed)
Verified. Ingediend 24 March 2026, technische briefing 13 May 2026, inbreng verslag 27 May 2026 after a postponement from 20 May, verslag published 29 May 2026 (36 912, nr. 5), on 2026-10-11 awaiting the nota naar aanleiding van het verslag, no debate and no vote. Docket summary: about 70% of income up to the minimum wage, waiting period about one year, opt-out with a qualifying private policy. Source: https://www.tweedekamer.nl/kamerstukken/wetsvoorstellen/detail?cfg=wetsvoorsteldetails&qry=wetsvoorstel%3A36912

#### Income tax filing deadline and uitstel (2020 to 2027)
Verified. The return must arrive before the date in the aangiftebrief, often 1 May. The filing period opens on 1 March. Uitstel must be requested before the filing date, in the annual period before 1 May. A granted uitstel gives 4 months extra, to 1 September. The form can be used only for the first uitstel request, and the fiscal partner must request separately. Uitstel usually means belastingrente. AWR art. 9 lid 1: for aanslagbelastingen the return is filed within a term set by the inspecteur of at least one month after the invitation, and art. 9 lid 2 lets the inspecteur extend that term under conditions (same text in 2020 and 2026). Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/belastingaangifte/content/wanneer-moet-ik-aangifte-doen https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/themaoverstijgend/programmas_en_formulieren/aanvraag-uitstel-aangifte-inkomstenbelasting https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/belastingaangifte/content/kan-ik-nog-uitstel-aanvragen-belastingaangifte https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2026-01-01

#### Uitstel channel (settled)
Verified and settled. The form page says uitstel can also be requested online in Mijn Belastingdienst until 1 May, giving uitstel to 1 September, only for someone who received an aangiftebrief. The uitstel page says online is not possible. Read in full, the uitstel page addresses the period after 1 May and a filing date other than 1 May, where the paper form sent by post is the route. Both are current Belastingdienst text, so: online until 1 May for the regular 1 May date, paper form otherwise. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/themaoverstijgend/programmas_en_formulieren/aanvraag-uitstel-aangifte-inkomstenbelasting https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/belastingaangifte/content/kan-ik-nog-uitstel-aanvragen-belastingaangifte

#### Definitive assessment term (2020 to 2027)
Verified, one rule added. AWR art. 11 lid 3 (same text on 1 January 2020 and 1 January 2026): the power to set the aanslag lapses three years after the belastingschuld arose, extended by the duration of filing uitstel. Added: if within the last six months of that term a request to be invited to file (art. 6 lid 2 or 3) is made or data under art. 9 lid 4 are supplied, the term is extended by six months. The Belastingdienst counts the 3 years from 1 January after the tax year. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2020-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/hoe_werkt_inkomstenbelasting/aanslag/definitieve_aanslag

#### Aanslaggrens and teruggaafgrens (2020 to 2026)
Verified, with a wording change found. The current page says tax is only due if the amount is higher than (hoger dan) the aanslaggrens and a refund is only paid if it is higher than the teruggaafgrens. Pages up to the 2023 captures used gelijk aan of meer dan and printed each grens one euro higher (2020 € 48 and € 16, 2021 € 49 and € 16, 2022 € 49 and € 16, 2023 € 52 and € 17). From the 2024 capture the table prints € 47 and € 15 for 2020, € 48 and € 15 for 2021 and 2022, € 51 and € 16 for 2023. For whole-euro amounts the two forms describe the same threshold, which is my reading. Monthly refunds received through a voorlopige aanslag before year end count toward the grens, a paid voorlopige aanslag does not, and a refund received after filing does not. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/hoe_werkt_inkomstenbelasting/aanslag/definitieve_aanslag https://web.archive.org/web/20210413/https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/hoe_werkt_inkomstenbelasting/aanslag/definitieve_aanslag https://web.archive.org/web/20230401/https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/hoe_werkt_inkomstenbelasting/aanslag/definitieve_aanslag https://web.archive.org/web/20250328/https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/hoe_werkt_inkomstenbelasting/aanslag/definitieve_aanslag

#### Navordering (2020 to 2027)
Verified. AWR art. 16 (same in 2020 and 2026 texts). Lid 1: a new fact, not usable if known or reasonably knowable to the inspecteur unless the taxpayer acted te kwader trouw. Lid 2: also when a voorlopige aanslag, voorheffing, voorlopige teruggaaf or voorlopige verliesverrekening was wrongly settled, and for a fout that was redelijkerwijs kenbaar, always so when the shortfall is at least 30% of the correct tax. Lid 3: 5 years after the belastingschuld arose, extended by uitstel, and for a fout-based navordering only 2 years after the assessment or decision. Lid 4: 12 years for income or assets held or arising abroad. Lid 5: extension by 6 months if in the last 6 months a request for an invitation is made or data under art. 9 lid 4 are supplied. The Belastingdienst page gives the example of tax year 2016 with 5 months uitstel, navordering possible until 31 May 2022. A voluntary correction within 2 years generally gives no boete. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2020-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/standaard_functies/prive/contact/rechten_en_plichten_bij_de_belastingdienst/navorderingsaanslag

#### Payment terms of assessments (2020 to 2027, added)
Verified. Invorderingswet 1990 art. 9, same text on 1 January 2020, 2024, 2025 and 2026. An aanslag is invorderbaar 6 weeks after the date of the aanslagbiljet. A navorderingsaanslag 1 month after. A naheffingsaanslag 14 days after. A voorlopige aanslag IB dated in the tax year is payable in as many equal monthly termijnen as months remain after the month of the date, the first one month after the date. A voorlopige aanslag dated before the tax year is payable in 12 monthly termijnen from the end of January. A voorlopige aanslag with a refund is paid out monthly from the month of the date. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0004770/2020-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0004770/2026-01-01

#### Bezwaar and verzoek om vermindering (2020 to 2027)
Verified. Bezwaar within 6 weeks after the date of the aanslag, for income tax online, by pdf form or by letter. The motivation can follow later if asked for in the bezwaar. Acknowledgement within 3 weeks. No bezwaar against a voorlopige aanslag IB, only a change. After the bezwaar term a verzoek om (ambtshalve) vermindering is possible. Online only if the return was filed online and fewer than 5 years have passed after the tax year (aanslag 2020 online until 31 December 2025, aanslag 2023 until 31 December 2028). Otherwise by letter. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/bezwaar-en-beroep/content/eisen-bezwaar https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/bezwaar-en-beroep/content/verzoek-om-vermindering-van-aanslag-inkomstenbelasting

#### Voorlopige aanslag (2020 to 2027)
Verified. An estimate based on known data such as the previous return. Changing is not mandatory. Reasons include buying a home, changing mortgage interest, higher or lower income, a change in business profit and stopping the business (stakingswinst). Entrepreneurs change the expected profit in the Winst uit onderneming screen. Paid or received amounts are settled in the return. A voorlopige aanslag can be requested or changed usually until 1 May of the next year, with an aangiftebrief until the filing date or the uitstel date, and no longer after filing. A change for 2025 after 15 October is not carried over automatically to 2026. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/voorlopige-aanslag/content/in-welke-situaties-moet-ik-mijn-voorlopige-aanslag-wijzigen https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/voorlopige-aanslag/content/tot-wanneer-voorlopige-aanslag-aanvragen-wijzigen

#### Belastingrente rates and rate setting (2020 to 2027)
Verified. All taxes except Vpb and toeslagen: 4% from 1 April 2014 to 31 May 2020, 0,01% from 1 June 2020 to 30 September 2020 (for the inkomstenbelasting the reduction started 1 July 2020), 4% from 1 October 2020 to 30 June 2023, 6% from 1 July 2023 to 31 December 2023, 7,5% in 2024, 6,5% in 2025, 5% from 1 January 2026. From 1 January 2024 the rate is set once a year for 1 January of the next year from the last ECB herfinancieringsrente published before 1 November, plus 3 points for these taxes, rounded to half percentages, minimum 4,5%. Vpb, bronbelasting, minimumbelasting and solidariteitsbijdrage plus 5,5 points, minimum 5,5%. After HR 16 January 2026 (ECLI:NL:HR:2026:59) the Vpb table from 2022 was aligned with the other taxes. The mass objection on the rate was granted for Vpb and similar taxes and rejected for income tax, btw and the other taxes. Correction: 25 February 2026 is the update date of the mass-objection page, the page gives no date for the collective decisions. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/standaard_functies/prive/contact/rechten_en_plichten_bij_de_belastingdienst/belastingrente/overzicht_percentages_belastingrente https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/standaard_functies/prive/contact/rechten_en_plichten_bij_de_belastingdienst/belastingrente/massaal-bezwaar-voor-de-hoogte-van-het-belastingrentepercentage

#### Belastingrente on income tax (2020 to 2027)
Verified. Charged when the return arrives on or after 1 May after the tax year, or when the aanslag deviates from the return, also if filed before 1 May. Period from 1 July after the tax year, also with uitstel. It ends 6 weeks after the aanslag date, and when the aanslag follows the return unchanged no later than 19 weeks after receipt of the return. For a navorderingsaanslag from 1 July after the tax year to 1 month after its date. No rente when the return is filed before 1 May and taken over unchanged. 30 days per month, 360 days per year, rounded down to whole euros. Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/standaard_functies/prive/contact/rechten_en_plichten_bij_de_belastingdienst/belastingrente/belastingrente_betalen_bij_inkomstenbelasting

#### Belastingrente on btw (2020 to 2027)
Verified. Charged on a naheffingsaanslag and on late payment. Late payment: from 1 January after the tax year to the payment date. Naheffingsaanslag: from 1 January after the tax year to 14 days after its date. No rente for a voluntary correction within 3 months after the tax year or a late payment within 3 months after the end of the tax year. The rentestop extension to requested naheffingsaanslagen (end at most 10 weeks after receipt) applies from 1 January 2023 but is not yet in force for btw because it is technically not executable. Source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/standaard_functies/prive/contact/rechten_en_plichten_bij_de_belastingdienst/belastingrente/belastingrente_betalen_bij_loonbelasting_btw_en_overdrachtsbelasting

#### VAT filing and payment deadline (2020 to 2027, added)
Verified. AWR art. 10 lid 2: a return for a tijdvak is filed within a term set by the inspecteur of at least one month after the end of the tijdvak. AWR art. 19 lid 1: the tax is paid within one month after the end of the tijdvak. The Belastingdienst tables give for every year the last day of the month after the period for return and payment together (monthly and quarterly) and 31 March of the next year for a yearly return. Return and payment must both be received by that date. Software can submit from the 24th of the month before the period ends. Uitstel for a btw-aangifte only in uitzonderlijke situaties (an ernstige calamiteit), in writing to the belastingkantoor before the filing date, and a naheffingsaanslag and boete remain possible. Btw aangifteverzuim: 7 kalenderdagen coulance, then a boete of € 68 (2020 to 2024) or € 82 (2025, 2026). Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/uiterste-aangifte-en-betaaldatums https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/uitstel-aanvragen-btw-aangifte https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_aangifte_doen_en_betalen/boetes/u_doet_geen_of_te_laat_aangifte

#### Income tax boetes and the missed deadline route (2020 to 2027)
Verified. After the deadline a herinnering, then an aanmaning with 10 werkdagen. Filing after the aanmaning term gives the verzuimboete. The Belastingdienst can stop toeslagen and the voorlopige aanslag. No return at all gives an ambtshalve aanslag plus verzuimboete. Intentional non-filing or an intentionally wrong return gives a vergrijpboete, also with a navorderingsaanslag. No boete when the person died on or after the date of the aangiftebrief. Requesting a return: within 6 months after the belastingschuld arose for IB and Vpb, 2 weeks coulance. Amounts: 2020 to 2024 verzuimboete IB € 385, up to € 5.514, not requesting a return € 2.757, betaalverzuimboete up to € 5.514. 2025 and 2026 € 469, up to € 6.709, € 3.354, betaalverzuimboete up to € 6.709 (the 2026 page adds 5% of the open amount, minimum € 50). Vergrijpboete 25% grove schuld, 50% opzet, box 3 75% and 150%, aggravated 100% and 300%. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/belastingaangifte/content/wat-gebeurt-er-als-ik-geen-aangifte-doe-of-te-laat-of-onvolledig https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/standaard_functies/prive/contact/rechten_en_plichten_bij_de_belastingdienst/boete

#### Filing without an invitation, five years back (2020 to 2027)
Verified. Without an aangiftebrief a return can be filed up to 5 years back, by 31 December of the fifth year after the tax year. Sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/hoe_werkt_inkomstenbelasting/aangifte_doen/hoeveel-jaar-terug-belastingaangifte-doen https://web.archive.org/web/20220408/https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/hoe_werkt_inkomstenbelasting/aangifte_doen/hoeveel-jaar-terug-belastingaangifte-doen

#### Bewaarplicht (2020 to 2027)
Verified. AWR art. 52 lid 4 (same in 2020 and 2026): administratieplichtigen keep the gegevensdragers for seven years unless the tax law says otherwise. Lid 2 covers among others natural persons with winst uit onderneming or resultaat uit overige werkzaamheden. Lid 5 allows conversion to another gegevensdrager except the paper balans and staat van baten en lasten. Belastingdienst table: basisgegevens 7 years, onroerende zaken 10 years, One Stop Shop data 10 years, other data 7 years or shorter by agreement with the belastingkantoor. The term starts when the gegeven is no longer actual (a 4-year lease is kept for those 4 years and then 7 more). Basisgegevens: grootboek, debiteuren- and crediteurenadministratie, voorraadadministratie, in- and verkoopadministratie, loonadministratie, and data relevant for the taxation of third parties. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2020-01-01 https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002320/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/ondernemen/administratie/hoelang-moet-u-gegevens-bewaren

#### KVK and Belastingdienst registration (2020 to 2027)
Verified. Handelsregisterwet 2007 art. 20 lid 1: the opgave for the first inschrijving of an onderneming is made within a period of two weeks that starts one week before and ends one week after the start of the business. Other required opgaven within one week after the fact. KVK issues the KVK-nummer immediately when registering within that window, an identity check is possible up to three months ahead, appointments up to eight weeks ahead. KVK passes the data to the Belastingdienst, so no separate registration. An eenmanszaak is registered with the bsn, other forms get an RSIN, the Belastingdienst derives the omzetbelastingnummer from it. Registration in the Belastingdienst administration takes at most 10 werkdagen. Where KVK registration is not possible, the form Opgaaf startende onderneming (niet ingeschreven in het Handelsregister) is used. Sources: https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0021777/2025-07-16 https://www.kvk.nl/hulp-en-contact/hulp-bij-inschrijven/ https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/ondernemen/onderneming_starten/schrijf-uw-onderneming-in/

---

## 4. SETTLED SOURCE CONFLICTS

Where two sources disagreed and a primary source settles it, the year files use the settled value below.

### BTW (VAT)

- KOR turnover test, baseline against the research: for 2020 to 2024 only the current calendar year counts (old art. 25 lid 1 "omzet in een kalenderjaar", no preceding-year wording in the 2020, 2021-07-01, 2022, 2023 and 2024 versions). The baseline "year of application and the year before" is right only from 2025 (art. 25a lid 5 and 9). https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2024-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2025-01-01
- KOR 2020 transition source: the research called stcrt-2019-4774 an official transition explanation. It is the Advies Raad van State with nader rapport of 1 februari 2019 on the Wet modernisering KOR. Its dates and examples stand. https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2019-4774.html
- Yearly return deadline "vóór 1 april" against "31 maart": no conflict, both put the last day at 31 maart. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_aangifte_doen_en_betalen/btw-aangifte-waar-moet-u-aan-denken/ and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/btw/content/uiterste-aangifte-en-betaaldatums
- MOSS replacement: MOSS lapsed on 1 juli 2021 and the eenloketsysteem took its place. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/zakendoen_met_het_buitenland/goederen_en_diensten_naar_andere_eu_landen/btw_berekenen_bij_diensten/wijziging_in_digitale_diensten_vanaf_2015/eu_btw_melding_doen/
- Suppletie boundary 2020: "minder dan € 1.000" (Handboek 2020). 2026: "€ 1.000 of minder" (Handboek 2026). 2021 to 2025 stay open. https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2020_aa9701z01fd.pdf and https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2026_aa9701z62fd.pdf
- Start of the 8-week suppletie term (baseline UNVERIFIED): 1 januari 2025, Art. XXIII and XXIV of the Eindejaarsbesluit 2024. https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2024-441.html
- Car cap (baseline "capped in the first years"): the cap is the VAT deducted in the year plus 1/5 of purchase VAT for four years after the year of first use, then the VAT deducted in the year. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0043783/2020-07-04
- Car 1,5 percent "after 4 years" against "more than 5 years in use, purchase year counts": the same rule. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0043783/2020-07-04
- € 227 BUA amount location: BUA art. 4 lid 1, version geldend 23-02-2019 t/m heden. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002636/2019-02-23
- € 1.883 yearly return limit for 2024 (baseline "not found"): not settled for 2024 because the 2024 handbook URL returns 404, settled for 2020 and 2026. https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2026_aa9701z62fd.pdf
- Belastingplan 2027 KOR amendment (research "unknown"): the bill text contains no KOR change. https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-37022-2.html
- Verlegging article range: Afdeling E is arts. 24b, 24ba and 24bb, not "24 to 24bb". https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002633/2026-01-01

### Income tax box 1 and the Zvw contribution

- AHK payout end date (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/heffingskortingen says 2024, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2025/heffingskortingen says 2023): settled at 2023. AK and IACK payouts ended from 2023 and the AHK payout ended from 2023 for people born in 1963 or later, continuing for people born before 1963. Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2021/heffingskortingen, https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2022/heffingskortingen and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2023/heffingskortingen.
- 2023 AHK afbouwpunt (€ 22.060 against € 22.660): € 22.660. Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2023/heffingskortingen table and https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/toelichting_m_biljet_2023_ib3271t31fd.pdf.
- 2023 whole-year AOW AK top band (€ 2.374 against € 2.604): maximum € 2.604 with 3,355% above € 37.691. € 2.374 is the starting amount of the third band. Deciding: https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/toelichting_m_biljet_2023_ib3271t31fd.pdf.
- AHK nil point 2024, 2025 and 2026 (table "from € 75.519 / € 76.818 / € 78.427" against FISIN "€ 75.518 / € 76.817 / above € 78.426"): one rule. The phase-out ends at the top bracket boundary and the korting is nil for any higher income. Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/algemene_heffingskorting/tabel-algemene-heffingskorting-2024 to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/algemene_heffingskorting/tabel-algemene-heffingskorting-2026 and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2024/heffingskortingen to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/heffingskortingen.
- 2026 AOW-age first-bracket rate for people born on or after 1 January 1946 (17,85% against 35,75%): 17,85%. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/belastingberekening prints the below-AOW table under that heading. Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/boxen_en_tarieven/box_1/box_1, consistent with https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/belastingberekening's own pre-1946 table at 17,85% and with https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 (8,10% income-tax part).
- Zelfstandigenaftrek at AOW age 2024 (€ 2.123 against € 1.875): € 1.875 (50% of € 3.750). Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/verandering_inkomstenbelasting_vorige_jaren/veranderingen-inkomstenbelasting-2024/ondernemersaftrek-2024/zelfstandigenaftrek-2024.
- Zelfstandigenaftrek 2025 (€ 2.470 against € 2.740): € 2.470. Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/verandering_inkomstenbelasting_vorige_jaren/veranderingen-inkomstenbelasting-2025/ondernemersaftrek-2025/zelfstandigenaftrek-2025 and the https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2025/ondernemersaftrek_en_investeringsaftrek table.
- MKB-winstvrijstelling 2024 (14% against 13,31%): 13,31%. Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/mkb_winstvrijstelling.
- Belastingdeel 2024 (9,28% on https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2024/heffingskortingen against 9,32%): 9,32%. Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/boxen_en_tarieven/box_1/box_1 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01.
- IACK co-ouder rule 2024 (156 days in 6 months on https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2024/heffingskortingen against 156 days per calendar year): 156 days per calendar year per household, prorated for at least 6 months of co-parenting. Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting.
- IACK 2027 phase-down status (unknown against enacted): enacted in Belastingplan 2024, nine steps to abolition per 2035. Deciding: https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D43301 and https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2025D26369.
- 2027 top bracket boundary (€ 78.426 in the bill against € 80.578): € 80.578 is now the bill text through https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D50329. Still proposed. Deciding: https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D50329.
- 2027 AK maximum (€ 5.929 against € 5.889): € 5.889 after https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D50329 (increase € 133 instead of € 173). Still proposed. Deciding: https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D50329 and https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D50335.
- 2027 AHK afbouwpercentage after the boundary move (not found): 6,350%. Still proposed. Deciding: https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D50335.
- 2026 whole-year AOW IACK rate (5,73% in the topic file): 5,72%. Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting-2026 and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/heffingskortingen.
- Zvw base for a winst-ondernemer (not found): belastbare winst after ondernemersaftrek and MKB-winstvrijstelling. Deciding: https://wetten.overheid.nl/jci1.3:c:BWBR0018450&hoofdstuk=4&paragraaf=4.2&artikel=43&z=2026-01-01&g=2026-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 art. 3.2.
- Werkgeversheffing 2020 to 2024 (not found): 6,70%, 7,00%, 6,75%, 6,68%, 6,57%. Deciding: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet to https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2024/inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet.
- Baseline note that Belastingplan 2027 has one technical nota van wijziging: a Tweede nota van wijziging (https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D50329) now carries the letter measures. Deciding: https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D50329.

Still open: the 2024 AHK AOW phase-out rate (3,421% against 3,420%), the 2024 and 2026 IACK maximum points, the 2026 IACK AOW maximum (€ 1.513 against € 1.514), the 2026 monthly AOW transition rounding, the 2020 oudedagsreserve maximum (€ 9.214 against € 9.218) and the 2027 Zvw maximum.

### Ondernemersaftrek and MKB-winstvrijstelling

- Startersaftrek prior-years condition (2020 to 2023 in the research). Settled: not an ondernemer in one or more of the five preceding calendar years, with the zelfstandigenaftrek applied at most twice in that period. The deepseek-3 reading "not an ondernemer in each of the past 5 years" is wrong, the Belastingdienst wording "niet elk jaar ondernemer" means the same as the statute. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 (art. 3.76 lid 3) and https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/inkomstenbelasting_voor_ondernemers/ondernemersaftrek/startersaftrek
- Oudedagsreserve maximum 2020: € 9.218 (statute art. 3.68 lid 1 valid on 1 January 2020). FISIN 2020 still prints € 9.214 in two places. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01
- Zelfstandigenaftrek at AOW age 2024: € 1.875 (art. 3.76 lid 2 € 3.750 and lid 4 50%). FISIN 2024 prints € 2.123 in the same sentence, a misprint. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01
- MKB-winstvrijstelling 2024: 13,31% (art. 3.79a valid on 1 January 2024 and the current Belastingdienst MKB page). FISIN 2024 prints 14%. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01
- Zelfstandigenaftrek 2025: € 2.470 (art. 3.76 valid on 1 January 2025 and the FISIN 2025 table). The € 2.740 appears only in the FISIN 2025 and FISIN 2026 carry-forward examples. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01
- Startersaftrek 2027 and 2028: three layers kept apart. Enacted 2027 text € 2.123 (adopted-not-yet-in-force), bill 37 022 € 10 in 2027 and abolition from 1 January 2028 (proposed), KVK statement not matching either. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 and https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-37022-2.html
- Meewerkaftrek and stakingsaftrek 2027: enacted text 1,25% to 4% and € 3.630 (adopted-not-yet-in-force) against the cuts in the Wet fiscale stimulering start-ups en scale-ups (proposed). The research gap on the carrying bill is closed: its Artikel I onderdelen B and C carry the cuts, Artikel II the abolition. https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/site/binaries/site-content/collections/documents/2026/09/15/wetsvoorstel-fiscale-stimulering-start-ups-en-scale-ups/wetsvoorstel-wet-fiscale-stimulering-start-ups-en-scale-ups-wetsvoorstel.pdf
- Meewerkaftrek boundary hours: the statute bands run from "gelijk aan of meer dan" to "minder dan", so 875, 1.225 and 1.750 hours fall in the higher band. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 (art. 3.78 lid 2)
- Oudedagsreserve percentage 2020 to 2022: 9,44% in each statute version valid on 1 January 2020, 2021 and 2022, so no AMvB change applied. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01
- Second-test lookback windows: FISIN 2020 prints 2014 to 2018 and FISIN 2022 prints "20167 tot en met 2021". The statute gives the five preceding calendar years, so 2015 to 2019 and 2017 to 2021. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 (art. 3.6)
- Tariefsaanpassing 2027: the enacted text holds 11,91% (maximum value 37,59%), the Belastingplan 2027 figure is 38,16% (proposed). Both layers kept. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 and https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/site/binaries/site-content/collections/documents/2026/09/15/fiscale-sleuteltabel-2027/fiscale-sleuteltabel-2027.pdf

### Investment deductions and depreciation

- MIA 2022 and 2023: statute 45% / 36% / 27% against baseline and FISIN 2022 and 2023 36% / 27% / 13,5%. Settled to 45% / 36% / 27% from 1 January 2022. Deciding sources: art. 3.42a lid 3 in https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01 (the 2021 text still reads 36 / 27 / 13,5), the Milieulijst 2022 regulation in force 1 January 2022 (https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2021-47737.html), the Rijksoverheid Belastingplan 2022 news "vanaf 1 januari 2022 verhoogd van 13,5%, 27% en 36% naar respectievelijk 27%, 36% en 45%" (https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2021/09/21/belastingplan-2022-beter-belastingstelsel) and the Belastingdienst Vpb-2023 code table. The FISIN 2022 and 2023 chapters are the losing source. The baseline 2024 change line "MIA 36/27/13,5 to 45/36/27" must go, 2024 brought no MIA change.
- Desinvesteringsbijtelling 2020: € 2.400 (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 art. 3.47 lid 1) against € 2.300 (FISIN 2020). Settled to € 2.400.
- Desinvesteringsbijtelling 2025: € 2.900 (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01 art. 3.47 lid 1) against € 2.800 (FISIN 2025). Settled to € 2.900.
- EIA and MIA maximum 2023: € 136.000.000 (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01 art. 3.42 lid 4 and the Vpb-2023 page) against € 128.000.000 (FISIN 2023 EIA sentence, while its MIA sentence already says € 136.000.000). Settled to € 136.000.000.
- Starters willekeurige afschrijving cap: baseline end of the 28% band against the top of the KIA table. Settled to the top of the table on the URWA art. 8 wording "maximum bedrag waarover kleinschaligheidsinvesteringsaftrek kan worden verkregen" (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0012035/2025-01-01/0) read with the Belastingdienst KIA window. This rests on reading a cross-reference, no primary page prints the cap as a number.
- Bodemwaarde 2021 and 2022: inferred in the research, now read directly in https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2021-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01 (50% rule for own use still present).
- 2027 EIA: not settled, see 2027 open points.

### Costs and the car

- Kilometre allowance 2026, € 0,23 against € 0,25. Settled: the statute prints € 0,23 (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01, art. 3.15 lid 6 and 3.17 lid 1 b). The Beleidsbesluit fiscale maatregelen naar aanleiding van de energieschok of 17 May 2026, nr. 2026-8260, Stcrt. 2026, 18302, approves € 0,25 "structureel en met terugwerkende kracht tot en met 1 januari 2026", and the decree lapses on 1 January 2027 (https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2026-18302.html). The Belastingdienst applies € 0,25 for 2026 (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/inkomstenbelasting/veranderingen-inkomstenbelasting-2026/zakelijk-gebruik-privevervoermiddel-2026). Use € 0,25 for 2026. The research word "structureel" and the baseline "for 2026 only" are both partly right: the approval is meant to be structural but its own legal life ends on 1 January 2027, and from 2027 € 0,25 depends on Belastingplan 2027.
- Youngtimer age 2027, 25 against 17 years. Settled by layer, not by value: 25 years is the enacted 2027 text (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01), 17 years in 2027 and 20 years from 2028 is the Belastingplan 2027 proposal with transitional rules in art. XLVII (https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-37022-2.html). The bill is not adopted on 2026-10-11 (https://www.tweedekamer.nl/kamerstukken/wetsvoorstellen/detail?cfg=wetsvoorsteldetails&qry=wetsvoorstel%3A37022). The research claim that the CPB note describes "a further gradual raise to 25 years" is not supported. The bill moves away from 25.
- Zero-emission 2020, "8% up to € 45.000" against "14% off capped € 6.300". Same regime: the 2020 statute prints 14% with a maximum of € 6300 (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01). Only waterstof was uncapped in 2020.
- Old-car minimum, 35% against 25%. Both apply to different cars: 35% of WEV for old cars (art. 3.20 lid 1 b), at least 25% instead of 22% for a car first admitted by 31 December 2016 (art. 10a.4) (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01).
- Solar-cell exemption from the zero-emission cap. The 2026 Belastingdienst page says at least 1 kilowattpiek. The statute says 1 kilowattpiek only in the 2021 to 2023 texts and from the 2024 text requires wattpiek divided by wattuur per km of at least 7 (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01). The statute decides.
- Youngtimer transition 2026, wording. Rijksoverheid says cars turning 16 in 2026 stay youngtimer until 1 January 2027. The legal basis is the Besluit van 12 februari 2026, Stcrt. 2026, 6596, an optional approval for a car that passed 15 during 2025 and was available on or before 31 December 2025 (https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2026-6596.html).
- Werkruimte owner route. The Belastingdienst page offers deduction for a non-zelfstandige workspace when the home is owned and in the ondernemingsvermogen. From 2025 art. 3.16 lid 14 bars costs that lid 1 would bar for a private home, the MvT names the huurderslasten (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01 and https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36602-3.html).
- STAP-budget amount. "At most € 1.000 per year" in research against "ten hoogste 100% ... tot een maximum van € 1.000 inclusief btw" per scholing in art. 7 (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0045419/2023-01-01). STAP start date settled as 1 January 2022 (https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2021-349.html).
- Studiekosten prestatiebeurs case in 2026. FISIN 2026 limits it to an extended diplomatermijn of at most 15 years, study years 2010/2011 to 2014/2015 (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/aftrek_studiekosten).
- Per-year status of telefoon, laptop, werkruimte and boetes, which research left as unknown. The statute text of art. 3.14 and 3.16 was read in every version 2020 to 2027. The only change is art. 3.16 lid 14 from 2025 (https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 to https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01).

### Box 3 savings and investments

- 2022 rechtsherstel percentages: the law table of Stb. 2022, 533 prints bank 0,01% and debts 2,46%, art. 4 orders both replaced after 2022. Operative values bank 0,00%, other 5,53%, debts 2,28%. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/met-welke-percentages-is-het-fictief-rendement-berekend https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2022-533.html
- 2023 schuldendrempel: FISIN 2023 says € 3.200 / € 6.400, the 2023 statute, the drempel page and the 2023 calculation page say € 3.400 / € 6.800. Settled at € 3.400 / € 6.800. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2023-01-01
- Start year of the tegenbewijsregeling: statutory effect from 1 January 2023 (arts. I and III), 1 January 2017 inside the Wet rechtsherstel (art. IV), the law itself in force 19 July 2025, the no-letter form route for 2021 to 2024, and reporting in the aangifte from 2025. "From 2024" is wrong. https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2025-195.html https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/box-3/content/zonder-brief-formulier-opgaaf-werkelijk-rendement-invullen
- Wet rechtsherstel entry date: statute art. 7 says the day after the issue date of 27 December 2022, so 28 December 2022, with retroactive effect to 1 January 2017. The Rijksoverheid timeline's "1 januari 2023" is not the legal date. https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2022-533.html
- Green facilities 2027: research and Rijksoverheid say full lapse on 1 January 2027. Statute: € 200 / € 400 and 0,1% in 2027, repeal from 1 January 2028. https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2025-444.html
- 2027 other-assets forfait: not an indexed base value but replaced by regulation under art. 10.6ter lid 3. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01
- Hoge Raad 25 June 2026: the secondary-only finding is now confirmed by the Rijksoverheid timeline for non-objectors. https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/themas/werk/inkomstenbelasting/box-3/tijdlijn-rechtsherstel-box-3
- 2026 heffingsvrij vermogen and forfait: € 59.357 and 6% against the dropped proposal of € 51.396 and 7,78%. The 2026 statute prints € 59.357 and 6%. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01
- 2025 box 3 rate: the research had no value. 36% per the 2025 statute. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01

### Box 2 and the substantial interest

- Loss-conversion korting 2024 to 2026, 24,5% against 26,9%: settled at 24,5%. Art. 4.53 lid 2 in the 2024, 2025, 2026 and 2027 texts refers to "het in de eerste regel van de vierde kolom van de tabel in artikel 2.12 opgenomen percentage", which is 24,5%. The live Belastingdienst page, FISIN 2024 and the FISIN 2025 example agree. The 26,9% in FISIN 2025 (rule text) and FISIN 2026 is a stale handbook figure. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2024/aanmerkelijk_belang
- Placement of the excessief-lenen fiction, art. 4.12a against art. 4.13 lid 1 sub f: settled at art. 4.13 lid 1 sub f with art. 4.14a to 4.14c. Art. 4.12a carries the inherited-belang facility (reguliere voordelen within 24 months after death). https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2022-531.html
- PEB start date, 1 April 2017 against 1 July 2017: settled. The measures apply from 1 April 2017 (consolidated BWBR0039380 "Geldend van 01-04-2017", Centraal Aanspreekpunt Pensioenen), the coulance period ran to 30 June 2017 and lapsed on 1 July 2017, which is the date the Belastingdienst page uses for the end of accrual. The published Stb. 2017, 115 Artikel XIX prints 1 January 2017 under the Wet raadgevend referendum. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0039380/2017-04-01 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2017-18118.html https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2017-115.html
- Art. 4.6 "unchanged 2020 to 2027" against the year texts: settled. Sub e from the 2022 text, sub f from 2025 (retroactive). https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2025-445.html
- "No box 2 change proposed for 2027" against the 29 September 2026 cabinet letter and the Overige fiscale maatregelen 2027 bill: settled. Box 2 changes are proposed (29,2% second bracket, excessief-lenen phase-down, two technical measures), none adopted on 2026-10-11. https://open.overheid.nl/documenten/5d1a02b8-ff11-4b87-b6a4-bb0f9828eca0/file https://www.eerstekamer.nl/behandeling/20260915/memorie_van_toelichting_56/document3/f=/vn15lhsbrhzz.pdf
- Belastingplan 2023 against Belastingplan 2024 as the source of the 2024 brackets: both are right for different parts. Belastingplan 2023 introduced the second bracket, Belastingplan 2024 (amendement Van der Lee c.s.) set its rate at 33%. https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2023/12/20/belangrijkste-belastingwijzigingen-per-1-januari-2024 https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36602-3.html

### The BV and vennootschapsbelasting

- Large class turnover bound: KVK prints more than € 51 mln, Stb. 2024, 52 art. 3 and Boek 2 BW art. 397 (2025-01-01 and 2026-07-01) print € 50 miljoen. Settled at € 50 miljoen. https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2024-52.html and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0003045/2025-01-01
- In-force date of the btw-eenheid beleidsbesluit: reported 1 July 2025 by a search summary. The besluit itself states "Dit besluit treedt in werking met ingang van 1 juli 2025". https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2024-38545.html
- Werkgeversheffing Zvw 2020 to 2023 (research gap): 6,70%, 7,00%, 6,75%, 6,68% from the FISIN pages of each year. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2020/inkomensafhankelijke_bijdrage_zorgverzekeringswet and the 2021 to 2023 equivalents
- Dividend stripping article: the rule is art. 4 lid 7 Wet DB 1965, not art. 4 lid 3, and it can be read in the dated texts. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002515/2020-01-01
- Statutory basis of the ten-year term for onroerende zaken: art. 34a Wet OB 1968 (nine years following the year of first use), and for OSS art. 28rl and 28sj (ten years). https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002629/2026-01-01
- 2026 gebruikelijk loon: the dated 2026-01-01 Wet LB text prints € 58.000, matching the Belastingdienst page. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0002471/2026-01-01
- Box 2 2027 conflict (conflict index rows for deepseek-9 i3): the future text keeps € 68.843, the bill multiplies by 1,01248 rather than suspending indexation. The exact 2027 amount stays open, see 2027 open points. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 and https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-37022-2.html
- Baseline row on the 2023 willekeurige afschrijving (UNVERIFIED, not reopened): confirmed on the Belastingdienst Vpb 2023 change page. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/winst/vennootschapsbelasting/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-vorige-jaren/veranderingen-vennootschapsbelasting-2023
- Besluit geruisloze omzetting citation: Stcrt. 2025, 37092 (published 4 November 2025), not Stcrt. 2025, 3709. https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stcrt-2025-37092.html
- Zvw route for an uninsured DGA: the BV withholds the bijdrage on net wage (Handboek Loonheffingen 2025 paragraph 8.2.2), not an aanslag. https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek-loonheffingen-lh0221t51d.pdf

### Pension, lijfrente and AOV

- 2022 look-back (research i1 seven years against i2 ten years): seven years, law text art. 3.127 lid 2 of 2022-01-01 reads "periode van zeven jaar". https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01/0/Hoofdstuk3/Afdeling3.7/Artikel3.127
- BAZ verslag date (27 May against 29 May 2026): both right. The committee inbreng took place on 27 May 2026 ("Inbreng geleverd"), the Verslag document 36 912 nr. 5 is dated 29 May 2026 in the docket document list. https://www.tweedekamer.nl/kamerstukken/wetsvoorstellen/detail?cfg=wetsvoorsteldetails&qry=wetsvoorstel%3A36912
- Wording of the 2026 maximum jaarruimte: the base is min(income, € 137.800) minus € 19.172 = € 118.628, and 30% gives € 35.589. The baseline phrase "30% of € 137.800 minus € 19.172" is loose wording, the euro outcome is right. https://open.overheid.nl/documenten/c3d48c04-1009-4ffe-a54b-cebf13427ab5/file and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01/0/Hoofdstuk3/Afdeling3.7/Artikel3.127
- Euro maxima of the jaarruimte 2020 to 2025 (derived and UNVERIFIED in the research and baseline): € 12.986, € 13.236, € 13.570, € 34.550, € 36.077, € 35.798 from the Ministry of Finance tables. Only 2023 differs from the derived figure (research € 34.549). URLs per year above.
- FOR maximum 2020 (law € 9.218 against FISIN 2020 € 9.214): € 9.218, law art. 3.68 and Ministry 2020 table. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2020-01-01 and https://open.overheid.nl/documenten/ronl-423637a0-c7a6-4123-a683-148a64e343fa/pdf
- Tijdelijke oudedagslijfrente 2022 (FISIN verzekering row € 22.443 against rekening row € 22.735) and 2026 (€ 27.192 against € 26.781): € 22.735 and € 27.192 by art. 3.125 lid 1 c and the Ministry tables. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2026-01-01
- BAZ wachttijd (docket summary "ongeveer één jaar" against 104 weeks): 104 weken, bill art. 16 lid 1 and MvT 5.3.1. https://www.tweedekamer.nl/downloads/document?id=2026D13313
- Location of the FOR transition and of the FOR conversion deduction: art. 10a.29 lid 10, 11 and 12 (from 2023) and art. 3.128 (2020 to 2022), not art. 3.127. https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2024-01-01 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2022-01-01
- 2027 law text "not found": it exists at https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2027-01-01 with 2026 amounts still in place.

### Work relationship and procedure

- VBAR hourly amount € 36 against € 38: both are right. € 36 is the statutory text, based on € 35,43 unrounded at peildatum 1 January 2025 (120% WML of € 14,06 plus opslagen times 1,5). € 38 is the same amount indexed to the WML of 1 January 2026 (€ 14,71), the peildatum the Eerste Kamer, rijksoverheid.nl and Ondernemersplein use. Deciding sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/kst-36783-3.html https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/03/06/kabinet-kiest-voor-meer-rust-en-duidelijkheid-voor-zzpers-en-opdrachtgevers https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/themas/werk/minimumloon/bedragen-minimumloon/bedragen-minimumloon-2026
- BAZ verslag date 27 May against 29 May 2026: both true. Inbreng delivered 27 May, verslag (36 912, nr. 5) published 29 May. Deciding source: https://www.tweedekamer.nl/kamerstukken/wetsvoorstellen/detail?cfg=wetsvoorsteldetails&qry=wetsvoorstel%3A36912
- Uitstel channel online against paper: online in Mijn Belastingdienst until 1 May for those with an aangiftebrief, paper form for other filing dates and after 1 May. Deciding sources: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/themaoverstijgend/programmas_en_formulieren/aanvraag-uitstel-aangifte-inkomstenbelasting https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/belastingaangifte/content/kan-ik-nog-uitstel-aanvragen-belastingaangifte
- KVK btw-number term two weeks against ten werkdagen: both wordings remain on the KVK page, ten werkdagen equals two calendar weeks, and the Belastingdienst states registration takes at most 10 werkdagen. Deciding source: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/ondernemen/onderneming_starten/schrijf-uw-onderneming-in/
- 2027 DBA enforcement against the baseline (nothing said about 2027): the handhaving page is silent, but the Handhavingsplan 2026 states that the remaining zachte landing lapses on 1 January 2027 and that regular integration is the ambition from 2027. Deciding source: https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z62fd.pdf
- Per-year IB filing deadlines 2020 to 2024 (research: not found): 2020 return before 8 May 2021, 2021 before 1 May 2022, 2022 before 1 May 2023, 2023 before 1 May 2024, 2024 by 1 May 2025, each with uitstel to 1 September of the same year. Deciding sources: archived Belastingdienst pages and Stcrt. 2021, 24144, listed per year above.
- Aanslaggrens 2020 (research: not found): € 47 and € 15 in the current higher-than wording, € 48 and € 16 in the wording used until 2023. Deciding source: https://web.archive.org/web/20210413/https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/hoe_werkt_inkomstenbelasting/aanslag/definitieve_aanslag
- Staatsblad 2016, 165 as the Wet DBA (research): it is the Besluit van 8 april 2016 on the opt-out for some fictieve dienstbetrekkingen. The Wet DBA is the Wet van 3 februari 2016, in force 1 May 2016. Deciding sources: https://zoek.officielebekendmakingen.nl/stb-2016-165.html https://open.overheid.nl/documenten/d2c6b4e8-537c-48a8-9e17-85f1acf4825d/file
- Regular DBA handhaving start date: 1 January 2026 in the tranche 2025 plan, moved to an ambition for 1 January 2027 in the 2026 plan. Deciding source: https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handhavingsplan-arbeidsrelaties-lh0021z62fd.pdf

---

## 5. OPEN POINTS AND GAPS

These points were open on 2026-10-11. Each year file repeats its own open points in its section 7.

### BTW (VAT)

- 2020: Per-state distance-sales thresholds before 1 July 2021 were not read on a listed primary source.
- 2020: The exact 2020 date table and the 2020 ICP deadline were not found.

A Belastingdienst ruling or a corrected page would settle it. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/btw_aftrekken/btw_en_de_auto/privegebruik_auto_van_de_zaak/privegebruik_auto_van_de_zaak and https://download.belastingdienst.nl/belastingdienst/docs/handboek_ondernemen_2026_aa9701z62fd.pdf

- 2027: The precise wording of the 2027 art. 6k change was taken from a summary of the memorie van toelichting. The adopted text (kst-36920 gewijzigd voorstel) would settle its exact scope.
- 2027: The rest of the 2027 deadline table and any 2027 handbook values are not yet published.

- 2022: Heffingskortingen in the AOW transition year 2022: no rule found.
- 2023: Heffingskortingen in the AOW transition year 2023: no rule found.
- 2024: AHK whole-year AOW phase-out rate: 3,421% (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/algemene_heffingskorting/tabel-algemene-heffingskorting-2024) against 3,420% (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2024/heffingskortingen). Arithmetic € 1.735 / (€ 75.518 minus € 24.812) is about 3,4217%. Wet IB art. 8.10 was not reachable in the fetched statute text. The baseline follows 3,421%.
- 2024: IACK maximum point: € 31.838 to € 31.839 (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting-2024) against € 31.837 (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2024/heffingskortingen). Both describe the same cap.
- 2025: The fetch of https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2025/belastingberekening returned 36,97% for schijf 2 in the AOW transition-year table. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/boxen_en_tarieven/box_1/box_1 and https://wetten.overheid.nl/BWBR0011353/2025-01-01 give 37,48%, which governs.
- 2025: IACK co-ouder wording: 78 days per 6 months (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2025/heffingskortingen) against 156 days per calendar year prorated (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting). The two tests come close but differ in how short periods are handled.
- 2026: Monthly AOW transition rates: https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/boxen_en_tarieven/box_1/box_1 against https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/belastingberekening differ by 0,01 point in six months. https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/boxen_en_tarieven/box_1/box_1 is the dedicated current page and the baseline uses it. No statute text holds the monthly rates.
- 2026: IACK maximum point: € 32.711 (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/prive/inkomstenbelasting/heffingskortingen_boxen_tarieven/heffingskortingen/inkomensafhankelijke_combikorting/inkomensafhankelijke-combinatiekorting-2026), € 32.719 (https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/fisin/fisin2026/heffingskortingen), € 32.720 (https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/vraag-en-antwoord/inkomstenbelasting/wat-is-een-heffingskorting-en-welke-heffingskortingen-zijn-er). ## 6. RECORD AND INVOICE DUTIES

These duties hold in every year.

Core business records are generally retained for at least seven years. Immovable-property records generally have a ten-year retention period. Keep sales and purchase invoices, bank and cash records, contracts, stock records where relevant, VAT records, and data supporting income, deductions, assets, and declarations. Digital source invoices should remain in their received digital form. Digital records must remain accessible, readable, complete, and authentic. See the [administration duty](https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/nl/administratie-bijhouden/administratie-bijhouden) and [invoice retention rules](https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/administratie_bijhouden/facturen_maken/uw_facturen_bewaren).

An invoice identifies the supplier and customer by legal name and address, includes the supplier VAT ID and applicable Chamber of Commerce number, shows a date and unique consecutive invoice number, describes the supply and quantity or extent, gives a different supply or advance-payment date when applicable, lists the unit price and net amount by VAT rate, and states the VAT rate and amount. See [invoice requirements](https://www.belastingdienst.nl/wps/wcm/connect/bldcontentnl/belastingdienst/zakelijk/btw/administratie_bijhouden/facturen_maken/factuureisen/).
